*I had very little stamina to begin with, and now fatigue was building up inside me. Deciding to stay standing up was good, as these heavy legs of mine were proof of.*
*As for things with Himeno, it would be best to talk them through later. If we talked them over in this tense state, we would probably just get into another argument. Hiyoshi-san might even serve as our mediator.*
*As expected in this gloomy building, he didn't have to walk far before he disappeared. The sound of his gradually fading footsteps had also disappeared.*
*The only thing I could hear nearby was my own breathing.*
*That's why that high, roaring scream pierced right through my eardrums.*
Hiyoshi: "Gyaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa!!"
*It was a scream that seemed to have echoed through every corridor. No... in and out of the whole building. It wasn't just someone shouting, but someone crying out as if splitting apart their throat.*
*At the sense of impending danger that voice bore, my own throat began to tighten.*
Mayu: "That scream......"
*That voice wasn't merely familiar. It was the voice of Hiyoshi-san, who had been right in front of me just one minute earlier.*
*After that single, loud scream, I could no longer hear his voice.*
Mayu: "H-Hiyoshi-san?!"
*I quickly stand up, running to the source of the scream.*
*This place connects the tube-tunnel booth and the souvenir shop. That's where I heard the voice coming from. I run toward the tunnel tank.*
Hiyoshi: "Huh, Mayu-chan? I told you to wait there for me."
Mayu: "More importantly, that scream a minute ago... did something happen?"
*Hiyoshi-san is sitting at the entrance of the tunnel tank, not far from the aquarium entrance.*
*At my question, he gives the wishy-washy reply of, "You were worried about me, I'm so happy."*
Hiyoshi: "Nah, it's a somewhat embarrassing story... but it looked like there was a person floating inside that tank. Under the circumstances, it just surprised me."
Mayu: "A person in the tank... speaking of which, Himeno mentioned something like that. But I'm glad... I thought you'd gotten caught up in some terrible situation like I had earlier......"
*I was relieved from the bottom of my heart. Following that relief, I became aware of my own fear of being isolated again.*
*Chuckling, Hiyoshi-san scratches his head.*
Hiyoshi: "Say, Mayu-cha--"
*I never got to hear the rest of what he was going to say.*
Mayu: "Huh...?"
*It all took place in a matter of seconds, leaving me unable to make sense of what had happened. However, I gradually realized what was unfolding before my eyes.*
*From Hiyoshi-san's neck up, I could see through to the tank.*
*I didn't understand what I was seeing. In other words-- his head was no longer where it should have been.*
*Having lost a piece of himself so valuable to the human body, he collapsed slowly on the spot. From there, he never moved again.*
*Soaking up the blue lighting, his reddish-black blood spread across the floor.*
Mayu: "Nooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo!"
*This time, it was my turn to split my vocal cords.*
*Why... how had he gotten like this? What could have happened to have left him in this state?*
*Hiyoshi-san's head tumbles along the ground and catches my eye. His eyes are wide and his pupils dilated, the grin I had seen hours earlier still spread across his face. There are marks at the nape of his neck as if his head has been bitten clean off.*
Mayu: "Hiyo... Hiyoshi... san......"
*I intended to run. I intended to run and leave the dead Hiyoshi-san. Whatever left those bite marks was still nearby. But I couldn't do it.*
Mayu: "It... hurts......"
*The area below my knees had become pitch black.*
Mayu: "My... boots... Mom......"
*There is no pain. Only the visualization of pain running through my mind. In spite of losing my legs, I can't actually feel the pain.*
*Of course not. That body of mine has lost its legs. I'm watching it from a separate place. This subjective point of view would have been impossible while I was alive.*
*My head was already just like Hiyoshi-san's, just rolling around on the floor.*
*The cold temperature of my blood. That was the last thing I felt.*
*Right before I lost consciousness, I saw its unbelievable true form through the wavy mist of my fading vision.*
*With many fangs and a giant body, it was a fish.*
Mayu: "Please wait, I'll go."
*Somehow moving my heavy legs, I follow behind Hiyoshi-san.*
*It would have been easier to leave it to Hiyoshi-san. However, Himeno is my best friend. Though I can't believe we had fought, that reality can't be changed, so I want to go get her.*
*Since the souvenir shop is near the entrance, Hiyoshi-san heads toward the closest tunnel tank. If Himeno is still in the building, then we need to look everywhere for her.*
*I'm able to catch up with Hiyoshi-san before he enters the tunnel.*
Mayu: "I'm going too."
Hiyoshi: "Huh, Mayu-chan? I told you to wait there for me..."
Mayu: "I can't do that. Because Himeno is... my friend."
*On hearing what I said, Hiyoshi-san gives me a patronizing look while grinning at me.*
*Looking into the tunnel from the outside, I realize it hasn't changed from the Manten Aquarium I had seen earlier that day. Large fish like manta rays and sharks swim about.*
Mayu: "This is..."
Hiyoshi: "The tunnel tank. If you two went in order earlier, then shouldn't we start with the first exhibit?"
*I nod, as what Hiyoshi-san said is right. He adds, "Right," and walks on.*
*I notice something there and pull him back.*
Mayu: "Look, over there."
*I say as I point to an area nearby that's visible from the entrance. As the silhouette becomes clearer on close observation, I become frightened.*
*Something hops freely from side to side. It bounces about the tunnel numerous times, then stops itself. After bouncing up and down on the floor several times, it ceases to move... completely exhausted.*
*Straining my eyes from far away, I can see that it is a fish. From what I can see from ten meters away, it has sharp fangs and a body as thin as a rail.*
Hiyoshi: "That... might be a cutlassfish."
Mayu: "At any rate, let's head inside."
*I suggest to him.*
Hiyoshi: "Guess we should, it would be good to get a look at something li--"
*I never got to hear the rest of what he was going to say.*
Mayu: "Huh...?"
*It all took place in a matter of seconds, leaving me unable to make sense of what had happened. However, I gradually realized what was unfolding before my eyes.*
*From Hiyoshi-san's neck up, I could see through to the tank.*
*I didn't understand what I was seeing. In other words-- his head was no longer where it should have been.*
*Having lost a piece of himself so valuable to the human body, he collapsed slowly on the spot. From there, he never moved again.*
*Soaking up the blue lighting, his reddish-black blood spread across the floor.*
Mayu: "Nooooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo!"
*My vocal cords were splitting.*
*Why... how had he gotten like this? What could have happened to have left him in this state?*
*Hiyoshi-san's head tumbles along the ground and catches my eye. His eyes are wide and his pupils dilated, the grin I had seen hours earlier still spread across his face. There are marks at the nape of his neck as if his head has been bitten clean off.*
Mayu: "Hiyo... Hiyoshi... san......"
*I intended to run. I intended to run and leave the dead Hiyoshi-san. Whatever left those bite marks was still nearby. But I couldn't do it.*
Mayu: "It... hurts......"
*The area below my knees had become pitch black.*
Mayu: "My... boots... Mom......"
*There was no pain. Only the visualization of pain running through my mind. In spite of losing my legs, I can't actually feel the pain.*
*Of course not. That body of mine has lost its legs. I'm watching it from a separate place. This subjective point of view would have been impossible while I was alive.*
*My head was already just like Hiyoshi-san's, just rolling around on the floor.*
*The cold temperature of my blood. That was the last thing I felt.*
*Right before I lose consciousness, I see its unbelievable true form through the wavy mist of my fading vision.*
*With many fangs and a giant body, it was a fish.*
Mayu: "We should get a better look."
*After waiting near the entrance for several seconds, a fish resembling a cutlassfish bounces around the tunnel, then flops down on the floor, causing the amount of fish corpses on the floor to increase.*
*The gathering of numerous fish scales reflects the flourescent light back like a kaleidoscope. Though it is the same as the flourescent lighting, the light reflected back is vivid, putting the sensation of the scales' slipperiness in my mind.*
Hiyoshi: "Ugh, that's pretty creepy..."
*Hiyoshi-san's lips curl up in disgust as he grumbles. I also thought it was creepy, but compared to the rotten corpse I had seen earlier-- That was as far back as I allowed my memory to go.*
Mayu: "Could... that be..."
*In contrast to Hiyoshi-san, who had averted his eyes, I just continued to stare and was able to pick up on something.*
Mayu: "Those fish hopping around... they're coming from below. From that place that looks like a vent."
*From the beginning of the tunnel, the fish hop up intermittently. It doesn't appear as though they are going to stop, with a new fish popping up after a fixed interval of time passes.*
*The fish mechanically shoot out of the vent, ricochet about the tunnel at high speed, then finally, their vigor exhausted, die as they land in a pile on the floor.*
*At the moment they fall, the fishes' long, narrow bodies wriggle like snakes. They collide with the other fish as they cease moving, the next fish hopping to its death in the same manner.*

Mayu: "So many strange things have happened that I'm probably becoming used to them."

*The fish pierces the handkerchief once as it dances in the air, then the fish passes through it a second, and then a third time as it bounces repeatedly.*
*In an instant, the fabric he tossed has become dust.*
*On placing myself in the handkerchief's position, I subconsciously cover my neck.*

*A form -- a dead body. I didn't want those words to pass my lips, but it was definitely as Hiyoshi-san had said.*

*I look down and tilt my head to the side.*
*I'm worried about Himeno.*
*I can no longer make baseless statements like, "Himeno is fine because she is Himeno."*
*The reality is that if Hiyoshi-san hadn't rescued me, I'd... no longer be in this world.*
*There is no way I can let that happen to Himeno. I have to hurry and meet up with her, then find a safe place.*

Mayu: "Could the fact that the lights are on mean that someone just came through here?"
Hiyoshi: "Even if they had, that person should be aware of all the unusual phenomena here. Anyway, if we go through this passage, we can circumvent the tunnel and look for Himeno-chan."
Mayu: "All right......"
*Hiyoshi-san walked ahead, taking several steps before I stepped in. I was hesitant about walking through the staff passageway without permission, but it was certainly better than going through the tunnel tank.*
*Moreover, if we are able to come across a staff member, we might be able to gain some information about this Manten Aquarium.*
Mari: "-- Big sister."
*The staff passageway is approximately ten meters, just wide enough that people can barely pass each other. Walking up ahead of me, Hiyoshi-san's shoulder width is so great that I wouldn't be able to pass him unless he turns sideways.*
*Even more than before, Hiyoshi-san seems stronger and more reliable than ever.*
Mari: "-- Big sister."
Mayu: "Huh...?"

*Hiyoshi-san asks me without turning around. I squeak out that I'm fine and turn my attention to his back.*

*However, that was my confirmation. It was neither a visual nor auditory hallucination.*

*His expression as he calls for me to calm down is frantic. A crease appears in his forehead as he strains his voice. I steady my breathing as I stand in fear of his threatening attitude.*
*Breathing deeply, I keep my back against the wall of the corridor. As my determination rises, so does my body temperature.*

Hiyoshi: "Owowowowowowowowowowowowowown--"
*My words don't seem to be reaching Hiyoshi-san at all, as his eyes open wide and his voice rings out.*
*Like a broken DVD player, he cannot move past that one syllable.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi... san..."
*I back up instinctively.*
*However, as if he's completely unaware, Hiyoshi-san just keeps yelling in a strange voice.*
Mayu: "Um... Hiyoshi-san... Please tell me you're okay... aren't you?"
*Three meters away from Hiyoshi-san, I peer at him.*
*He sways, his legs unstable, but he's definitely moving towards me.*
Hiyoshi: "Wn........."
Mayu: "Kyah!"
*Almost at the same time the strange voice stops, he falls over on the spot. As if his power supply had been cut off, he suddenly tumbles to the ground.*
*The sound of him flopping to the ground sends a chill down my spine.*
*I'm not familiar with what you would call ukemi, but I know the way he fell is dangerous.*
Mayu: "Um... are you injured?"
*Sitting down, I extend a quivering hand. It's probably a bit rude to the person who saved me, but he would understand.*
*For at this moment, the image of his strange behavior is seared into my mind.*
Mayu: "......"
*Even when I call out to him, there is no response.*
*But if I strain my ears, I can hear shallow breathing.*
Mayu: "Thank goodness, he's alive..."
*That alone is enough to satisfy me. There's something I can rely on within this mysterious aquarium.*
*What in the world was all that...? Still having my doubts, I am, for the moment, relieved.*
Mayu: "What should I do?"
*I can't just leave him unconscious and defenseless while I proceed further into the staff passageway.*
*On the other hand, carrying someone an entire head taller than I am is impossible.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san, please wake up..."
*I call his name over and over, but he still doesn't respond.*
Hiyoshi: "... Nm..."
*I hesitate a bit, and then I touch his back.*
Hiyoshi: "Ughhh..."
Mayu: "........."

*His body is now facing up with his eyes closed... his chest goes up, then down to form a breath.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san!"
*I can't make the distinction between whether he is just asleep or has passed out. I have never seen someone who has lost consciousness.*
*I take deep breaths to keep myself from giving way to panic.*
*Other than the sound of Hiyoshi-san's breathing, the sounds of machines running echo. Sometimes, I can hear the sound of my own breathing.*

*My awareness of the situation quickly turns to anxiety. I am all alone inside this incomprehensible Manten Aquarium.*
*The only other existence I can rely on, Hiyoshi-san, currently has his eyes closed.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san, please wake up!"
*Growing impatient, I shake his shoulders. He doesn't even moan, and his lack of a reaction only increases my anxiety, so I call his name in a louder voice.*
*Whether or not my voice reaches him, his eyes flutter open. On closer inspection, he has distinct double eyelids.*
Mayu: "Thank goodness, you're awake... Huh?"
Hiyoshi: "Are you... looking down on me too...?"
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san, what...? I-I'm Nakanobe Mayumi. I'm not looking down on you or anything like that!"
Hiyoshi: "You tooooooooooo!"
*Screaming, he grabs my left ankle as I squat next to him. His strength is unthinkable compared to his gentle demeanor from only moments ago.*
*His rough hands dig into my soft skin. Terror rages within my heart at this first brush with a man's real power.*
Mayu: "Please... st... op......"
*Whenever he seems to be losing his grip on my leg, he tightens it more. Every time I verbalize my pain, his grip intensifies a little further.*
*My concern for him is gone; only the feeling of his rejection remains.*
Hiyoshi: "You littleeeeeeeeeeeee!"
*I realize that, little by little, he's pulling me closer to him. He really is that strong.*
Mayu: "I... haven't done anything. Ow...... Hiyoshi-san..."
*Gripping my ankle tightly, Hiyoshi-san pulls himself up little by little. His chest is huge, and the muscles in his arms bulge and form a mountain-shape.*
*His face lacks the appearance of sanity, and his eyes are looking in different directions. They roll without end, seeming as if they will go completely white.*
*Though I intended to put distance between us, I'm unable to do so. As if coaxed, I'm pulled closer to him.*
Mayu: "No..."
Hiyoshi: "Rejected, I'm being rejected...... The ones doingggggggg it, are yoooou all...!"
Mayu: "No!"
*While standing himself up, he sluggishly lifts his left hand.*
*His fingers point in my direction. There is no mistaking his intent to grab me with both hands. I'm filled with the desire to be free from him.*
Mayu: "No... no!"
*In time with my words of rejection, both of my legs jerk frantically.*
*At any rate, I don't think I can take it if he grabs my right leg, so I struggle with all my might, not even worrying about whether my skirt flips up.*
*He is as strong as before, but his movements are slow, somehow allowing me to continue my resistance.*
Mayu: "Huff, huff......"
*Nevertheless, between the pain and my resistance, my stamina is fading rapidly. Before I realize it, my haggard breath is louder than a machine's motor.*
Hiyoshi: "Don't rejjjjject me. You shhhhhhhhhhould be more accccccccepting."
*Hiyoshi's words give an unchanging, disordered image.*
*Whether he's given up on grabbing my other leg or not, his body leans toward me. The upper half of my body falls back, but he quickly yanks my leg and throws off my balance."
Mayu: "Kyah!"
*Upon hearing me let out a scream, the corners of his mouth twitch.*
*... He is enjoying this.*
*He is enjoying watching me lose the ability to resist. His expression makes this clear to me.*
*One more thing has become clear to me. That is, that I can no longer see him as the Hiyoshi-san I once knew.*
Mayu: "I'm... sorry!"
*Even so, my conscience is tugging at me. My words of apology are proof of that.*
*I use the heel of my boot to kick Hiyoshi-san, who'd pulled himself up, in the nose. My skirt flutters up and I reflexively hold it down.*
Hiyoshi: "Gah..."
*Seizing the opportunity, Hiyoshi-san yanks my left leg hard towards himself. Whether it is because of his hesitation or not, my other leg slides free of his right hand.*
*A single line of blood trickles from his nose. The heels must be harder than I thought.*
Mayu: "Ah!"
*Upon seeing the blood, I shut my eyes immediately. However, I realize I no longer have the luxury of instinctually worrying about him.*
*Pulling myself upright, I break into a run in order to escape him.*
*In no time, he has moved in front of me.*
*I want to get away from Hiyoshi-san -- with that one thought, I decide to go in a direction unknown to me.*
Hiyoshi: "Kickkkkkkkkkking me, isn't that impudent...? Could the stench on the sole of that shoe be Ruka? Or is it Mikiiiiiiiiii? Haha... hahahahahahahaha!!!!"

Hiyoshi: "Feels good, does it? Haha... that's fine, that's just fiiiiinnnnnnnne!!!!"
*Hiyoshi-san yells, writhing on the floor.*
*The transformation of the one who had rescued me is too devastating.*
*Even so, there is no changing the fact that he tried to hurt me.*
*I understand somewhere in my heart that, through no fault of his own, he is no longer sane, and the desire to protect my own body is winning inside of me.*
Mayu: "I'm sorry, but--"
*As I shut my eyes, I turn away from Hiyoshi-san.*
*I'm sure he isn't looking at me anymore. In other words, he is no longer conscious of anything.*
*His voice attempts to stop my fleeing legs many times, but no matter how fast he is, I've already decided to head for the exit of the staff passageway.*
Mayu: "I have to... get his hand loose..."
*As I try to loosen the hand clutching my leg, he frantically pushes me off.*
*As if pushing against a rock, his hand remains unperturbed.*
Mayu: "No way..."
*Even giving those words form can't change reality.*
Mayu: "No good."
Hiyoshi: "Gaaaaaaaah."
*As he lets out a groan, Hiyoshi-san's left hand reaches for me.*
*His movements are slow, but his fingers are moving in my direction.*
Mayu: "Hurry... I have to hurry and get him off of me!"
*As before, Hiyoshi-san's hand won't move.*
*It has an uncanny strength.*
*Even trying to loosen his fingers one by one, my strength is no match for his.*
Mayu: "What should I do?"
*Then I realize I'm concentrating too hard on my left leg. He has latched onto my right leg with that same strength.*
Mayu: "Wha...?"
*Having grabbed on to both of my legs, I have no room to put up a fight and am pulled in closer to him.*
*His bloodshot face appears right before my eyes. His pupils are split in opposite directions, much like a fish's eyes.*
Hiyoshi: "Ssssssssso... very well. You allllllll... will..."
*What comes from Hiyoshi-san's mouth are not words. Without realizing it, I've stopped listening to him.*
Mayu: "Ow..."
*He is definitely splitting apart my legs with all his strength.*
*An intense pain shoots through me as if my leg is being torn off at the hip. Even as I try to escape, my legs will not move at all.*
*On account of him lifting me by my legs, my torso goes limp as if hanging in midair.*
Mayu: "No... Hiyoshi-san..."
Hiyoshi: "This stench is... Ruka? Miki? Either one is fiiiiiiiiiiiiiiiiiine...... Oooooooooh."
*My words suddenly no longer reach him.*
*But... that is for the best.*
*I can no longer hear his words either.*
*When I open my eyes, Hiyoshi-san is no longer there.*
*Moreover, I no longer care about him.*
*Losing consciousness was surely to prevent my mind from going all to pieces.*
*Really, what a useless effort.*
*Seeing as how my body has already gone all to pieces--*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san! What's wrong...? Please come back!"
*Even after what he's done to me, I still resist using violence against Hiyoshi-san.*
*Of course, I take into account that my strength would be no match for his.*
*More than anything, he had saved me at the jellyfish booth.*
*The one who saved my life -- my only thought is that I have to save him.*
Hiyoshi: "Gaaaaaaah!!"
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san... calm down. It's all right, it's all right..."
Hiyoshi: "You all... you all!"
Mayu: "Ouch...!"
*Ignoring my attempt to calm him, his large arm tenses, shooting out towards my leg.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san!"
*Even so, I want to save him.*
*This isn't something that comes from some sense of duty or ethics.*
*Just for myself, I want to save him.*
Hiyoshi: "You allllllll, after all you won'ttttttt evennnn!"
*As he scales my body, I somehow keep him from grabbing both legs.*
*Thanks to the fact that he is distracted, I am somehow able to get through the moment without being grabbed.*

Hiyoshi: "You all... won't lllllllook at meeeeeeee!"
*His words get caught up in my chest.*
*You won't look at me -- this thought seems to have been etched into his deranged mind.*
*However, denial comes first in my mind before any question.*
Mayu: "That's not true...! Because if I weren't looking at you, I wouldn't be thinking that I want to help you now, would I?!"
Hiyoshi: "You... wo..."
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san! Hiyoshi..."
Mayu: "Shinji-san!"
*Having been called by his name, the hand reaching toward me slowly lowers. Although the hand clutching my leg does not loosen, it is correct to say that he has stopped.*
Mayu: "Umm......"
Hiyoshi: "Guh......"
Mayu: "Huh......?"
Hiyoshi: "...... Me, please look at meeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeee!!!!!!!"
Mayu: "Kyaa!"
*Having suddenly broken his silence, Hiyoshi-san grabs onto my right leg with both hands. He pulls me towards himself with a strength that feels as though it could rip my leg apart, scraping the floor and coming closer to me as I resist.*
Mayu: "No, please stop!"
Hiyoshi: "Me! I want you all... you all toooooooo!!!"
Mayu: "Ow... it hurts... it hurts!"
*Grabbing tight onto my buttocks, he bites at me with the same vigor he had attacked my leg.*
*With a sensation as if I'd been pricked by a single needle, my skin bristles in sync with the flow of my blood.*

Mayu: "No, get away from m--!"
Hiyoshi: "You...... too......"
Mayu: "No... no...!"

Mayu: "Hrk..."
Hiyoshi: "... Kyahaha......"
*Hiyoshi-san wipes away the blood trickling down my butt with his tongue.*
*The sliding sensation creeps up my inner thigh, causing a ticklish sensation in addition to my disgust.*
Hiyoshi: "That's it... This is good... that reaction..."
Mayu: "No, no, no!"
*With a look of ecstasy on his face, he stares down at me. The hand clutching my leg has let go, but my whole body has gone limp.*
*I become aware that the area around the wound is gradually going numb. It's similar to the feeling of receiving anasthesia at the hospital. Even though I want to move, even though I want to run, my body won't listen to a thing I say.*
Hiyoshi: "This is... all you're good for."
Mayu: "Please st... op."
Hiyoshi: "If you won't look at me... I'll do this, even if it's bad... in exchange...!"
Mayu: "Nn...... sto-- Hahh..."
Hiyoshi: "Kyahahahahahahahahaha!!"
*Before I realize it, I've lost consciousness.*
*Whether loss of blood was the cause, or my heart, there is no one around to tell me the answer.*
*Floating in darkness, I can no longer see -- my eyes refuse to open.*
*That's because this is, so to speak, the end of me.*
Mayu: "Kenji-san!"
*Having been called by his name, the hand reaching toward me slowly lowers.*
*Though the hand clutching my leg does not loosen, it is correct to say that he has stopped.*
Mayu: "Kenji-san, it's... all right now! I'm looking... right at you!"
Hiyoshi: "Uh......"
*For some time, this serene period continues to permeate my entire being.*
*Unable to relieve the tension, I stare at the suspended Hiyoshi-san.*
Mari: "...... Is that so... big... sist--"
*Suddenly, the voice passes through my brain, piercing the silence. Then it just as suddenly cuts off.*
Mayu: "Wha?"
Hiyoshi: "That you... Mayu... chan?"
Mayu: "Kenji-san... Huh, just now, for a second--"
*Mari's voice-- I started to say, then shut my mouth.*

*Watching him as he bites down on his bottom lip so hard it bleeds, I decide to believe in his honest nature.*

Hiyoshi: "My body still feels stiff."

Hiyoshi: "Somehow-- Yeah. Oof."
*Resting his hands on his knees, he pulls his large body up.*
Mari: "...... Op......"
Mayu: "Huh, again...?"
Hiyoshi: "Gah!"
*A girl's voice echoes in my mind. A voice that whispered to me here in this aquarium -- my little sister's voice.*
*Almost in time with my frustration, Hiyoshi-san grimaces once again.*
Hiyoshi: "Crap, my head..."
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san!"
*I have no time to worry about Mari.*
*Having stood up almost halfway, Hiyoshi-san crumples as if his strings had been cut, tumbling to the floor of the passageway.*
*I rush over to him, grabbing him by the shoulders instinctively.*
Hiyoshi: "Don't... worry..."
Mayu: "Huh...?"
Hiyoshi: "It's not... unthinkable that I might revert... to the way I was earlier right...? If I do, leave me, go on..."
Mayu: "B-But..."
Hiyoshi: "You don't... need to worry. I'll... be fine."

*Despite the state he is in, the corners of his mouth twitch up with a "Haha..."*
Hiyoshi: "Aren't you... going to look for your friend? I'll follow you later on... so just go."
Mayu: "But... but..."
Hiyoshi: "Go!!"
*His bellow drowns out even my own breathing, reverberating down the passageway. For some time after he closes his mouth, the air seems to quiver.*
*However, I do not find him or his sudden outburst frightening.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san..."
Hiyoshi: "It hurts... that I caused you pain... Mayu-chan. You don't need to worry, if I rest a bit... I'll be fine. So..."
Mayu: "I understand... Are you sure? On my own... it's been nothing but frightening experiences..."
*As he closes his eyes, he gives me a thumbs up.*
*His haggard breathing continues as he deals with his own impending madness. He's trying to tough it out so that he doesn't hurt me again, creating puddles of sweat on the floor.*
*He is seriously worried about me.*
*Therefore I also need to seriously trust in him. No matter how many times I want to look back I fight off the impulse and, at a quick pace I'm not used to, I put the passageway behind me.*
Mayu: "Kanji-san!"
*No good, I'm at my limits. I give up trying to persuade him and begin moving my body in an attempt to escape.*
*As soon as the door is between us, I put my ear against it and listen to the sounds in the passageway I just left.*
*However, it is only my own violent pulse and not Hiyoshi-san going crazy that I can hear.*
*Honestly, I almost feel relieved.*
*Being separated like this leaves me feeling worried about him, but at the moment I don't have the time to spare.*
*I wonder if this is wrong...?*
Mayu: "It's natural that... I'd have... these doubts."
*I say over my shoulder.*
*The only thing to respond to my words is the wind rustling my hair.*
*As soon as the door is between us, I put my ear against it and listen to the sounds in the passageway I just left.*
*However, it is only my own violent pulse and not Hiyoshi-san's breathing that I can hear.*
*He was in a strange state. My mother had once had a high fever, but Hiyoshi-san seemed like he was suffering even more than that.*
*In spite of the circumstances, he had pushed me to go. Since I was worried about Himeno, he was giving my situation priority.*
Mayu: "I'll come back, no matter what."
*I mutter to no one in particular.*
Mayu: "That's right, this is..."
*Finally, I begin to confirm my location.*
*This place is semi-outdoors and even colder than the passageway. Despite the fact that it's summer, I shiver instinctively.*
*Below eye level, there are several plastic chairs in a row. In the center of the chairs, which are lined up like dolls on a tiered stand, is a stage that is a little smaller than a school classroom. Bowls and buckets are lying atop it.*
*In the center of the stage is something resembling a small shed.*
Mayu: "A stage... That's right, for things like the sea lions..."
*A stage, one facility that no aquarium should lack.*
*From kids to adults, the show is something everyone can enjoy. This small city aquarium, Manten Aquarium, still has a stage despite its size.*
Mayu: "Looks like it hasn't been used in awhile......"
*Upon muttering this, doubt enters my mind.*
*Hiyoshi-san said this place was on a shifted time axis, but even so, each section just feels too different.*
*The Fish of the World booth where the first strange phenomenon occured. The jellyfish booth and tunnel tank... thinking back on them in order, they each felt like their own distinct time period.*
*The countless fish that decayed, the immensely entrancing beauty... With each new facility I entered, the look changed.*
Mayu: "Ridiculous. There can't be..."
*I start to say, then shake my head. I dont know how many things I had thought impossible have come to pass here.*
*This crazy theory, perhaps--*
Mayu: "Never mind, I have to find Himeno..."
*Pushing my doubts to the edge of my mind, I head down the stairs for the guest seating towards the stage.*
Mayu: "Huh, this is...?"
*About halfway to the stage, I find a booklet on top of a seat that is thick enough to contain about fifty pages.*
*"Manten Aquarium Guidebook" is written on the front cover. That's all I see, just the name and nothing else.*
Mayu: "This has a map inside......"
*I suddenly realize as I am finishing my sentence.*
*Thumbing through the pages, I see that it has an introduction to Manten Aquarium. Other than that, there are bits of trivia about the fish on display, like that the pirarucu is the biggest freshwater fish, or that orca communicate using ultrasonic waves.*
*I close the guidebook with a slam. Other than the map, the rest is just information introducing the aquarium and doesn't seem as if it will be of any use.*
Mayu: "I want to read through this later."
*Though I don't want to part with the guidebook, I detach the map from it.*
*Not knowing what might happen, I come to the conclusion that I don't want to carry anything bulky.*
Mayu: "... Okay."
*Suppressing my desire to sit down and read it, I return the guidebook to the seat.*
Mayu: "Staff room. There might be someone there."
*On weeknights, they hold the adult-targeted "Manten Aquarium Exploratory Tour." It supposedly shows off the other side of Manten Aquarium.*
*For that expedition, pathways that customers are normally unaware of are printed in the guidebook.*
*According to the map, there appears to be a staff waiting area. It seems they don't stop there on the exploratory tour, but the place itself is printed on the map. If I go there, someone may be there.*
*Looking at the map, I wonder which way I should go as I follow the route.*
*It seems there are two paths to the staff room. The first path is through the passageway I went through earlier. The other seems to be through an underground passage connected to the inside of the shed on the stage.*
*Even though it's labeled as underground it is more accurate to say it is one floor down. I still don't know how Hiyoshi-san is, so I think I should take the latter path.*
Mayu: "I'm scared after all... Himeno... where are you...?"
*Lowering my defenses, these weak-hearted words well up inside me.*
*I mumble them as I step onto the stage.*
*Following those words, anxiety descends upon me.*
Himeno: "Ma...... yu...... I...... am... right...... here......"
Mayu: "No way, Himeno!"
*I doubt that voice, which seemed ready and waiting on me, was an illusion.*
Himeno: "No way... That's cruel, Mayu......"
Mayu: "Himeno!"
*I can hear Himeno's voice, but I can't see her. "Where is she?" I think as I glance about the stage. Himeno is nowhere to be found.*
Mayu: "That's right, inside the shed..."
*When the shed at the edge of the stage enters my line of sight, I head for it at double speed.*
*The floor is wet enough that it seems I should slip, but somehow I hold on and move forward.*
*The inside of the shed is as if a waiting area and a warehouse had been combined into one room.*
*The fishy stench is probably from the stains from food used during shows. In the past, I had seen a fish thrown to a sea lion that caught a ball with its nose.*
Mayu: "Himeno... Himeno...!"
*At the edge of the shed, in a plain metal chair against the wall, Himeno is leaning against the back in what appears to be a state of total exhaustion.*
Himeno: "Ma...... yu......"
Mayu: "I... I have so much to say to you Himeno... Because I-I..."
*Seeing her face, any thoughts of our argument are completely blown out of existence. Words that found no form are forever pushed away.*
*The fact that she is all right, all on its own, makes me happy.*
Himeno: "It's fine...... First of all, calm down...... Urk."
Mayu: "Himeno! What's wrong?!"
Himeno: "Ack... urk...... Huff, huff...... Mayu, don't make that face. There's nothing... to worry... about."
*I have a lot of things to say to Himeno, but my words get caught in my throat.*
*Seeing Himeno breathing heavily, my own pulse quickens. As I stand there, forgetting how to breathe, Himeno lays a hand on my chest.*
*Even when she had a cold, Himeno would still come to school with a smile on her face. When she got injured at club, she wouldn't take time off from gym class.*
*That's why, even in this aquarium, I thought somewhere in my heart that Himeno would be fine.*
*If only we could reunite, surely she'd lead with a smile. We'd go home side-by-side. That had been my hope.*
Himeno: "I'm...... sorry. Mayu, I...... don't think I can make it."
*It is so easy for Himeno's soft complaints to punch me right in the heart.*
*With just that one phrase, I drop to my knees in front of her.*
Mayu: "Hime..."
Himeno: "Mayu...... get up."
Mayu: "I won't. No, because...!"
*I shake my head at Himeno's words.*
*I can't remain standing, not while seeing my precious friend like this, and hearing her frail voice.*
*Everytime Himeno speaks, she spews white nematodes from her mouth.*
*I am not sure if she is aware of it or not, but thin creatures that look like mealworms wriggle at her feet.*
*With a clatter, Himeno seems as if she might fall from the chair. I support Himeno from under her arm, propping her up."
Himeno: "Thank...... you......"
Mayu: "Don't thank me. Himeno... what happened?"
*Himeno's body is hot. The same as when I had my 104 degree fever -- no, higher than that.*
Himeno: "I'm glad I got to see you again...... But...... I didn't want you to see me in this sta...... Hurk!"
*Unable to finish what she was saying, Himeno coughs up several nematodes along with some spit.*
*I don't want to see it. Not necessarily because it is weird or disgusting... I just don't want to see Himeno this way.*
Mayu: "Himeno, let's go to the hospital. We'll... eat ice cream, alright?"
Himeno: "Haha...... that's probably...... impossible now."
*Hearing the words that had fallen instinctively from my mouth, Himeno smiles back at me.*
*Even as I ignore the lie in Himeno's smile, I still become aware of it.*
Mayu: "It's not impossible!"
Himeno: "...... Mayu..."
Mayu: "It's not impossible. I'll take you to a hospital, ok...? So let's go."
Himeno: "Thanks...... That you feel that way...... fills me up."
Mayu: "It's not just my feelings, got it, Himeno? Can you stand......?"
Himeno: "Don't overdo it...... okay, Mayu? That's right... I... wanted to apologize to you, Mayu."
*In an attempt to lend her my shoulder, I take Himeno's arm, but she quickly brushes me off.*
*Himeno's hands are shaking. Despite this, she reaches into her pocket and pulls out the keychain.*
Mayu: "That's..."
Himeno: "Yeah, by the way, when I thought back...... to when I picked it up...... it most likely wasn't yours, Mayu. I'm...... such an idiot."
Mayu: "You're not! It was because I didn't listen at all to what you said... so..."
Himeno: "I... Because I was so convinced you had dropped it, Mayu...... Thinking back...... Ack, ack..."
*She's repeating the same thing over and over. Himeno's consciousness must be getting hazy.*
*Every time Himeno gets choked up, she vomits up viscous sputum and lively bugs.*
*Even so, I can't look away from Himeno.*
*I get the feeling that if I don't look at her face, I will never again see her smile.*
*I don't think I'm being overdramatic... or anything of the sort.*
*Since... I had never heard of someone throwing up bugs.*
Himeno: "Here...... you take it, Mayu."
Mayu: "Wha...? This keychain?"
Himeno: "Yeah, there seems to be no mistake someone dropped it...... That's why...... Give it back to them."
Mayu: "Give it back, that's... We're going back together, right? Manten Aquarium is small, surely we'll find them right away... won't we?"
Himeno: "Geez, Mayu you idiot. Can't you...... tell by looking? There's... no way I can...... go home."
Mayu: "No, don't Himeno! If you say that... it'll come to pass!"
*Himeno places the keychain in my palm, then softly places her hands once more on my shoulders.*
*She moves her hands from there to the back of my head. Then with a light, feather-like sensation she pulls me close to her.*
Mayu: "Hime... no..."
Himeno: "Mayu, get home... no matter what."
*That hug lasts only a few seconds. Then Himeno lightly pushes me away.*
Himeno: "I couldn't take it...... if I infected you. Urk... Ooh...... Just... just leave me here. Hahh, ugh...... ack..."
*Right as the end of her sentence fades away, her head flops forward.*
Mayu: "No way, no way..."
*Himeno's body, with her eyes shut and not even one fingertip moving, is hot.*
*Even so, Himeno won't turn her smile my way. She won't respond to my voice.*
Mayu: "No way, there's just... no way...!"
*Without a second thought, I hold Himeno. She said I might get infected, but that doesn't matter to me.*
*Having welled up inside her mouth, nemotodes crawl about Himeno's puffed up lips. Experiencing feelings of intense denial, I wipe away a nemotode and crush it.*
*A murky green liquid remains on my finger. I quickly rub it off on the floor.*
Mayu: "She's moving...?"
*With her chest so close to mine, I can feel a pulse.*
*No mistake, it is a heartbeat. Pulling away from Himeno, my trembling hand somehow lands on her left breast. Having sunk into that softness, I definitely feel a pulse going ba-bump ba-bump.*
Mayu: "She's alive! Himeno... I'm so glad."
*Just like that, I forget about rubbing off the filth and sit down.*
*Even with the bugs getting into her body, as long as she is alive -- I think it can work out somehow. I won't even consider the possibility of it not working out.*
*But I can't think of a concrete way to save Himeno. Within my completely blank mind, there is only anxiety and fear.*
*Having slumped more than sat down, a third person's voice reaches my ears.*
Rieko: "Anisakis and pseudoterranova... philometra that aren't supposed to infect humans... I wonder what exactly could cause something like this to happen...?"
Rieko: "Heehee, if the situation wasn't what it is, I would immediately preserve some of them in a petri dish."
Mayu: "Wha...?"
*From behind me, a cold voice interrupts my thoughts. In contrast with the feverish Himeno, that voice clings coolly to the nape of my neck.*
*It is a woman's voice I've never heard.*
Rieko: "It's a pity I cannot collect this precious sample. I don't have the luxury of time right now. I must find that child, quickly."
Mayu: "Uh, umm..."
Rieko: "My, what a cute girl. What is it? Might you have come to the aquarium to have fun with your friend?"
Mayu: "Aquarium... this is Manten Aquarium, right? That's right, me... and Himeno..."
Rieko: "So this girl, she's called Himeno-chan then."
*The woman looks to be in her thirties, her blue eyeshadow leaving quite the impression. Her appearance coupled with her calm manner of speaking both tell me she must be quite mature.*
*Her light-colored hair is collected into an updo and her suit is impeccably chosen.*
Mayu: "Those clothes... Um, could you be... an aquarium staff member?"
Rieko: "Yes, I am Saginuma Rieko. At this aquarium I am, I suppose, a... representative of the director?"
Mayu: "Like a... vice director?"
Rieko: "A little bit different. It would take some time to explain... More importantly, you should move away from Himeno-chan."
Mayu: "Huh...?"
*The woman called Himeno by name, with a coquettish tone. It gives me the impression of being very forced.*
*Crossing her arms as she looks down at me, the woman narrows her eyes.*
Rieko: "Would it be easier for you if I use the word infection? You have a wound on your leg, don't you? It's as if their fertility has intensified, meaning there are probably eggs amongst all that as well."
Mayu: "Wha...?"
Rieko: "She doesn't appear to be dead, but it is only a matter of time. You can no longer save Himeno-chan."
Mayu: "Th-That's... Please wait a second."
Rieko: "It's fine to leave her like that. After I've finished my business, I'll take her with me as a sample."
*The corners of Saginuma-san's mouth widen as she says this.*
*My fear of this woman is even greater than my anger at Himeno being referred to as a sample. Even though someone right in front of her eyes might die, she is smiling.*
*How in the world can she make that face -- because I can't understand, Saginuma Rieko-san seems terrifying to me.*
Mayu: "Please wait! If you're a member of the staff, then can't you take me to this building's first aid room or call an ambulance or something? Since... Himeno's alive?"
Rieko: "It seems you care for Himeno-chan a great deal. So, try to imagine this... if you die because of her, think about how Himeno-chan would feel."
Mayu: "Himeno is alive. Please don't say something like that... I'm begging you, please save Himeno!"
Rieko: "I am not a doctor. It's not possible. Moreover, I don't think the rescue workers would want to respond to such a hopeless call."
*Saginuma-san says this bluntly in a cruel voice. Her voice is colder than the first time I heard it, like water that has frozen over.*
*She can't be saved anymore -- everytime those words come from her mouth, my pulse grows louder and quicker. My blood is rushing through me fast enough for my veins to be worn down from within.*
Mayu: "That's not good enough. Please... save her... Please save Himeno!"
*Before I know it, I'm facing her and pleading.*
*However, my desperate pleas are being shattered by her cruel smile.*
Rieko: "I don't like disobedient children. I also have work to do. Important work... that matters."
Mayu: "You have work...? This place isn't normal, isn't that something a staff member should get...?"
*After fear makes a lap through me, I push aside my emotions.*
*Leaping over my fear and anxiety toward this woman, I wholeheartedly just want to save Himeno.*
Rieko: "Not normal? Whatever do you mean? As aquariums also serve as research institutions, the situation itself isn't normal, now is it?"
Mayu: "I don't... understand at all."
Rieko: "That's enough. No matter how much time I spend here it won't help. If you wish to join me, you are free to do so."
Mayu: "Free to join you... Saginuma-san, aren't you staff here? Are you saying you don't know anything about this aquarium filled with nothing but phenomena?"
Rieko: "I don't understand a word of what you're saying. Are these good things?"
Rieko: "I'm telling you I'm going now. If you wish to die along with her, then just sit there staring about like that. Later, I'll study you in full as well -- though one sample is more than enough."
*I shudder as a chill runs up my spine.*
*I am finally able to understand that this isn't a joke, that she is saying she sincerely doesn't care about whether Himeno lives or dies.*
Mayu: "Wait, please..."
Rieko: "You have wasted enough of my time. I must hurry up and find that child."
*The last bit of what Saginuma-san says is inaudible, as she runs out from the small room on the stage.*
*Being neither harmful nor harmless, that's the type of person she is.*
*Thinking she could point me down the one path that leads away from Himeno and I dying together, with just one phrase I am thrust down to rock bottom.*
Mayu: "Himeno... can't be saved."
*That single phrase bounces around my head numerous times.*
*No no no.*
*Turning it over in my head with such frequency, the truth Saginuma-san had stated is becoming engraved upon my heart.*
*I can no longer hear the click clack sound of her high heels. Instead, what resonates is the sound of drops falling from my eyes.*
Mayu: "I--"
Sayo: "If you're having doubts, then just die."
Mayu: "Huh... who...?"
*It is a girl's voice, much younger than Saginuma-san... Around the same age as me, in fact.*
*I turn towards the voice coming a short distance away from where I'm holding Himeno.*
*A slender girl who is a little taller than me, with eyes a slightly different color than Saginuma-san's cold eyes, looks down at me scornfully. Her long hair is tied back, dangling behind her like a tail.*
*She is a bit different from Himeno, but is still a girl in fashionable attire. I watch her with cautious interest as she glares at me.*
Mayu: "What's this...... all of a sudden?"
Sayo: "Who knows? I'm just annoyed with you, since I hate people who are so wishy-washy."
Mayu: "Wishy-washy... You think you can just show up and know how I'm feeling?"
Sayo: "I don't know. I'm not interested."
*I feel irritated at this girl who knows nothing of my circumstances showing up with all of these complaints. To think that Saginuma-san left and now this.*
*She is directing her futile impatience at me as well.*
*The girl is calm, but speaking fast. Hearing such a tone, I can't go on without saying something back.*
Mayu: "If you're not interested, then why did you speak to me? More importantly... you haven't gotten swept up in anything dangerous have you?"
Sayo: "Who knows?"
Mayu: "What's with that answer...? Just say what you want to say... How mean."
*When this girl started speaking to me, it raised hope somewhere inside of me.*
*I briefly hold out hope that she's talking to me because she knows some way I can save Himeno.*
*That's why I begin to feel something more than just irritation towards this girl who has come to kick me while I'm down.*
Sayo: "That girl's gonna get eaten up by bugs and die, right? But parasites aren't all that quick or efficient."
Mayu: "What are you...?"
Sayo: "Because it's 'this' Manten Aquarium, she will die. If it were me, that's what I would think."
Mayu: "Then what are you saying...?"
*My heart begins to beat faster at her words.*
*Now I regret placing so much hope in this girl. Nevertheless, from her way of speaking, could it be that...?*
Sayo: "If you get out of here, there may be a way to cure her. If it comes down to crying and dying, I would fight back rather than giving in."
Mayu: "Cure her...... Just now, you said 'cure her'...? Really? But that staff member earlier... said she could no longer be saved..."
Sayo: "I don't know. There's no guarantee she can be saved. But the one who said she couldn't be saved was an employee of the aquarium, right? Not a doctor."
*Not a doctor... She seemed like she knew a lot about parasites, but sure enough, that woman was not a doctor. She said so herself.*
*Also, the girl said something previously that caught my attention.*
Mayu: "Earlier, you said 'this' Manten Aquarium, right? What is this place?! If you know... then tell me..."
Sayo: "Don't take me for a fool."
Mayu: "But... I-I don't know. Since this is an aquarium where nothing but dangerous things happen, I want to know how to get out of here. I can't... save Himeno."
Sayo: "Which path you take is completely up to you. I don't have any more time to worry about other people. I'm just saying if it were me, I wouldn't quit until I was dead."
*Upon making her blunt assertion, the girl heads for the innermost part of the shed. Somehow, in contrast to the employee earlier, she had entered from the side of the stage.*
Mayu: "If you don't have time... then why are you encouraging me?"
*Encouraging me... Upon saying those words I get an awkward feeling.*
*The cutting assertions she made in a blunt tone and the words she said to me weren't exactly kind.*
*However, I found them kind in the moment. It isn't that I can't save her -- nor is it guaranteed that I can.*
*Having received even a small amount of hope, my tears have stopped.*
Sayo: "I told you already. I was just getting annoyed."
*Without looking back, the girl walks away.*
*Sitting on the ground huddled close with Himeno, I think over the two possibilities in my mind.*
*Since it's unlikely that I can save her, should I just stay here with her a second longer?*
*Or should I believe the girl's words and escape from this danger-filled aquarium?*
*Yes, that's best.*
*The possibility that I might be able to save her is low, isn't it? It isn't zero, but it's close to it.*
*I can't carry her around the aquarium with me, so I'll definitely have to leave Himeno here in this shed.*
*In that case, there's no guarantee I can get back here. With things like the rupture at the jellyfish booth and Hiyoshi-san's sudden change, the dangers present in Manten Aquarium are innumerable.*
*If I die along the way, it'll be what you'd call a dog's death.*
*Separated from Himeno, the both of us dead.*
Mayu: "No... I want us to be together."
*Still squatting, I hold Himeno even tighter. Her feverish body occasionally chokes up, then Himeno vomits out several nematodes. Several parasites squirm at the base of my throat.*
Mayu: "It's all right... it's okay."
*No matter what, it's okay. As long as I'm with HImeno...*
*I have no idea how much time has passed.*
*I no longer have a sense of time, but not more than an hour could have passed.*
*My body is no longer my own.*
*The depths of my body have been corroded with fever, and I no longer have any feeling in my hands or feet. I can no longer feel the softness and warmth of embracing Himeno.*
*My vision is filled with countless lines.*
*It feels as if something is actually crawling on my eyeballs.*
*This is my body at the moment. The lines covering my vision are probably all the same as the bugs eating away at Himeno's body.*
Mayu: "But... this is... fine..."
*As long as I can be with Himeno.*
*Before I realize it, my own consciousness has melted away.*
*I can no longer see Himeno's smiling face...*
*The possibility of saving Himeno is probably zero.*
*Or, just as the girl said, it may not be zero.*
*It is not completely outside the realm of possibility.*
*Moreover, Mari is here. Just as she appeared that day, Mari is here.*
*This aquarium where nothing but oddities occur -- Himeno's condition is also an undisputed abnormality. Saginuma-san herself said that the parasites eating away at Himeno were ones that wouldn't normally attack humans.*
Mayu: "If that's the case... I have to go."
*Right now, the only one who can save Himeno is me. It isn't as if that staff member, or the mysterious girl, or Hiyoshi-san, or anyone else will save Himeno.*
Mayu: "I have to walk on my own two legs..."
*If I don't, Himeno will not be saved.*
*At the same time as I stand up, I accept that determination. I have no time for my heart to be breaking.*
*My legs are still shaking, but all I have is the certainty that I am standing right now.*
*I spread a nearby blue tarp on the floor and lay Himeno on top of it. I find a towel, fold it up several times, and lay it over her. As crass as it is, she now has a futon and blanket.*
Mayu: "Just a little while... Just wait a little while."
*First, I should check the entrance once more.*
*If we came to this warped space-time Manten Aquarium, then somewhere there should be a "connection." It shouldn't be one-way. I go back to my original idea, deciding to head for the entrance gate.*
*If I can get out normally from the gate, then next I'll seek a safe route. It will take some time for me to move Himeno, so I need to carefully ensure that no more phenomena will occur.*
*In the event that I can't get out through the gate--*
*It would mean that we're trapped inside Manten Aquarium. If that's the case, then I'll look for a way to break out of this cage. Circumstances won't be any worse, just different. It will be all right.*
*My plans clarified, I feel as if even I can do this.*
*I have no special skills, and my personality is probably introverted, but my desire to save Himeno is second to no one's.*
*Hiyoshi-san is probably in the staff passage I came through earlier, but it shouldn't be a danger I cannot overcome.*
*If I can run past him in one shot, surely it will all be okay.*
*I open the door carefully with a soft ka-chak.*
*It's at that moment.*
Hiyoshi: "Gaaaaaaaaaaah!"
Mayu: "Gyaah!"
*A sharp pain runs up from the base of my neck. Whatever bit my neck knocks me to the ground.*
Mayu: "No... not good..."
Hiyoshi: "Gaah... gah..."
*With each bite, the area around my neck makes a squelching sound.*
*The cold stench of iron and the smell of blood.*
*It's the smell of my blood and the saliva of whatever is doing this to me mixing together.*
*Choked up by the stench, I realize that the air filling my mouth is hitting the bottom of my chin.*
*Breath from my own mouth shouldn't reach the bottom of my chin. The inconsistency here is because that breath is being vented from an airhole that has opened in my neck.*
Mayu: "Ka... Kahi..."
*My voice isn't a voice anymore. It is nothing more than the sound of air escaping as, unable to offer resistance, my body is overrun by that "something."*
Hiyoshi: "Gahhhh..."
Mayu: "...!"
Hiyoshi: "Gaah, gah."
Mayu: "...! ......! ... Haah...!"
*There is no pain. All I can feel is the gradual depletion of my own flesh. That "something's" head bobs up for a second.*
*It's someone I know, the face of a man I know.*
Mayu: "........."
*Before long, everything is engulfed in silence.*
*Surely, this is my death.*
*Being eaten by the one who saved my life -- Hiyoshi-san. It is merely my tiny death, and nothing more--*
*Moreover, I am concerned about Hiyoshi-san's condition. Just before I came to the stage, his sanity had returned.*
*Walking across the center of the stage, I head for the door that connects to the passage.*
*I open the door cautiously, peering in through the crack.*
*With no hint of a person in the darkness, I open the door all the way.*
Mayu: "Huh, no one's here..."
*There are no signs of human life, just the sound of machine engines going "whirr, whirr" echoing.*
*The grey tone of the walls, contrasted with the pale glow of the flourescent lights, is eerie.*
*At a bit of a loss, I decide to head back to the stage.*
*Even though he could move, he didn't come to the stage for some reason.*
*I can think of several reasons, like that his physical condition deteriorated or that he had gone crazy. In the end, the fact that he decided not to join me remains the same.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san."
*He had urged me to keep going. Not being able to see him has me worried, but I will keep moving.*
*In order to get back to the gate, it seems there are two similar routes, but I'm afraid to take the path containing the insane Hiyoshi-san.*
*Hiyoshi-san was clearly not normal.*
*I have the desire to save him, but speaking honestly, protecting myself takes priority.*
*Moreover, as opposed to Himeno, he didn't seem to be headed down a straight path to death.*
*If I can get out of the aquarium, I should go check on Hiyoshi-san's condition once more at that time.*
*Because there's no changing the fact that he is the one who saved my life.*
*I head back inside the shed and decide to move on using the underground passage. Even if by accident, that was the way the mysterious girl had disappeared.*
*Opening the door, I step down a rusty stairway. It's a spiral staircase, and as I step off at the very bottom, I see a dim underground passage stretching out before me.*
*After going down the passageway a bit, I run into a door. It seems that if I do not pass through this room, I cannot proceed. Upon slowly opening the door, I see several computers and various other machines.*
*Since I see lots of levers and dials with numbers and up/down symbols on them, this must be where all the machines in the aquarium are controlled.*
*They probably control the tank temperature, oxygen regulation, lighting, and such from here as well.*
Mayu: "At any rate, I should keep moving."
*As I proceed, the sound of my footsteps echoes with a click-clack, click-clack. An echo lingers with a cla-clack sound.*
Mayu: "It figures, this place is creepy too..."
*I mumble something like that after all that has happened. Even as I reflect on this and walk on, a jarring sound blends in with my footsteps.*
Mayu: "What was... that sound just now?"
Sayo: "Gh... Ugh..."
*Puzzled, I strain my ears and can hear someone moaning.*
*The voice is coming from my destination, the interior of the cellar. Despite my hesitation, I proceed onward with caution.*
Sayo: "Pl... ea... se... lp..."
Mayu: "Wha...?"
*After taking several steps, the voice becomes clearer. Peering through a gap in the machines, I discover the origin of the voice.*
Mayu: "K-Kyaaaah!"
*I scream without thinking. In a corner of the control room, something completely detached from reality is taking place.*
Mayu: "Is that... an orca? B-But how...?"
*I give voice to what lies before my eyes. A plump, round body with a distinct contrast between its colors of black and white. On the opposite side of its sharp, shark-like fin is its mouth, with its tapered teeth all lined up.*
*That's right, orca are swimming around in here. In this room with no water, they swim about in the air.*
*It isn't just that they float in the air; they move and swim about using their fins and bodies.*
*Moreover, there isn't just one. Two... no, three. There are three of them swimming right before my eyes; marine creatures that are much longer than the span of my two arms if I were to spread them wide.*
Mayu: "H-How...?"
*The orca continue to swim about, unaware of my presence.*
Mayu: "No... at this rate, I'll be attacked..."
*As I realize this, I step back. However, immediately after I do so, I catch sight of the owner of that small voice.*
Mayu: "Th-That girl is..."
*The three orca are swimming around, encircling something as if it were their prey.*
*That something at the center is a girl. A girl around the same age as me. A girl I ran into only a short while ago on the stage.*
*Her eyes are wide, and she trembles with a terrified expression, standing stock still as her only escape route is blocked...*
*Her mouth is flapping open and closed, as if she is having trouble breathing.*
Mayu: "No, I can't worry about that right now."
*I avert my eyes from the girl and the orca, backing against the wall as much as possible.*
*Against the wall I stealthily move on, a step at a time.*
*I'm sorry for the girl, but I have no time to waste right now. I have to help Himeno as quickly as possible.*
*Trying to make no noise, I head cautiously for the exit of the passage.*
Mayu: "Ugh..."

Mayu: "Huh... Wha...? I'm spinning round and round..."

Mayu: "No, no. Why......?"

*My vision is spinning, leaving me unable to grasp whether the floor is the wall or the wall is the floor.*
Mayu: "... Ahh... Ack..."
*Gradually, breathing begins to feel difficult. I can't breathe. I can't get enough oxygen.*
Mayu: "Ack... Hahh..."
*Before I know it, I'm in the ocean. I struggle to right myself in this unchanging ocean with no up or down.*
*If there's no ground, that means it's impossible to do something like stand...*
*When the orca hunt, they fire off ultrasonic waves. This causes their prey to lose the sense of equilibrium in their semicircular canals.*
*Long ago, I heard that somewhere.*
*So, it's already too late.*
*I am prey. Merely bait for a predator to feed on.*
*Becoming their focus before I realize it, the three orca are swimming around me. Having no sense of equilibrium, there is no way I can tell which orca is to the left of me, and which is to the right.*
*Adjacent to my spinning vision is a dark brownish-red clump. The deep red liquid flowing from that clump blends with the sea water.*
Mayu: "... Guh......"
*Whether I flee or not, my fate has been decided.*
*Within this ocean where I cannot see the bottom, I await the same conclusion as that piece of meat.*
Mayu: "--!!!!"
*With me chosen as their next target, I will be eaten by those orca.*
*The tips of those tapered teeth sink into my skin. Being torn apart is just as it is explained in reference books, with the orca taking my arm completely off.*
*No scream will come out.*
*That's because my voice dissolves into the water.*
*That distinct pain detaches me from reality.*
*But I still haven't eaten ice cream with Himeno...*
*Those words appear, vaguely out of place in my wavering consciousness.*
*I can think of nothing but saving Himeno.*
*So I'm sure... she'll be happy...*
*I don't even know that girl's name.*

Mayu: "I'll save that girl."
Sayo: "... Some... one..."
*I hear those timid words she mumbles.*
*Little by little, the three orca swimming in the air seem to be tightening the circle they have made.*
*I have to hurry--*
Mayu: "But what should I do?"
Mayu: "That's... a fire extinguisher......"
*At the edge of the room, I discover a fire extinguisher.*
Mayu: "If I can attract their attention with this..."
*And somehow while the orca are distracted, I would need to get away. I pull the yellow pin from the fire extinguisher and point the hose at the orca.*
Mayu: "Aghhhhh!"
*In time with my shout, I pull the nozzle of the fire extinguisher with everything I have. At that moment, white smoke sprays out.*
*Hidden in the smoke, the orca's forms immediately vanish.*
Mayu: "Take this opportunity and run for it!"
*I shout, leaving the fire extinguisher on the ground. I then run for the exit.*
Mayu: "Huh..."
*It is only a second later that I feel something strike my back.*
*Hit so hard it's incomparable to bumping into people in town, I pitch forward and fall.*
Mayu: "No, kyaaaaaaaaaah!"
*That scream is involuntary.*
*Little by little, the area around my neck starts to hurt, and I scream once more.*
*My breath grows light. Air is blowing out of the hole that has opened in my neck.*
Mayu: "Ack... Hahh, huff..."
*After awhile, my screams will not even come out anymore.*
*An orca is right in front of me. The three orca had been with that girl, and one more... was right where I had been heading.*
*It is useless to wonder about where it came from.*
*With the orca swimming in air, the concept of space itself has become more than ambiguous.*
*My body is overrun with the orca's small, sharp teeth.*
Mayu: "Urk."
*I try to raise my voice, but I realize my mouth is full of seawater. Before I figure out what's going on, I'm in the ocean.*
Mayu: "--!!!"
*The blood exiting from my body gradually flows upward. I'm not just in the ocean, but it seems I'm in the deep part of it.*
*As I try to fill my chest with air, water flows into my lungs from the hole in my neck.*
*My body will likely return to the sea...*
*As I think this, my body, which has taken on buoyancy, seems as if it belongs to someone else.*
Sayo: "Yes, you are dead."
Sayo: "Being too kind-hearted is mere foolishness."
Sayo: "Well, I'm in a hurry."
Sayo: "I have something I have to do..."
Mayu: "What...?"
*It is only a second later that I feel something strike my back.*
*Hit so hard it's incomparable to bumping into people in town, I pitch forward and fall.*
Mayu: "No, kyaaaaaaaaaah!"

*My body will likely return to the sea...*

Sayo: "... Yes... you too..."
*Before my eyes is one solitary red eyeball.*
Sayo: "...... Let's both curse... this aquarium......"
*Who is that?*
*From someplace up high, I'm following a pink shadow.*
*It's moving.*
*It's moving... beneath me.*
Himeno: "Mayu... where are you...?"
*I see... she's alive.*
*If so, then I need to bring her with me.*
*Into this malevolent aquarium.*
*It's dangerous to get close to the orca. That's why I can't think of a way to undo these circumstances.*

Mayu: "Something I can use..."
*Hoping against hope, I place my hand on the computer console. The liquid crystals on the touch panel seem to power on, causing some sort of control screen to be displayed.*
Mayu: "This is..."
*The tunnel tank, the stage, the jellyfish booth...*
*Aside from those, the names of various facilities in the aquarium are displayed. From this computer, I can probably access each of those facilities.*

*The control screen for the air, the speakers, and so on is displayed.*
*How much does the habitat of these orca have in common with real orca?*
*Realizing I don't have the time to think it over, I decide the temperature settings are the best place to start. It's ideal for humans, but it could burn the sea creatures.*
Mayu: "It's no use, this will take too much time."
*The roar of the air is fierce, but there is little change in what I can feel on my skin.*
*The orca have begun probing the girl with the tips of their noses.*
*The girl provides no resistance, her mouth flapping open and closed.*
Mayu: "This is bad, I have to... "
*Small but sharp teeth peek out from the small opening in the orca's mouth. The tips of its teeth seem to sparkle, and my pulse quickens.*
*There is no time left to test another method.*
*... The sensation of pain.*
*I have heard that fish have no sense of pain, but orca are mammals. The same as humans. There's no doubt they would have the ability to feel pain.*
*I take my eyes off the computer and glance about my surroundings. I search for something that can serve as a weapon.*
Mayu: "That's..."
*I spot a mop that has fallen over in a corner of the room. I approach the mop and grab it, deciding that this will be my weapon.*
*The orca have begun probing the girl with the tips of their noses.*
*The girl provides no resistance, her mouth flapping open and closed.*
Mayu: "This is bad, I have to..."
*Small but sharp teeth peek out from the small opening in the orca's mouth. The tips of its teeth seem to sparkle, and my pulse quickens.*
*There is no time left to test another method.*
*-- The sense of smell.*
*No, I have to calm down. Sharks have a keen reaction to the smell of blood, so it could be the same for orca.*
*No, that shouldn't be it. I have heard that in exchange for the development of their sense of hearing, orca hardly use their sense of sight or sense of smell.*
*When I take my eyes off the monitor, I spot a mop that has fallen over in a corner of the room. Orca are supposed to have the ability to sense pain. If that's the case, then that will be my weapon.*
Mayu: "If I aim for the eyes, even I can do it!"
*Even I know how powerless I am. But if it's a soft spot like the eyes, I should be able to get them to fall back with this mop.*
Sayo: "Ergh..."

Mayu: "Aaaahhhhhhhhhhh!"

Mayu: "No! No! Aaaaaaagh!"

*When I pull the mop out, the left eye dangles from the still-connected red muscle.*

*Before my eyes is a large body.*
*It presses against my abdomen with a strength that contrasts with its cute exterior.*
*No, it can no longer be considered cute.*

*It's left eye, dangling from a red muscle, glares at me.*
Mayu: "Wh... y...?"
*The stench of a rotting corpse is already wafting off the orca's body.*

*The mop is easily broken by the orca's body and floats around me.*
Mayu: "Urk..."

*No. There is no longer a ceiling there.*

*Instead of air, I breathe in water. It's salty. Seawater.*
*I can no longer breathe.*
*A dull sound resonates. Called by the sound, two orca follow.*
Mayu: "Haah..."
*As if playing around, two of them ram me with their bodies. My insides are jumbled. Something bitter wells up in my mouth. I vomit up something reddish-brown.*
*It resembles the guts on an anatomical model. The only difference is how soft this is.*
*The orca bare their fangs, as if they've finished playing. Little by little, my body is diminished. I am becoming a part of the orca.*
*Unable to be properly consumed by the orca I was supposed to kill, my flesh spills out from its eyeballs.*
*That's right, the eyes and mouth are connected.*
*There's one more thing I've realized.*
*That I'm probably already dead.*
*The orca have begun probing the girl with the tips of their noses.*
*The girl provides no resistance, her mouth flapping open and closed.*
Mayu: "This is bad, I have to..."
*Small but sharp teeth peek out from the small opening in the orca's mouth. The tips of its teeth seem to sparkle, and my pulse quickens.*
*There is no time left to test another method.*
*I have to find some sort of solution from within the machine right in front of me.*
Mayu: "The speaker..."
*I discover the volume setting on the liquid crystal display, and hazily recall something inside my mind.*
Mayu: "Sound... ultra-sonic waves..."
*... Their sense of hearing!*
*I remember something I saw on TV about orca and their environment.*
*Dolphins, whales, and other similar species use sound for communication. The various species make noise and can comprehend that sound. These are creatures with excellent hearing.*
Mayu: "Anything's fine, just some noise!"
*That's my only hope, I think.*
*If I can make a loud enough sound, I can probably repel the orca.*
*Setting aside my feelings of impatience, I set the volume as high as possible. All that's left is where the playback comes from.*
Mayu: "I wonder where the sound comes out... huh?"

Sayo: "------..."

Sayo: "----..."

Sayo: "... Right. I accidently...... the howling and the clicking sound... You may...... a bit of a...... hearing."

*The clicking sound -- the ultrasonic waves the orca emit. The loud volume of that sound probably repelled the orca. The illusion of being in the water is also gone.*
*Somehow, we seem to have escaped danger.*

Mayu: "Good..."

Mayu: "Huh... Mari...?"

Mayu: "W-Wait a second..."

Mayu: "Malicious... alternate dimension? What exactly do you mean?"

*The girl rejects my proposition flat-out, giving the impression that she would never accept.*

Mayu: "But I was planning to go this way too..."
*That's right, in the end moving forward means our destinations are the same. Moving at a trot, I follow the girl.*
*There are stairs at the end of the underground passage. Climbing those stairs leads to a waiting room for staff. According to the map, it seems this waiting room is connected to the passageway where Hiyoshi-san went crazy.*
*However, I cannot hear any particularly strange sounds.*

Sayo: "Why are you following me?"
Mayu: "Because... in order to find the clues to escaping from this aquarium, I have to keep moving forward."
Sayo: "Is that so?"
Mayu: "Um... by the way, can you tell me your name?"
Sayo: "If you're going to ask someone their name, you should give yours first."
Mayu: "R-Right. I'm Nakanobe Mayumi. I'm usually called Mayu... How about you?"
Sayo: "There's no need for me to tell you."
*The girl mixes a sigh in with this brief conversation. Thinking that I'm the one who feels like sighing, somehow the conversation has come to a close.*
*Though her attitude towards me is cold, she has shown no outright aggression, reassuring me that I'm no longer alone.*
Mayu: "It... feels like someone was here recently."
*I mutter, on spotting someone's clothes and partially consumed sports drink.*
*As if having reached the same conclusion, the girl returns my glance.*
Mayu: "In this aquarium, each room is completely different... Being completely ruined, or being in use like this room..."
Sayo: "You think so too, then."
*She's surprisingly receptive to what I said.*
*The girl sits down on a sofa in the staff room, motioning for me to sit opposite her. She seems to be telling me she wants to talk everything over thoroughly.*
Sayo: "The phenomena of Manten Aquarium. You mentioned how every room is different, but I think that each door you take is different."
Mayu: "Umm... what exactly do you mean?"
Sayo: "I hoped you'd be sharper, but you're a block head after all? You have also experienced opening a door to find things have changed. Am I wrong?"
Mayu: "Yeah... I have. Things like hearing strange voices or having a normal person go crazy."
*The girl places her hand on her chin, a serious expression appearing on her face. I didn't think she would listen to what I had to say at all, but apparently Manten Aquarium is the exception.*
Mayu: "You said 'also,' does that mean you have as well?"
Sayo: "When I entered one room, I was choked by the stench. I thought it was from the tanks not being filtered. But no matter how many rooms I passed through, I realized the situation in each room was completely different."
Mayu: "It was the same for me..."
Sayo: "Thinking I didn't completely grasp the situation, I tried going back the way I came. When I did so, the situation was completely different the first and second time around. At first, the fish in the stinky tanks were swimming about. Do you get it?"
Mayu: "When you open the door, the circumstances of the room change...?"
Sayo: "At the very least, the doors are the key. After hearing your story, I'm convinced."
*After saying this, the girl rises and adjusts the hem of her skirt.*
Sayo: "Well, I'll be off."
Mayu: "Umm... wait a minute... Shouldn't we share information, like what things are like in each area?"
Sayo: "There's no need. It changes each time you open a door, so all of our experiences up to this point are in the past."
*With that cold attitude, the girl puts her hand on the exit door.*
*Somehow I feel that trying to stop this girl from going on alone is pointless.*
*Slumping my shoulders with the realization that I will once again be alone, I discover a book lying on the sofa.*
Mayu: "Where you were sitting just now, is that... a diary?"
Sayo: "A diary? Could it belong to one of the staff of Manten Aquarium?"
*The girl reacts with sudden interest and returns to the spot facing me.*
*Somehow, just as the girl said, it seems to be the diary of a Manten Aquarium staff member. A bit larger than a paperback, it is similar to a pocket notebook.*
Sayo: "Let me see it."
Mayu: "Ah..."
*The girl takes the diary from my hands and opens it.*
Sayo: "This date, it's from five years ago."
Mayu: "From... five years ago?"
*Five years ago.*
*The date Mari went missing and that the incident the girl mentioned occured. Manten Aquarium was the thing these two incidents had in common.*
*This diary has something important written inside. That is what my intuition is telling me.*
Mayu: "I... want to look, too."
*Pulling back the girl's arm as she reads the diary on her own, I force my way in so I can peek at it as well.*
*In the beginning, it is all a simple description of this person's job.*
*I'm interested in how the fish are cared for and the hidden workings of the aquarium, but now is not the time.*
*The girl flips through the book, looking for important information. This is what I want as well.*
Mayu: "This is..."
Sayo: "Let's take our time going through this part."
*The date is one I recognize. A cold day, halfway through January. The date of my first visit to Manten Aquarium, a day of destiny.*
Diary: "There's been an accident. A big one. The fish in the Deep-sea Fish booth died due to an error in the pressure control valve, and in the ensuing panic, Director Sakuragi died as problems with the machinery increased."
Diary: "The loan on the Deep-sea Fish booth hasn't even been paid off yet, but tomorrow it will be closed down right away. It's being called a closure, but the staff is being called together. Ah, I'm so down."
Sayo: "I'm moving on."
Mayu: "... Okay."
*The girl continues flipping through the diary. Could this director's death have been the incident the girl was talking about?*
*For some time after that entry, it seems to be just his grumblings. Evidently the girl is skipping over these parts. Seems this employee was going through some tough times.*
Diary: "It's been three months to the day since we started operating with the Deep-sea Fish booth closed down. Customer traffic has decreased, even though we do have the tunnel tank and jellyfish booth."
Diary: "Thanks to it being promoted as a date spot, the amount of couples coming in is increasing. Our bonuses have probably been cut, but it seems we can continue without losing any staff."
*Finally, an optimistic entry.*
*Three months since the accident. For a recovery, it happened pretty fast.*
*I don't think I was as positive as he was three months after Mari disappeared.*
*The girl finally begins to read on in the diary. After a month passes in the diary, the speed with which she flips through the pages slows down.*
Diary: "Lately, strange rumors have begun to spread, like that the water in the tanks turns to blood, or that the director's ghost swims about."
Diary: "They're urban legends. I don't know who's spreading these rumors, but they seem to be popular amongst high school girls."
Diary: "Thanks to that, the amount of high school girl customers is increasing... It's probably a one time boom. Once the boom is over, there's no doubt that the amount of customers will recede in contrast."
Mayu: "They've been around for five years... these rumors."
*The girl nods at my words. She still doesn't take her eyes off the diary.*
Diary: "Though the rumors should be fading into obscurity, they seem to be steadily increasing in their grandeur."
Diary: "I've been asked things by girls like 'The director was killed, right? It was a murder, right?' That shouldn't be happening. Well, it's fine as long as the girl is cute."
*For a second, a scowl appears on the girl's face.*
*This staff person's candid thoughts aren't really reassuring. It would probably be impossible for anyone to remain neutral about this, so I can sympathize with the girl's sullen expression.*
Diary: "Even amongst the staff here, rumors have begun to spread. Even those who are decent adults are keeping them alive. I want Kozuki to stop with the 'There are so many fishy things about the accident in the Deep-sea Fish booth' and playing detective." "
*The girl pauses briefly as she thinks something over, but when she realizes I am looking at her, she flips the page noisily.*
Diary: "That Kozuki, he's been out for three days now. He's pushed his luck too far by encouraging these rumors."
*It doesn't seem to be... just someone skipping work. The girl's puzzled expression gives a hint of something more.*
