*Neither Kenji-san nor Rieko-san appears agitated by this.*
Hiyoshi: "Listen, okay?"
*I realize that the tone of Kenji-san's voice has changed, and I swallow.*
Hiyoshi: "The Red Manten Aquarium is itself a gathering of numerous wills. You can call those wills 'souls.' The murder of the director and the resulting grudge serve as a core, and the base of each of those souls is made up of that directive."
Hiyoshi: "Primarily, the fish souls, which lack the same thinking as humans, use that directivity to maintain this malicious world."
Sayo: "In order to gather those souls, you spread that rumor amongst stupid junior high girls, didn't you?"
Rieko: "Hehe. 'Stupid,' you say, even though you're the same as they are."
*Sayo clicks her tongue in response to Saginuma Rieko's words.*
*Kenji-san continues, unaffected.*
Hiyoshi: "Generally, those trapped souls lack individuality. Just like an animal, the existence known as the Red Manten Aquarium has no instinct but to preserve itself. Since more than half of the souls comprising this world are fish, it only makes sense."
Hiyoshi: "However, it is possible for individuality to be regained in concert with the visit of a human they are linked to."
Hiyoshi: "You told me you ran into someone you knew long ago. If that person's link to you is strong enough, they may appear as an 'individual.' You could also call them a ghost or a vengeful spirit."
Hiyoshi: "Just like the girl at your side... Mari-chan."
Mari: "Thbbt~"
*Even as Kenji-san looks at her suggestively, Mari merely sticks out her tongue and pulls down her eyelid.*
*His story up to now isn't that different from what Sayo and I expected.*
*But as we're waiting, he continues the conversation.*
Hiyoshi: "Your bond is too strong, or it's possible that the directivity that Mari's soul possesses is completely different. The two of you being brought together alone may have been enough to put Manten Aquarium in danger of being destroyed."
Hiyoshi: "If we carelessly killed Mayu-chan and the two souls came together, this place would surely be liberated from the curse."
Hiyoshi: "Like the plankton who can't go against the ocean current, the souls pooled here in Manten Aquarium would follow the direction of the pure love the two of you share. It would defeat the hatred that serves as this place's directive."
Hiyoshi: "That's why, at first, I tried to keep you two apart. After that, I would get rid of Mari. If I killed Mayu-chan a month later, we could safely continue supplementing the aquarium with life energy. We could continue on peacefully..."
Hiyoshi: "Since you brought Himeno-chan with you, fate could have gone either way. That was a close call."
Mayu: "Are you saying that's the reason you saved me from danger, then?"
*I want him to deny it.*
*I had sincerely thanked him, and I had respected him.*
Hiyoshi: "Yeah. It'd be a burden if you died so carelessly."
Mayu: "You..."
*It feels as if everything supporting me has crumbled away.*
*Even so, the fact that Mari and Sayo are with me becomes my strength.*
*Somehow, I'm able to continue standing.*
*I'm not sure if Sayo has stopped paying attention to me or not, but she begins to ask Kenji-san her own question.*
Sayo: "But I heard you once tried to kill Mayumi?"
Hiyoshi: "That's right. That was careless. There is proof that your bond created resentment."
Hiyoshi: "Still not completely in possession of her individuality, but in connection with Manten Aquarium, Mari-chan was trying to warn you, Mayu-chan, when you were with me."
Hiyoshi: "My connection with Manten Aquarium was temporarily severed. Because of that, I was ensnared by the grudge of Manten Aquarium, and Hiyoshi Kenji's true nature became exaggerated."
Hiyoshi: "If I had just been a soul, I would have disappeared on the spot and never attacked you, Mayu-chan."
Sayo: "So you are living after all. You're the main one spreading all of those urban legends amongst junior high girls."
Hiyoshi: "You could say that. But it's difficult to say whether or not I'm actually living."
Rieko: "You talk too much... Kenji-san. There's no point in telling the ones you'll kill all of this. Time is eternal, but it's best to leave out the unnecessary."
Mayu: "Kill...?"
Sayo: "Myself aside, wouldn't it be a problem if you killed these two?"
Rieko: "I told you, didn't I...? Heehee... Our plans have changed."
*As Saginuma Rieko says those words, the entire room undergoes a change.*
*A faint tremor reaches the back of my legs.*
*It gradually grows larger.*
*The antique furniture in the director's office shakes even more violently.*
*I've heard of poltergeist phenomena. It's when objects move on their own or people suddenly hear noises. I'm sure that's what this is.*
*When I notice the water, it's turned red like blood.*
*The red color doesn't stop with the tank, but overflows into the rest of the room as a red light.*
Mari: "Big sister, Sayo-chan, watch out!"
Mayu: "I know. This is..."
Sayo: "They're connected with 'this place.' In other words, they can interfere with the phenomena that happen 'here.'"
*The vibrations become more violent, like a strong earthquake, leaving me unable to stay standing.*
*Mari clings to me, as I cling to the shaking desk.*
Mayu: "Kyaah!"
*The books that fly off the bookshelves come straight at me.*
*Somehow I shield myself with my hand, but a corner hits the bone of my arm, leaving a bruise. The bookshelf is overflowing, scattering everything on top of the desk.*
*The glass picture frame has fallen onto the floor, breaking on impact, and tearing the picture inside. The smile of the girl depicted in the photo has been split in two, right down the middle.*
Sayo: "Mayu, we have to get out of here now."
Mayu: "But... the exit is blocked!"
*Moreover, the murderous intent of that pair is unmistakably real.*
*But just as Sayo says, if we don't get out of here, we can't regroup.*
*Just like when we were attacked by orca or frogs, this phenomena is probably also being orchestrated by those two.*
Sayo: "Under the chair! There's a hidden passage that leads to the Deep-sea Fish booth!"
Mayu: "Huh...? I can't hear you!"
*The violent shaking cancels out our voices.*
Mari: "Big sister, under the chair! Let's go together!"
Mayu: "Y-Yeah... Kyaah...!"
*As soon as I take a step, I stumble.*
*The shaking of the room has gotten so violent, I can't walk very easily.*
Mayu: "Guh..."
Sayo: "Mayumi, you're caught up in something."
*Sayo's voice sounds like she's in pain. Unable to stand the shaking any longer, she sinks to the ground. Unlike me, she has nothing to grab onto, so it's natural.*
*Leaning on the desk as it continues to shake, I twist my body and move under the chair.*
Mayu: "This is..."
*Just as Sayo said, there's a square on the floor made of a different material from the rest. In the middle of the wooden floor is an out-of-place metal area. It also has a handle, which I grab onto.*
*With all my might, I pull it up.*
Mayu: "No......"
Sayo: "Hurry, do it."
*I almost can't hear Sayo's voice, only understanding that she's hurrying me along.*
Mari: "I'll help too."
*Mari stretches her hand around my waist and pulls. But the door in the floor won't budge.*
Mayu: "Why?!"
*As far as we can tell, it isn't that it's locked.*
Rieko: "Ahahaha! How amusing!"
Mayu: "I have to hurry..."
*This won't end with the poltergeist phenomena alone.*
*But as we struggle against insurmountable odds in the deep red room, Saginuma Rieko merely laughs.*
Hiyoshi: "Sorry, Mayu-chan. This is for what they call 'the survival instinct of a seed.'"
*Kenji-san is saying something.*
*It's similar, as if to embody my bad premonition.*
Mayu: "What... is that...?"
*Within the red light, a large black shadow wriggles about.*
*It doesn't take much time to realize that the shadow itself is a living thing.*
Mari: "Mr... Whale..."
*We've stopped trying to pull on the door, looking up at the large creature.*
*Unable to fit into the room, it crashes into the wall. No, if it were just a ghost, then a container like a mere room would be irrelevant.*
Rieko: "My... Kenji-san. So it's making an appearance. The world's largest living creature -- the blue whale."
Hiyoshi: "It's not something I can completely control. With the malice being born fron Manten Aquarium under these circumstances, it's likely been created spontaneously."
Sayo: "It is a whale. It wasn't here originally, like the orca and frogs and jellyfish, so why...?"
Hiyoshi: "This is another bond. The memory of the greatest terror engraved onto the hearts of the fish souls. It's an overwhelming fear for living things. A form that swallows everything."
Rieko: "We're cut off, in a completely closed-off world. After this, we will sublimate everything into one ideal, a true grudge with no impurities."
Hiyoshi: "It will become this world's new truth. Do you understand?"
Rieko: "Either way, it means you will all be returned to nothingness. More than death, more than erasure, it will be a complete and pure eternity... Heehee... I'm sure you can hardly wait."
*I can't sense individual will in their words. If they were humans, they wouldn't adhere to something so ridiculous.*
*It's the outer limits of survival instinct.*
*I can't understand the full story behind their objective. It's specifically because I don't understand the true nature of it that I find it terrifying.*
*As if embodying the fear I feel, the shadow that I can see as nothing other than a whale finally shows its form clearly.*
Mayu: "We have to run...!"
*When my thoughts finally return to the present, it's almost completely like the whales I've seen in picture reference books and images.*
Sayo: "I will... help too..."
*When Sayo takes her hands off the floor, the bookshelf aims for her and flies her way. It's not a direct hit, but even that light impact with her shoulder sends Sayo's body flying into the wall.*
Sayo: "Ow!"
Mayu: "Sayo!"
*Sayo applies pressure to her shoulder and leans her back against the tank wall. Bathed in the red light, it creates a deep shadow.*
*When Sayo is thrown back, something falls down with a flash of light. It slides across the floor and stops at Kenji-san's feet.*
*It's the keychain I lent to Sayo.*
*The strange sticker photo stuck on the back is facing up.*
Hiyoshi: "This... i... s... "
Sayo: "Papa's k--"
Mayu: "We don't have time to grab it! This way!"
*Holding on to the desk, I reach out my hand for Sayo.*
*The desk is shaking so hard I have no idea when it will fall. Moreover, there's no time, as the shaking grows more and more violent.*
Hiyoshi: "Sa... yo..."
*... In an instant, the shaking stops.*
Mayu: "Huh...?"
*Shocked at the sudden turn of events, I return my gaze to Kenji-san.*
Mayu: "Hiyoshi-san is... blurry..."
Mari: "Big sister! It's open!"
*The light in the room returns to a blue color, and the shadow of the large creature disappears.*
*As I listen to Mari's voice, I open my eyes wide.*
*As if it's being projected onto him, there is the form of an unfamiliar man layered over Hiyoshi-san's body.*
*It's the man shown in the sticker photo with Sayo.*
*Kenji-san-- No, that man, has dropped his gaze to the keychain and is trembling.*
Sayo: "What's that?"
Mari: "Sayo-chan too!"
*Despite Mari calling out to her, Sayo gives no response, so I grab her arm.*
*There's no mistake about it, this is our chance to run.*
Rieko: "This little--!"
*Saginuma Rieko stomps on the keychain that has fallen to the floor.*
Mayu: "Ah..."
Rieko: "This thing... This little thing!"
*When it doesn't break on the first try, she stomps on it over and over again with her hard high heel. Cracks finally appear in it, and she smashes it to pieces.*
*At almost the same time, the three of us go through the door in the floor.*
Rieko: "Tch... Only one step further and...!"
*By the time we flee the director's office, Kenji-san has turned his sharp eyes on us.*
Sayo: "That was, without a doubt, my papa."
*Even after reaching the Deep-sea Fish booth, we can't calm down.*
*Sayo says, wiping away the sweat that has gradually appeared. Her breathing is still rough.*
Sayo: "I have a general idea of what happened just now. The thing connecting that man to the aquarium is his possession by my father."
Mayu: "The moment he saw the sticker picture on the keychain was the first time he came to his senses and seemed to recognize you, Sayo... I wonder why he hasn't recognized you this whole time."
Sayo: "You saw, didn't you? Papa's in his own world. It's no surprise that he saw me having grown as I have and didn't recognize me."
Mayu: "But you're family...? Mari was able to recognize me, after all...!"
Sayo: "It sickens me to use their words, but it's thanks to your bond. Having her individuality, Mari understands the concept of time. Hiyoshi-- Papa... is an existence bound to the will of Manten Aquarium. He no longer has any connection to time."
Mayu: "That's-- But if you talk to him, really talk to him, he might understand. You said you wanted to see your father, after all!"
Sayo: "It's fine. I came here to part ways with my father anyway. More than anything, I'm sure Papa has, whether directly or indirectly, taken many lives. Could you forgive him, Mayumi?"
Mayu: "When you say it like that..."
*With me being at a loss for words, Sayo cuts in and continues.*
Sayo: "I'll erase the Red Manten Aquarium. My resolve to do so has not wavered."
Mayu: "Even so... your father..."
Sayo: "Stop already!"
*The screaming voice resonates as if its owner's throat is being burned.*
Sayo: "I was just at the point where I wasn't thinking about it!"
Mayu: "So you are still worried about it then."
Sayo: "No! No! No! That my father would do something so terrible, I can't... won't accept it! But--!"
*Sayo's cries erupt over and over again like a volcano.*
Sayo: "No matter how you look at it, it's clear. That's not a gathering of wills. That's Papa's true self. That giant whale is a mass of malice, and I'm descended from that malice!"
Mayu: "But Sayo, you're a different person from your father. If you're upset by it... you don't need to be."
Sayo: "That's not all!"
*Sayo turns her back to me and places a hand on the Deep-sea Fish booth tank.*
*The surface of the tank reflects Sayo's expression like a mirror. A droplet glistening on her cheek gives her away. It makes a heartrending stain on the floor.*
Sayo: "I can't bring myself to stand against my papa. I still feel love for him. Evil lives within my heart..."
Mari: "Sayo-chan, no!"
Mayu: "The light in the room...!"
*The once all blue room begins to flicker with flashes of red.*
*It's an omen of an impending phenomena. Like lightbulbs flickering on and off, the lighting alternates between blue and red.*
*All of the light shining through from the tank mixes together, becoming nothing more than a gaudy violet.*
Mayu: "Mari!"
*My instinct tells me this is bad, and I embrace Mari.*
*I have to get Sayo and us out of this room.*
*That's what I think, but it's too late.*
*From a spot on the floor -- the spot where Sayo's tears overflow and gradually spread as a highly viscous, violet liquid.*
*It's muddy like melted concrete.*
Mari: "Sayo-chan's crying."
*Before we realize it, we've ended up inside of the vivid, violet space.*
Mayu: "Imprisoned...?"
*This isn't malice -- it's anguish. This is clearly what Sayo is feeling right now. She's truly stuck between a rock and a hard place.*
*It's not the red of hatred or malice, but a violet world.*
*The mud spreads all across the floor, and we sit ourselves on the handrail, narrowly avoiding it. Still, if we wait around like this, the whole room will eventually be covered in mud.*
Mayu: "Sayo! Listen to us!"
Sayo: "I am...! I am...!"
Mari: "I don't think she can hear us..."
*Our voices seem to be swallowed up by the mud.*
*In contrast, Sayo's sobs echo throughout the room. Her anguished voice seems to overflow from the room itself.*
Mayu: "That's..."
*The mud stretching out below me is like the bottom of the ocean.*
*The proof of that is that fish silhouettes begin to appear in the Deep-sea Fish booth.*
*Within the violet light, the fish swim about.*
*They're all fish of the deep sea, from footballfish to oarfish, slightly sparkling pancake octopi and transparent-headed barreleye. The fish that once inhabited this booth.*
*Giant isopods with large carapaces squirm about in the mud, while atrocious brown jellyfish and vivid flower hat jellies float about the ceiling like UFOs.*
*They all originally inhabited parts of the ocean with varying depths, but now they swim around in Sayo's ocean of anguish. The reason these creatures from varying depths are all able to inhabit the same tank is thanks to the nanobubble technology.*
*Each of the creatures faces the sky, their feelers outstretched and some of them with fangs bared. There are also luminescent fish. I can clearly grasp that they're threatened.*
Mari: "I feel bad for them... They're also suffering!"
Mayu: "Mari, you understand, don't you...? Sayo is definitely feeling the same!"
*So as not to start a panic, I am trying with all my might to calm down.*
*But when this phenomena started, I knew how things would change.*
*We're being pulled into the deep sea. When that's done, we'll become their prey. Even if we don't, we'll be unable to breathe, or be crushed by the pressure. It's not just being in the water, but the darkness of the deep sea.*
*The mud swept up by the waves spreads all throughout the room.*
*There's nowhere to run. Moreover, stopping here will lead to us being swallowed up by the mud.*
*No, the threat of being swallowed up by the mud is almost completely gone.*
*The reason why is because our bodies are already floating. I cling tight to Mari, and I can feel her almost nonexistent body heat.*
Mayu: "We're in--"
*The ocean...*
*Our words are snatched away in mid-air. We search for Sayo amidst the confusion, but as even the light itself is going dim, we cannot find her.*
*It really is becoming the deep sea...!*
*Her mouth closed, Mari clings to me. They said Mari has "individuality," so she's being pulled in along with me, but she shouldn't be an existence that can be pulled in in the first place.*
*Not good.*
*There's nothing we can do.*
*If I were alone, I could probably escape, but there's no point in that. I will escape from this Red Manten Aquarium with Mari and Sayo.*
*Even so...*
Mayu: "--!"
*The fish swimming about push my body aside, causing me to slowly hit the wall.*
*This is like the weightlessness I've seen depicted on television and in movies. When I hit the wall once, I lose any sort of momentum, but my own body slowly begins floating toward the opposite wall.*
*I'm lucky that any velocity I had is lost to the water resistance, but at the same time, it's getting harder to breathe.*
Mari: "!"
*I have to do something to change my direction so I can protect Mari. After hitting my back against the ceiling, I should be heading downward.*
Mayu: "!"
*As if it had been waiting on me, a feeler appears before my eyes.*
*The clump-like creature that owns the feeler is stuck to the corner of the wall.*
*The dahlia anemone -- a deep-sea anemone that gets its name from the fact that it looks like the dahlia flower. I've heard it has monumentally powerful venom for a sea creature.*
*On just the strength of its venom alone, it's a ferocious creature whose sting could cause instant death.*
*Not good.*
*At this rate, I'll be preyed upon by that massive feeler.*
*But with my pace so slow, the sea anemone draws closer.*
Mayu: "Mf~~~~~!!"
*It's unavoidable. I'm unable to gain control of my body, and avoiding a direct hit seems unlikely.*
*Then... I have no choice but to gamble.*
*The sea anemone's mouth should be located at its center. It shouldn't be able to pierce the sole of my shoe.*
*I have no choice but to use it as something of a stool to escape in the other direction.*
*I frantically muster my strength and stretch my leg out toward the center of its body.*
*It feels as if I had jumped into a bucket of pudding, but in spite of that, I continue to extend my foot with all my might.*
Mayu: "... Hahh!"
*My foot has sunk in and I can't get it out.*
*Along with it, my body is being swallowed by the anemone. What I thought was its body is only feelers.*
*Somehow, I have to do something!!*
Mayu: "........."
*Despair piled on despair.*
*A large shadow descends from overhead.*
*The shadow opens a mouth so grandiose, it seems as if it could swallow the room whole.*
*It's a whale. Although this one isn't bigger than the one that appeared in the director's office, the difference between them is no more than the difference between the woods and the forest.*
*It's trying to devour me. No, not just me individually, but surely this space itself. Or perhaps it could be trying to achieve the same objective as the director and the others.*
*At this rate, Mari herself will be swallowed up. Even though this place is already underwater, if I push her away, she could be saved.*
*Mari looks at me with an anxious expression.*
*If at least Mari can get away...*
*With that one thought, I push Mari away.*
Mari: "Big siste--"
*Even against the water resistance, Mari is swept further and further away.*
*At the very least, she is able to get out of range of the whale's mouth.*
*My relief is almost simultaneous.*
Mayu: "Ah--"
*What is that breed called? A lancefish, isn't it?*
*A fish with a long, thin body and baring sharp teeth. It has a ferocious desire for every type of prey in the deep sea.*
*That fish is approaching Mari's neck. Even with the bit of distance between them, I can still clearly see that is the situation.*
*The lancefish closes in on Mari and slams into her, biting at her neck. When its fangs sink in, red particles spurt out instead of blood.*
*Showered in the particles that spurt out, it takes another bite further in. Just like that, her neck is swallowed up.*
*Her body is severed from above the neck, the deep red particles scattering about as her head, which has now become all of her, drifts through the deep sea.*
*I lock eyes with the head floating before me. I can't look away from her expressionless eyes.*
*It's the end of the one I tried to protect, the extinguishment of her very existence. Death piled on death. Before I know it, the once violet room is dyed red.*
*The world itself seems to be trying to change.*
*But...*
*From my head to my toes, I am already swallowed up by the whale.*
*Unable to see fate through to the end, I am washed away by the torrent of a violent death.*
*Trying to protect Mari from the mouth that is after her, I reflexively hold her closer.*
*Even if it means being her shield, I will protect her. It's not as if I can ever let her go.*
*We finally got to see each other again. Finally, I can protect her.*
*Just like that, I'm swallowed by the darkness...*
*When I come to, I'm standing where the waves are breaking.*
Mayu: "This is...?"
Sayo: "I don't know. When I came to, I was here."
*It's quiet, but a gentle wind blows. Underneath the starry sky, we're in a fantastical world.*
Sayo: "You saw it, didn't you? That violet was my true nature. It's just like Papa, the form of a darkness that seems as if it will swallow everything."
Mayu: "Right, I was swallowed up by that whale... with Mari. Where is she?"
Sayo: "She's fine."
*Sayo smiles. It's the first time I've seen her do it.*
Sayo: "That isn't the only poison I possess. Even though I should have told you, Mayumi, I couldn't."
Mayu: "Huh...? I really haven't grasped what was going on from the start... Am I dead...? Is this the Sanzu River? But it's not a river..."
*Ignoring me in my confusion, Sayo continues.*
Sayo: "I met Mari five years ago at Manten Aquarium, when she was alone. We were close in age, so I have a feeling that's why she quickly opened up to me."
Sayo: "I was so proud, I wanted to show her the Deep-sea Fish booth. Even though it was already closed down, there were creatures left as part of Papa's collection, but in the end it was locked and we couldn't get in."
Sayo: "As thanks for telling her about it, I received her keychain. I wondered if it was okay even though we couldn't see the booth, but I was happy. Ironically, I was able to ascertain that reason today."
Sayo: "On that day, Mari hid somewhere after the aquarium closed. She waited until a time when all of the staff should have gone home. Because I was so proud of it, she probably wanted to see the Deep-sea Fish booth."
Sayo: "Moreover, that night, the door to the Deep-sea Fish booth was open."
Sayo: "You already know. At that time, my Papa was killed by the hands of those two. From there, it went as Mari said."
Sayo: "I could never tell you my own sin. I'm the worst. Really awful. I clearly understand that I inherited my boastful nature from my father."
Sayo: "My ego, my poison, killed Mari."
Sayo: "I should die, just like Papa did. Mayu, you and Mari should erase this Red Manten Aquarium. I'm counting on you."
*As usual, Sayo says this flatly.*
*I can feel a sense of unease in Sayo's story. Somewhere there's a warped sense of distance.*
Mayu: "Don't say it like that. Tell me how you really feel."
Sayo: "I just told you."
Mayu: "Then I'll say it, Sayo. I wanted to be your friend, so I kept quiet."
Sayo: "Huh?! You're mad, right?!"
Mayu: "When you told Mari the truth, did she get mad? Did she resent you? Mari was swept up into a tragic fate, yet she still said she wanted to see me. She said she was happy she did get to see me. Her feelings are still pure and clean."
Mayu: "That she died because of you -- Mari doesn't think anything of the sort. That's why I don't blame you either. I don't think you should die. And on that note, Mari is thinking that she wants to save you, Sayo. I'm her big sister, so I know."
Mayu: "Moreover, please don't talk about yourself dying anymore. Himeno entrusted this to me. I made the decision to abandon you once... which is why I won't falter anymore."
*The starry sky twinkles, bathing us in faint light.*
Mayu: "That's right, Mari. You're here, right?"
Mari: "Yup."
*When I look over my shoulder, there's a small silhouette.*
Mayu: "And... you're glowing?!"
*Mari is tinged with a transparent blue light.*
*Her hair color is strange, almost sublime.*
Mari: "Ehehe. Isn't it great?"
Mayu: "I don't know if I'd say great... or cool?"
Mari: "Right? Eh!"
*Mari's whole face is in a smile, and she spins around once. With almost the same ease that her skirt flutters, the light enveloping Mari spreads throughout the whole room.*
*The room illuminated in blue light gradually returns to its original form as the Deep-sea Fish Booth. No, it's not the same.*
*There's not one trace of the aggression that had swelled so high in the deep-sea creatures up to a short time ago, and they swim about within the light.*
*They resemble the fish who swam about without regard to the containment of the tunnel tank, but they are infinitely more free.*
*It's the exact opposite of negative feelings such as hate or malice.*
Sayo: "Pancake octopi, footballfish, oarfish, brown jellies and flower hat jellyfish, barreleye and chiroteuthis imperator, and there's a dahlia anemone, as well as inshore hagfish."
*Sayo is following the fish with her eyes. Those eyes bear the purity that lives in Mari. Along the same lines, I am also enjoying being surrounded by so many different creatures like this. They can be forgiven for their previous attacks.*
*The creatures begin to gradually swim around Mari.*
Mayu: "Mari... what did you do?"
Mari: "I became one with the fish. I prayed and made a wish -- that I wanna protect my big sister."
Mayu: "Just like the director and the others-- No, it's different. You don't have that sinister feeling."
*The only ones who do are those who make corrupt wishes.*
Sayo: "With this, we may be able to hold off that whale. On the other hand, we may also be able to destroy the Red Manten Aquarium."
Mayu: "Malice, hatred, anger... The imprisoned souls have been freed from these things. Look and you'll understand."
*It's different from one unified form following one giant will. All of them being touched by Mari's light, their liberated souls are each cooperating. You could say it's numerous individuals responding as one community.*
*We've received hope from an unexpected source.*
Mayu: "Mari, you're really amazing!"
*I become happy and embrace Mari.*
*But it doesn't work the way it did before. Even as I pitch forward, Mari's body gives no tactile resistance, and I fall to the floor.*
*I reach out my hand for Mari's to try and stand up, but our fingers cannot touch.*
*My eyes surely widen, as Mari gazes as me with an intense look.*
Mari: "Big sister, I understand as well."
*I exhale without thinking.*
*Her serious expression is one I've never seen before. I realize she's grown up within eternity itself.*
Mari: "If you take care of the bad people, big sister, I'll disappear too. Because this aquarium itself will cease to exist."
Mayu: "...... Yeah."
Mari: "If everyone gets eaten up by the big whale, it might not disappear, but I don't want to stay in such a scary place forever!"
*What Saginuma Rieko and her cohort called a whale. That giant, malevolent aggregation is the means of closing off this world.*
*I, personally, would not want to spend an eternity in such a place.*
*More importantly than anything else, I refuse to let Mari be trapped. My precious little sister... She can't be saved if she can't go to heaven.*
Mari: "I won't give up! Leave it to me, and you all go back to the real world where you can rest easy, big sister!"
Mayu: "What are you saying, Mari? We'll stand up to them... together."
*I put my hand out in front of Mari.*
Mari: "Huh, but big sister, you guys can't do anything, can you?"
Mayu: "We can be with you. Even if it's just for a little longer."
*With the back of my hand facing up I stretch my arm out toward Mari, and finally it seems she realizes what I'm trying to do.*
*Mari also extends her hand, laying it with, naturally, no feeling to it over mine. Even with her hand giving off no body heat, I am sure I can feel a warmth to it.*
Sayo: "Are you trying to leave me out?"
Mari: "You want in, Sayo-chan?"
Sayo: "If you say so as well, Mari, but I'm fine. I haven't atoned just yet. If I don't at least say what I want to, I can't go home."
Mayu: "That seems... so like you, Sayo."
Sayo: "It's not bad, is it?"
*Saying this, Sayo places her palm on top of Mari's hand. Oddly enough, it's as if I can feel her skin.*
*If this sensation is what we call individuality, then what they said is wrong.*
*It's precisely because each individual can come together that we are strong. It's incomparable to just existing on survival instinct alone, having hatred and malice as one's driving force. One can't consume everything. Humans weren't born just to survive.*
Sayo: "We'll definitely do it."
Mari: "I'll protect the two of you. So you definitely need to make it home!"
Mayu: "Let's not have any regrets anymore. Mari, if you're protecting me, then I will support you... and definitely return to Himeno's side."
*I take a small breath and look up at the ceiling.*
Mayu: "Let's head for the other side of the truth."
Mari: "Here is... all right as well."
*Taking the front entrance out of the Deep-sea Fish booth, we've arrived at the tunnel tank.*
*Mari is praying at the center of the room, addressing the fish and living creatures. The creatures do not respond verbally, but the blue light emitting from Mari envelops them and just like at the Deep-sea Fish booth, they begin to swim about freely.*
Sayo: "I wonder why the fish don't disappear. Since they're souls, they should also be able to cross over."
Mayu: "Because they're all Mari's companions. They've been under the influence of a heart of fear up to now, but having been moved by Mari's pure heart, it seems like they want to help her."
Sayo: "The fishes' emotions, huh? Think you could hand in a paper on that?"
Mayu: "Should I write that Mari is glowing... or something? If I write such a paper, they won't let me into high school."
Mari: "Big sister, you'll be a high school student soon! How cool~ How cool~!"
*Mari is emphasizing her point by jumping up and down.*
*The ultramarine light that seems to be not of this world is making an imprint out of Mari. As Mari says this, I can't really seem to answer her.*
Mari: "Big sister, are you in the same class as Sayo-chan?"
Mayu: "No. We go to different schools. I go to Mitsuba Junior High, close to home. What about you, Sayo?"
Sayo: "A girls' school in the city. It includes elementary, junior, and high school. It's near Manten. Well, since the high school's begun recruiting, why not try for it? Though the score you need is high."
Mari: "Sayo-chan, you want to be in the same class as my big sister!"
Sayo: "Pfft, that isn't necessarily-- It's not what I mea-- Stop picking on me!"
*Sayo's face becomes red and she shouts at Mari. Mari has a mischievous smile on her face, she seems to be attached to Sayo.*
*I'm not used to seeing the power dynamics between these two play out so clearly, but it's quite funny.*
Mayu: "Anyway, let's keep moving. We need to go around the aquarium to all the areas and make all the creatures Mari's allies... right?"
Mari: "The fishes say that they'll help us and spread my light, so we can leave it to them. We should go look for that man and the lady!"
Sayo: "Allies, eh? Here, they're probably our most reliable option."
Mari: "But you know, if we don't hurry and find the man, no matter how I helped them, they'll be taken over by the scary feelings again."
Mayu: "Overwritten by the grudge... you mean? If so... we should return to the director's office."
Mari: "Yeah, that's right. I'll go in the front! Because the others are following me for now. Big sister, you two... think about what you want to say to the man and the lady."
*Mari says, pointing her fingers towards the way we came.*
*When Mari spins to the side, the dust in the air reflects the glittering light. It's as if she's clad in blue specks of light.*
*Mari's words weigh heavily on my chest. She has also probably realized it.*
Mari: "This is the end. The man and the lady, and all of the imprisoned fish souls, even the big whale, I'll bring them all along. To the other side of that fuzzy world!"
*I want to stay a human.*
*This is what Mari's heart is screaming out.*
Mari: "But I'm not sad. If I ke~ep waiting the whole time, I'm sure I'll see you."
Mayu: "At that time, I'll be an old lady. Will you recognize me?"
Mari: "Big sister as an old lady... Hehe, how funny!"
Sayo: "If their ambitions come to pass, Mari will never be able to go to heaven, forever trapped within the cage of being a grudge. It sounds like a ridiculous story."
Mayu: "Sayo... if you still have doubts, maybe you shouldn't push yourself?"
*Sayo clicks her tongue, then continues.*
Sayo: "I don't want to accept it, I still have doubts. I also feel that I don't want Papa to disappear, but to be imprisoned by a shapeless grudge, unable to be rescued... I pity him. He's already dead, so at least let him be relieved by Mari's kindness."
Sayo: "My true resolve is that no matter how terrible he was, I still accept him as his daughter."
Sayo: "It's not wrong is it? Going about it that way."
*Sayo smiles with the most relaxed expression I've ever seen her use.*
*It's not the strained resolve she had when we met. The person who recklessly said she would throw away her life if it meant clearing up the truth is a different person.*
*Like the time that Mari said she wanted to protect us, this is a pure wish.*
Sayo: "Mayumi, you should also probably be sure of your feelings. Even if this Hiyoshi guy is the real culprit who killed Mari, there's no need for him to die. If he can be saved, you should save him. If he's alive, he should have to atone for his sins."
Mayu: "Sayo..."
Mari: "I don't hate him or anything. He was scary, but I've already forgotten about him. My only wish is... to protect you two!"
Sayo: "Parting isn't all there is. You two taught me that. That's why, no matter what conclusion you reach, Mayumi, I've decided not to deny it."
Mayu: "I still have things I wanna know."
*Sayo turns to me with a serious look.*
*I look Sayo directly in the eyes and continue.*
Mayu: "If I think about it, we still haven't heard the truth. We know why Saginuma Rieko planned to kill the director, but then why is Kenji-san her accomplice... and what caused him to go mad?"
*Since she saw the scene of the murder, they probably killed Mari to shut her up. Saginuma Rieko was probably killed as well, being afraid to give herself up and hesitant to kill Mari.*
*It's nothing more than a hunch, but I have the feeling it's right. Back when Kenji-san attacked me, he tried to trick me by pretending to attack Saginuma Rieko instead. His threatening attitude wasn't normal.*
*Even though I can't think of it as something a human would do, he did it.*
Mayu: "One more thing. Saginuma Rieko."
*Sayo looks at me with a mysterious expression.*
Sayo: "Do you have some connection with her, Mayumi?"
Mayu: "Why did she keep her -- Mari -- around at that time?"
*After a moment of silence passes, I clap both of my hands together.*
Mayu: "Without knowing the truth, I can't reach a conclusion. So we should get going. I'm sure the night of the full moon will be over soon. For Mari, and for everyone in the aquarium... we cannot fail, no matter what!"
Mari: "Right!"
*With Mari's cheerful reply, she stands in front of us and begins walking, just as she had said.*
*As I start to follow behind her, her form cloaked in light, Sayo pulls me back by the hem of my shirt. It's unexpected... and a little surprising.*
Sayo: "Listen to me for a moment."
Mayu: "Huh? What is it?"
Sayo: "Once we return to the real world, I probably will be too embarrassed to say this, so I'll say it now."
*Before I can respond, Sayo continues. Her cheeks have been tinged a slightly red color.*
Sayo: "Our conversation earlier. About high school. Since it's still about six months away, we have some time. Just give it some serious consideration."
Mayu: "You mean about going to the same high school... Hey, don't just say that and walk ahead."
*I follow behind the two of them at a jog.*
*My time with Mari has been fun, but it's surely against the providence of this world. The amount of absurd and frightening things we have encountered is probably keeping the balance.*
*Soon, this time of terror will end.*
*And so will my time with Mari.*
*I want to keep being with her, forever, but that wish won't be granted. If it won't be granted, at least let me engrave these feelings onto my heart.*
*I will protect Mari. Along with my steadfast vow.*
Mari: "Big sister, you're too slow!"
Mayu: "That's not true, I'm with you."
*We exit the tunnel tank and head on from the Fish of the World booth.*
Mari: "It's dark..."
*On opening the door to the director's office, the first thing that flies out at us is darkness.*
*It's pitch black, dark enough to swallow up even the blue light flowing out from Mari.*
Mari: "Big sister, be careful."
Mayu: "Right...!"
*It's not just that it's dark, but there's some sort of ominousness to it.*
*Something is actively swallowing the light. The light flowing in from the door should illuminate the whole room, but it's safe to say that it isn't happening at all.*
*I have no idea what has happened to the room, which should have been in complete disarray from the poltergeist phenomena.*
Sayo: "They're not here?"
Mari: "Nope, they are. I can feel them."
Rieko: "What a useless thing you've done."
Mayu: "That voice... Saginuma Rieko! I knew it!"
*Saginuma Rieko's voice came almost in sync with the answer to Sayo's question.*
*But from where...?*
*Even as I look around, I don't see them anywhere.*
Rieko: "Kenji-kun and I, heehee... I'm sure you all haven't the capacity to understand."
*Even if we can't see her, we can clearly hear her voice. It sounds as if it's generally coming from the director's office, but I have no idea where she is.*
Rieko: "I'll tell you. The thing I realized when we met here that I've wanted you to know. Because he... loved you."
Mayu: "Don't say nonsensical things. Please... show yourself!"
Rieko: "You may not have realized it yourself, and you might have disappeared without knowing it, but within this aquarium, the yearning you felt when you were saved by him... I won't let you say that wasn't from a heart filled with love."
Sayo: "Mayumi, don't engage her."
Mayu: "I know, but..."
*If we let her go at this point, I get the feeling we won't learn about Saginuma Rieko and Kenji-san.*
*Not just that... I don't think we can completely ignore Saginuma Rieko.*
Rieko: "To speak of our relationship... perhaps I should start with Kenji-kun."
Rieko: "Hiyoshi Kenji. He's a criminal who killed his mother when he was little."
*Without realizing it, I've forgotten to breathe.*
*At this rate, even Mari and Sayo can't help but be interested in Saginuma Rieko's story.*
Mayu: "Killed her? You mean he... murdered her?"
*I realize that my voice has completely changed.*
Rieko: "He had a sister four years younger than he. His innocent little sister accidentally swallowed a toy. He tried to make her cough it up, but it became hard for her to breathe. She was likely writhing in pain."
Rieko: "At that point, Kenji-kun didn't save her. For someone with normal feelings, that would be unthinkable. Kenji-kun watched his sister struggle against death and was amused. So she died... like that."
Rieko: "Discovering the scene of the crime, his mother criticized Kenji-kun. Of course she would, since if he had helped his sister sooner, she wouldn't have died."
Rieko: "Lacking that understanding, Kenji-kun wrung his mother's neck. As he did so, he saw his mother writhe in agony like his sister had, just as he expected."
Mayu: "Then she..."
Rieko: "Yes. She was killed by her son's hands, and... he enjoyed himself doing it."
*Watching his sister die... Actually killing his mother...*
Mari: "No... that's scary, big sister..."
*As if it in reaction to Mari's frightened voice, the blue light flickers, but rather than illuminating us, it's swallowed up by the darkness.*
*I take hold of Mari's tiny hand and squeeze it. Even though I can't hold her hand, I get the sense I can feel her shaking.*
Rieko: "How old are you right now?"
Mayu: "I'm fourteen, right now..."
*Having been asked so suddenly, I answer on instinct.*
Rieko: "This story is from when Kenji-kun was around eight or so, so you all probably weren't born yet."
Sayo: "That has nothing to do with this. Just tell us everything from the beginning."
Rieko: "My, don't you want to hear it? There should be no need for you to be so cold, right?"
Sayo: "Don't act so high and mighty."
Rieko: "Is that the attitude you take when you want something? That part of you is very much like your father, isn't it?"
*At her words, Sayo silently casts her eyes downward.*
Rieko: "That's fine, but I'm the one who started telling the story, so I'll let you hear it through to the end."
*Her voice, resonating through the darkness, finally continues.*
Rieko: "He was remanded to an institution, where he received behavior modification. It is no exaggeration to say that these inhumane treatments made Kenji-kun who he is now."
Rieko: "Having become able to lead a respectable life after sealing off his mental deformities, he began attending a high school near Manten."
Rieko: "After some time, he began a part time job... at the souvenir shop here at Manten Aquarium."
Rieko: "The past is something that follows the person who created it. His peaceful days were unable to continue as rumors of 'Heinous Criminal Hiyoshi Kenji' began to spread, and he became the target of unavoidable bullying."
Rieko: "To be sure, he was on the other side of fear, now."
Mayu: "In crimes comitted by minors, the name is not supposed to have been released... How did anyone find out?"
Rieko: "Reality doesn't work like it does in a textbook. Getting back to the topic at hand... You see, I covered for him."
Rieko: "At the very least, at that time, Kenji-kun was serious, an employee overflowing with enthusiasm. He treated this part-time job as a true job. He was more reliable than the kids who saw this as a way to prolong their fun or meet people."
Rieko: "Even so, in the end, Kenji-kun's isolation didn't change. I tried to close the distance between us. Having graduated from graduate school and going straight to work, I probably found some warmth within him."
Sayo: "That's unexpected. To think you also require warmth."
Rieko: "That narrow point of view was draining to me. Just like the fact that I was labeled a workaholic. Heehee... much like Director Sakuragi."
Sayo: "Tch."
*Her voice, mixed with sarcasm, lowers in tone, sounding as if she's increased its viscosity.*
Rieko: "I gave him a mother figure, and he gave me a sense of purpose. As my friction with the director grew, before I knew it, I was being supported by him... Heehee..."
Mayu: "In the end, that resulted in murder."
Rieko: "Oh, you're so impatient. Well then, since it seems you don't care to hear about our softer days, I'll leave that part out. A woman's jealousy is equal to her instinct."
*I ignore the fact that she says all this with not a hint of embarrassment.*
*In reality, it's because I really don't know how to respond to what Saginuma Rieko is saying.*
Rieko: "He was different than I. I was unable to sense that his mental deformities were growing larger. However, the fact that I had no doubts regarding his proposition to kill someone shows how worn down my own mind was."
Sayo: "Hey, that's..."
Rieko: "The second he once again set eyes on a person's death, it was like Pandora's box had opened."
*Without allowing Sayo to continue, Saginuma Rieko says her next thoughts without any room for pause.*
*The genesis of the plan for the director's murder was... Kenji-san...*
Rieko: "Oh, don't make that face. I cannot have you misunderstanding me. At that time, my malevolence and murderous intent were certain and the one who slaughtered Sakuragi Isao was me."
Rieko: "To be sure, even if Kenji-kun hadn't tempted me, I would have reached the same conclusion in the near future anyway."
Rieko: "His deformities grew further and he viewed death with even more excitement."
Mayu: "Then you're saying the reason Mari was killed... was just for his enjoyment! No way! That's inhuman..."
Mari: "Big sister, calm down... She is surely trying to make you angry and upset."
Sayo: "She's intending to stir you up so that your heart may be consumed by darkness. Everything she's saying is in the past. Rest assured, you aren't the only one upset."
Mayu: "But!"
*Speaking in a loud voice, both of their faces come into view.*
*I have to calm down. I'm the one who wanted to know the truth.*
*Saginuma Rieko's loud laugher reverberates. It grates on my nerves, and I narrow my eyes.*
Rieko: "Heehee... I wouldn't do something so underhanded. Moreover, just now you said that Kenji-kun was 'inhuman,' didn't you?"
Rieko: "You mustn't misunderstand. It's exactly because he's 'human' that he feels pleasure at death. I can't empathize, but... I suppose I can understand. Specifically because he's a human, he can feel happiness, anger, sadness, and enjoyment."
Rieko: "From the instant the director died, this place was born. Since that child and I died inside the Red Manten Aquarium, we were likely treated as missing persons. That's why Kenji-kun's sins didn't come to light."
Rieko: "It's impressive how he continued to peacefully live on and melt into society. While carrying the swirling desires of his heart... of course."
Mayu: "That's..."
Rieko: "Kenji-kun used this world of malice, killing the girls he brought here. If he killed them here, no proof remained. In the end, Kenji-kun's heart called out to those who were of this place's grudge... and melded together with them."
Sayo: "What do you mean?"
Rieko: "Just as you suspect. Having a body, Kenji-kun has been entrusted with the role of bringing fresh lives here. His soul is already assimilated into the Red Manten Aquarium. The Hiyoshi Kenji you met originally is merely a leftover will."
Mayu: "Half dead, half living... Then, in other words, Kenji-san is already..."
Mari: "Wait. The lady said 'assimilated' just now."
Rieko: "If that child's power can release souls, then Kenji-kun's soul is the same. Since he has a flesh body, then wouldn't he return to his original body? It's so irregular that there's no precedent for it, but theoretically there should be no mistake about it."
*No matter how unpleasant it may be, I wasn't lying about wanting to hear the truth.*
*But if I had the chance to talk to him, I wanted to hear the truth from his mouth. I don't know how much of Saginuma Rieko's story is true.*
*Kenji-san's past is so over the top, there's no way I can empathize. I can think of it as nothing more than a tale.*
*However, if I've believed him once... then I want to properly understand the situation. I at least want to get a proper farewell.*
Mayu: "Why would you two do something that would put you at a disadvantage...?"
Rieko: "It is a woman's obstinance. I wonder if you can grasp at least that much?"
Mayu: "Don't toy with me!"
*I shout at Saginuma Rieko, who decides to mess with us further in this situation. My voice doesn't have an echo, as if it's swallowed by the darkness.*
Mayu: "At least let me see you. You're alone now, aren't you? If you were with Kenji-san -- the director -- he would try to swallow us up."
Rieko: "I wonder, is that really the case...? Heehee..."
*Her laughter gradually becomes louder. Her ominous voice completely fills the room.*
Sayo: "How annoying."
*It probably wasn't a reaction to Sayo's words, but the voice suddenly stops.*
*At the same time that the voice disappears, a light comes on in the director's office. The blue light flowing from the tank lights up the room.*
*In the center of the director's office, lying on his side on the desk, is Hiyoshi Kenji's corpse.*
Mayu: "Kenji... san......!"
*As if a stake has been thrust through his large chest, there's a red stain where his heart should be.*
*The antique furniture is wet with blood, Kenji-san's body not making the slightest movement. Even without checking his pulse or breathing, instinct tells me that this is a dead body.*
*Being cautious, we do not go near him. Saginuma Rieko is likely hiding somewhere.*
Mayu: "What exactly... is going on here?"
*The three of us crowd together, each looking in a different direction.*
*Even as I ask that in a loud voice, I receive no response.*
Sayo: "He's dead. What is this?"
Mayu: "Where is Saginuma Rieko...?"
Mari: "She's here, big sister. Within that light..."
*Mari is pointing to the blue light illuminating Hiyoshi Kenji's corpse.*
Rieko: "Is comprehension still not coming to you? This man... I killed him."
*From within the light, only the voice reverberates.*
*Before long, the light creates an outline, and the transparent form of Saginuma Rieko faintly appears. Once she is visible, I notice that she seems to have taken on the appearance of the past version of herself.*
*Her chignon-styled hair has come undone, now somewhat long as it was in the video from two years ago.*
Mayu: "Killed him, but... wasn't he your ally? Didn't you... love him?"
Rieko: "That's right. That's why."
*Saginuma Rieko stretches her spine so she stands tall like a model, folding her arms and sliding her right hand along her cheek.*
*Thanks to her thick make-up, she looks older, though I get the sense that with no make-up, she'd be quite the young-looking beauty.*
*Things have settled down enough that I can calmly observe her. Although it's not my trembling heart, but rather the air surrounding the director's office that has. Contrary to the circumstances, it's a gentle atmosphere.*
Rieko: "I didn't consider him an ally. He was a part of me needed to achieve one objective. Equal to one seedling of the grudge."
Rieko: "I told you didn't I, that it's a woman's obstinance? Sincerely, as a human -- as a woman -- I decided he had to die."
Sayo: "Are you saying that you betrayed the aquarium... and Papa?"
Rieko: "Heehee... From the perspective of humans, it would seem that way. At any rate, since if I am a higher order being, I wouldn't be able to revert to a merely logical one. That is your fault, Mari-chan."
Mari: "........."
Mayu: "Mari?"
*When she hears her name called, Mari covers her face. She seems to be turning into something we cannot comprehend.*
*I'm sure that, if I understand Saginuma Rieko's story correctly, that's what we should be lead to believe.*
Rieko: "That's due to you releasing the souls. I am also being affected by that. I remembered that I loved the aquarium, studied, put in effort... all of the memories leading me to this point."
Rieko: "Just before I was going to kill you for witnessing the director's murder, I also remember that I hesitated."
Rieko: "I couldn't run from who I was."
Mayu: "That's why you didn't kill Mari, isn't it? You couldn't kill a girl who loved the aquarium. You really... are a kind person, aren't you?"
Rieko: "Hahaha... What a foolish thing... Kindness, beauty, hatred, anger, they're all pieces of the same whole. Nothing more than pieces of the human-created ego..."
Rieko: "It's probably a strange conversation for one who has already passed, but I chose death for myself. Even if I can't go to some place like heaven, I wanted to die as myself, as Saginuma Rieko."
Sayo: "Why did you kill him? What happened to Papa?"
Rieko: "To make a distinction. That's why I brought Kenji-kun along. In the end, love just was not enough. That's why we both sought understanding."
Rieko: "The director, Sakuragi Isao, had already detached from Kenji-kun's body. If he wasn't going back to the real world, then he didn't need it."
Rieko: "Thanks to that girl's power, the aquarium has already begun to crumble. Even if it's by himself, he likely intends to be sublimated into this place's new reality."
Rieko: "That man is an individual, after all. Though guided by the assembled will of the aquarium, no one can run from the poison he carries, his desire to 'exceed the limits of humanity.'"
Rieko: "Humans cannot exceed their jurisdiction. No... you in particular may be the exception."
*Saginuma Rieko looks at Mari as she says this.*
*I realize that, gradually, Saginuma Rieko's form is becoming pale.*
*The light flowing from the tank turns and blends with the blue light emitting from Mari's body, mixing in the center of the room to create a violet color.*
*Each is bound by the limits of the human heart, and it seems as if there is no way but to combine them. What's stopping this is the looks, flesh, and heart that make an individual.*
Rieko: "I will not yield my soul to you. Do not expect me to be your salvation."
Rieko: "You can all fall into the very pits of despair for all I care. If we do ever meet in confrontation, you can choke on your own poison..."
*Cursing us, those are her last words.*
*Saginuma Rieko caresses Kenji-san's body and gives a loud, wicked laugh.*
*We are not shocked by this. Saginuma Rieko's body, already transparent, gradually breaks up into light blue colored particles, and from her toes to her head, disappears within three seconds.*
Mayu: "If you're going to disappear, at least go in peace."
*Looking at the disappearing Saginuma Rieko, I place my hands together in front of my chest and offer my prayers.*
Sayo: "Mayumi?"
Mayu: "Sorry, Sayo. She is the one who killed your father, but I feel just a little bit... sorry for her. She seemed so sad and lonely."
Sayo: "You don't need to pay me any mind. I told you that I would accept any conclusion you reached, Mayumi. This is one of those conclusions. It's just that... doesn't having such a personality bring you trouble?"
Mayu: "It's all right. I'm not alone."
*I've realized something.*
*I wasn't able to talk to Kenji-san directly about the deaths he caused himself, or how he finally was able to express his love in the death he caused. Or about how he could personally cause death after death with one woman.*
*Even in the moment Saginuma Rieko disappeared, I wasn't able to get the one thing I needed from her either.*
*Either way...*
*As if it's sneering at me, that loud laugh echoes throughout the room.*
Mayu: "Let's go, Mari."
Mari: "Is that man okay?"
Mayu: "Yeah. I can't do anything for him."
*Borrowing her words, the reason is because of my own obstinance.*
*Taking Mari's hand, which I am unable to feel, we leave the director's office.*
Mayu: "What is this...? The aquarium, it's being worn away...!"
*As soon as we leave the director's office, we are shocked at the scene before our eyes.*
*Spots here and there on the walls and floors are being dyed in black. It looks as though it has been eaten away by insects.*
*The spots with holes don't allow any light to pass through, causing the darkness to spread. Little particles of light blue are floating up from the outlines.*
*We return to the Fish of the World booth through the staff passageway.*
*The path on the way there is in a similar worm-eaten state. On the other hand, the aquarium itself seems to be becoming light.*
*If Saginuma Rieko's disappearance can be considered her crossing over, then the scene suggests that this space itself is also crossing over.*
*One by one, the particles themselves seem to be proof of the life that was here. The humans and fish, there's no mistaking that they are becoming the light.*
Mari: "You don't need to worry, big sister. Since I freed everyone, this place is coming to an end."
Sayo: "It's losing the life energy -- the strength from the souls used to maintain itself. Mari, about how long do you think is left?"
Mari: "I don't know, but probably less than an hour."
Mayu: "An hour..."
*In other words, that's how much time I have left with Mari.*
Mayu: "What happens if we're too late?"
Mari: "You'll become fuzzy like the rest of us. You guys aren't supposed to get like that yet!"
Mayu: "I know that. All we've done up to this point would be for nothing."
Sayo: "If we have an hour left, I want to say something to my papa. Mari, do you know where Papa is?"
Mari: "Yeah."
*Mari points in the direction of the exit.*
Mari: "That man is towards the entrance. I'm sure he's waiting there intentionally."
Mayu: "The director is probably... waiting for you, Sayo."
Sayo: "Then it works out for us. Sorry, but will you come with me?"
Mayu: "Of course!"
*Our voices come out in unison, and we begin walking.*
Director: "I was waiting for you."
Mayu: "Director..."
Sayo: "Papa!"
*Naturally, it's not Hiyoshi Kenji that's there.*
*This time, it's the director, the body of Sakuragi Isao himself, standing at the Tokyo Bay booth near the entrance.*
*His body isn't transparent like Saginuma Rieko's was just before she disappeared. Thinking back on it, since he had a proper existence here, it would probably be more notable if he were transparent. Cases like Mari's should be the exception.*
*However, his objective is supposedly to assimilate with the aquarium. When he does, I am sure he will cast off the appearance of his spirit.*
*Sayo takes a step ahead of us and begins speaking to him.*
Sayo: "Finally, I can see my real papa."
*The Tokyo Bay booth is no exception, as it is in the process of having holes open here and there as it turns into blue light.*
*Even as I look around, I don't see the giant whale that appeared in the director's office anywhere. If that was created by the souls or the grudge, then it may no longer be able to come together.*
*The higher order beings swallowed up by that whale, they were said to be turned into mere grudges, but if the whale can no longer take its form, he may still try to do something.*
Sayo: "This whole time, I've been chasing the truth, but no matter how much I pursued it, I couldn't seem to meet up with you, Papa. I finally found you, but I'm sure this is the last time, so I want to tell you how I feel."
*I decide to just watch.*
*I needed to reach my own conclusion, so I think Sayo needs to do the same. At this moment, Sayo is trying to make a distinction. I cannot hinder her.*
Director: "Sayo, won't you come with me?"
Sayo: "Huh? Papa, what--"
*Before Sayo can finish speaking, the director takes over the conversation.*
Director: "We are humans in the end. Nothing remains after we die. No matter how intensely we display our emotions, in the end, they are meaningless to this world."
Director: "Once we cease being individuals and give ourselves to the collective will of this place's grudge, we can become something new. We would remain as one part of this world's reality. It is the purest form of significance, and in it, we become eternal."
Director: "Now that Saginuma Rieko has disappeared, my own directive is all that remains."
Director: "If I don't create a new concept, then under the circumstances, I'll be swallowed up by the current reality of this place, but if you lend me your strength, Sayo, I can definitely achieve my goal."
Sayo: "Papa, you've changed after all. I really don't get you."
Director: "Sayo, you're so clever, so you would understand right away."
Sayo: "I refuse. I have no interest in doing so."
Director: "That's a shame. Being rejected by my daughter."
*The director's words are nothing more than words.*
*He doesn't seem at all remorseful. His words have no weight, and his eyes lack emotion.*
Mayu: "Kyah."
*As if the room itself is expressing its emotion, its destruction progresses. The area at my feet begins to disappear, as it is swallowed up by something black.*
*That black is-- Surely, it's nothingness.*
*Since it didn't really exist to begin with, the destroyed parts of the Red Manten Aquarium are returning to nothingness.*
Mari: "If we don't avoid it, we'll be in danger."
Mayu: "Yeah, it's okay. More importantly..."
*Sayo is a step ahead of us, facing the director. Even the increasing destruction around her hasn't shaken her.*
Sayo: "How long has this been going on? You being swept away by your delusions. Wanting to become a mere concept is something so stupid, even an elementary school student wouldn't wish for it."
Director: "Sayo, this isn't an individual aspiration. Please understand. This is what you might call the cooperative desire of living beings. I no longer bear my will alone. The way I am now, I am like a CPU. To move this large machine -- that is precisely my directive."
Director: "The moment this world was born along the lines of my grudge, I have aspired to a higher order existence. The numerous souls were accumulated here for that reason."
Sayo: "That's enough. When it was just about your grudge, I could empathize with you. If it all had ended with your resentment and your hatred like a normal human, it would have been fine. Why would you create the world of this Red Manten Aquarium?"
Director: "Even if I didn't tell you, you could understand by becoming one with me."
Sayo: "Papa, I can't understand your way of thinking."
Director: "Sayo... I feel so sad. Aren't you my daughter? You should already understand that time is short. Come with me... Come with your father."
*The director extends his right hand toward Sayo. He places his hand on her head and rubs her head with soft movements.*
*When compared with Sayo's seemingly small form, the director's hand looks large. At this point in particular, he definitely seems like a father.*
*After staying silent and having her head rubbed for awhile, Sayo looks down and bites her lip.*
*After about ten seconds of saying nothing, Sayo finally clicks her tongue as if she's become impatient.*
Sayo: "That's enough!"
*In sync with her shout, she brushes the director's hand away.*
*Before we know it, as if reacting to the intense expression of emotion, the erosion reaches Sayo's feet.*
Mayu: "Sayo... watch out."
*Sayo shows no reaction to my words and doesn't move out of the way. With that same forcefulness, she continues.*
Sayo: "I came here to say farewell to you, Papa. Trying to do something now will not change my mind."
Sayo: "However, I have one final wish. Will you hear it?"
*Sayo tilts her head and raises the pitch at the end of her sentence. Precisely because this is between a parent and a child, I feel like I can get a clear sense of the distance between them.*
Sayo: "Stop this nonsense. Be a good sport and cross over. It shouldn't be hard to give yourself over to the freedom Mari is offering."
*It's a hope the size of the eye of a needle. Even so, Sayo is gambling on the feelings her father carries.*
Sayo: "Yeah, if it's no good, then it's fine. Even so, I am your daughter. I've decided to maintain my sense of pride in being your daughter."
*Without leaving any time to hear his response, Sayo brusquely finishes speaking.*
*She spins her body back in our direction and signals us with a bitter smile. I feel like I don't want to see that expression on Sayo's face.*
*To see her trying to deceive us by hiding her painful feelings behind a smile...*
Mayu: "Huh..."
Mari: "Sayo-chan!"
*Suddenly, Mari and I both raise our voices in astonishment.*
*But, unmistakably, the most surprised is Sayo herself.*
*The director has grabbed on to Sayo from behind.*
Sayo: "Papa, why?"
Director: "..."
*The director says nothing. His eyes closed, he seems to be holding on to his one and only daughter for warmth.*
*It feels like a long time. He may be trying to take up the remainder of our time here.*
*Even so, it seems the love he finds there opens his eyes. Within this crumbling world, the two of them together stand out.*
Sayo: "Will you please be good and cross over for me, Papa?"
Director: "I don't like the way you said that, Sayo."
Sayo: "There's nothing I can do, since I don't know any other way to say it."
Director: "Sayo, I'm sorry for the trouble I've caused."
Sayo: "I won't forgive you, but since you're dead, it's okay. I'm not mad."
*The air enveloping them is a gentle one.*
*I feel that since this is the parting of a parent and a child, it is more serene than it is sad.*
*... That's why I feel uneasy about it.*
Mari: "Sayo-chan, it's dangerous!"
*At almost the same time as Mari speaks, I jump up.*
Sayo: "Huh?"
*It appeared from the floor.*
*The black giant. Comprised of the darkness of human hearts, the whale itself.*
*As if appearing from the director's shadow itself, it leaps up from the floor that served as its sea level.*
*Being only a second away from being consumed, he pushes Sayo away. Even so, the giant crashes into them, knocking them to opposite sides.*
Mari: "Stay back!"
*Reacting to Mari's words, the intense blue light envelopes the room.*
*However, the black shadow casts a crimson light, mixing it with Mari's light. The room is dyed in a violet that seizes my vision for several seconds, until finally I can comprehend the situation. By that point, the light has already vanished.*
Mayu: "This is terrible, too cruel! Using such a tactic to trick her...! You're no real fath-- Ow!"
*As I hurry to sit my body up, I feel the pain in my right shoulder from hitting the wall. Still, it's not bad enough to stop me from moving. I grit my teeth and stand up.*
Mayu: "Mari's... gone. The director and whale too. Could they--?!"
*I wasn't able to see the whale for more than a second, and I couldn't see what happened after that at all. Just like when it appeared in the director's office, it was in the midst of casting a wriggling shadow. In that case, it is incomplete.*
*I wonder if the true intention behind the director trying to drag Sayo in was in order to deceive all of us.*
*To throw Mari and I off guard, then swallow us up. Sayo and I were able to avoid this fate in the end, but we needed Mari. Taking Mari was the equivalent of taking in the last of the aquarium's power.*
*He deceived Sayo and is trying to use Mari.*
*Whether as a human or not, I feel this is something unforgivable. We have no choice but to pursue him. This time, I have to save Mari.*
*Before that, Sayo is...*
*I spot her shadow, lying face down against a tank on the opposite side of the room. The impact apparently caused her to crash into the tank. The acrylic side of the aquarium is smeared with blood.*
Mayu: "Sayo!"
*There is no response.*
*As I try to gently sit her up, I see that her forehead is split open, and a large amount of blood is flowing out. It seems as if the spot where she was beaten by Saginuma Rieko has opened and gotten much worse.*
Mayu: "I have to stop the bleeding, even just a little..."
*I remember what Kenji-san did with his hands and press on Sayo's head with a handkerchief. In the blink of an eye, the light blue handkerchief is dyed red, the viscous blood sticking to my own fingers.*
*The vividly red-colored blood is proof that Sayo is alive. I feel no objection to or fear of the blood.*
*The intensity of the blood is decreasing. This is Sayo's--any person's ability to keep on living. It feels like I'm seeing a miracle.*
*Her heart is beating, she's breathing. Still, I can't be completely relieved just yet. Just like last time, it's no shallow wound, and there's the chance that the impact may have injured her in other places as well.*
*I have to get her to a hospital as soon as possible. I have no time for doubt.*
*Even though the exit is near, if I move Sayo, I may not be able to go after Mari!!*
*Having made it this far, why...?*
*I will save Mari.*
*I stand up in sync with my decision.*
*Up to this point, many things have happened.*
*I made a promise with Himeno and came back here again. I was able to become closer with Sayo. I was able to part with both Saginuma Rieko and Kenji-san. I overcame numerous hardships.*
*On the other hand, I came to know my own smallness as a human being.*
*One person alone is relatively powerless. One person alone is essentially dead.*
*That's exactly why I made this decision.*
*To choose one thing, to not choose the other.*
*Himeno denied this, but I understand it once more. I cannot accept what the director said, but we are all miniscule individuals, after all.*
*Wanting everything, wanting anything and everything... this is what we call desire, but fumbling around carelessly just to fulfill it will not yield success.*
*Just one strong will.*
*I'm sure that this is the one thing I'm clinging to.*
*... Is it okay?*
*I hear a voice.*
Mayu: "It's fine, Himeno."
*... Is it okay?*
Mayu: "It's fine, Sayo."
*... Is it okay?*
Mayu: "It's fine, Mari."
*... Is it okay?*
Mayu: "It's fine, myself."
*... Really?*
Mayu: "It's already been decided."
*... Is that your answer?*
Mayu: "'He who chases two hares catches neither,' isn't that how it goes? So, it's fine."
*... Me.*
Mayu: "Well, what's that?"
*... Huh?*
*Behind me, a large, black shadow.*
*... A whale?*
Mayu: "That's right. This is the shape of your doubt."
*Before I know it, my vision is dyed violet.*
Mayu: "You should already understand by now. That no matter which you choose, you won't be forgiven."
*... That's not true at all. I--!*
Mayu: "You rejected yourself. You negated everything you've done up to now."
*... But I had no choice but to choose.*
*The whale is gradually taking form and finally emerges as one cluster.*
*The whale becomes large enough to cover the whole room, seeming like a cumulonimbus cloud.*
Mayu: "You shouldn't lie to yourself."
*... It's painful, making this confession.*
*I shouldn't have to doubt.*
*I shouldn't have to choose.*
Mayu: "If you're in pain, then hurry and get swallowed up."
*The whale, with its large mouth open, chases after me.*
*Before long, the violet world is cut off.*
*I realize it, within this eternal cloudiness.*
*As soon as I give up on either of them, my heart will break...*
*I will save Sayo.*
*I shouldn't have time for doubt.*
*No matter what, I can't choose either one. In this case, I can take Sayo back, call an ambulance, and quickly return to save Mari.*
*This is the only way.*
*I'll do it, I will.*
*I came this far. If I don't give it my all, then what am I doing?*
Himeno: "Geez, Mayu, it's because you're still putting everything on yourself."
Mayu: "Huh...?"
*Searching for the voice's owner, my eyes grow large.*
*An unexpected person has appeared before my eyes.*
Mayu: "Himeno...! Why are you here?!"
Himeno: "I guess I couldn't sit around and wait either. Just like you said, I tried coming in through the entrance. I said 'Please let me in, I forgot something inside,' and the security guard made a fuss, then let me through."
Himeno: "I probably disappeared partway through the gate, but I'm sure I can come up with some excuse for that later."
Mayu: "Making a fuss... Well you caused him trouble, right?"
Himeno: "Well, it is what it is. I learned my obstinance from watching you... By the way, what's going on here? With all those big holes, this place has gotten dangerous..."
Mayu: "This place is coming to an end. Moreover, how are you feeling?"
Himeno: "A little fuzzy. Like I'm fading into my exhaustion, but I'm aware that I'm not myself, which probably means the medicine is working. I wanted to come here and support you."
*Himeno smiles with a, "Hehehe." The circles under her eyes tell me she's still not recovered, but I don't intend to refuse her outright.*
*The liberation of the grudge that coordinated the Red Manten Aquarium, by way of Mari's power. That will also lead to the undoing of the curse that has leapt into the outside world as well.*
*Just as Himeno says, it's probably just the effects of the medicine. However, I believe that Mari lead her here.*
*At this moment, her being here... No matter how strong my heart is, I can't put it into words.*
*I control the urge to embrace her for now, and look in her eyes as I say...*
Mayu: "Take this girl and go back to the real world. If you call an ambulance right away, I'm sure she'll be saved."
Himeno: "I see, this is the girl you tried to save! Her injuries are terrible...!"
*Himeno seems to have finally noticed the girl lying on the ground. Seeing that striking red color, Himeno's pupils seem to contract. Even for Himeno, her focus seems to be wavering.*
*But at this point, Himeno is the only one I can rely on. I'm kind of glad that the person I'm counting on in the end is her.*
Mayu: "I'll explain... after we get home. I'm going to rescue Mari."
Himeno: "Mari-chan is your little sister, right? By going to save her, you..."
*I still haven't explained anything. It's not unthinkable that Himeno would be confused.*
*However, I don't have time to explain it from the start. I look Himeno straight in the eye.*
Mayu: "Trust me... and wait for me. I'll definitely come back."
*I squeeze Himeno's hand and say this.*
*Along with this wish, I entrust her with my feelings. That is my intention.*
*Just like when we were in the taxi, I expect to receive various objections. Himeno leaves only enough time to blink, then responds.*
Himeno: "I understand. This is something only you can do. When you come back... let's eat ice cream together!"
Mayu: "Cookies and cream. I'll have a double order."
*Looking at Sayo's watch, it's getting close to midnight. This is when I'm usually asleep, but in these circumstances, I'm not the least bit sleepy.*
*There's no time... It's not just the time limit before the destruction of Manten Aquarium, but the end of the night of the full moon as well.*
Himeno: "Mayu, I believe in you."
*Himeno, carrying Sayo on her back, stands up. When she extends her legs, she wobbles and stumbles, but straightens up her back with some confidence in her stability.*
*Sayo has about the same build as I do, so it should be no problem for Himeno to carry her.*
Mayu: "Don't overdo it, Himeno. Your health is the most important..."
Himeno: "What are you saying? I will do this, even if it's to the point of ridiculousness, because you entrusted me with this. Anyway, that should have been my line!"
*When Himeno takes a step, I hear the sound of something falling.*
*It's the keychain. The one with the jellyfish drop attached. The same as the one I dropped. It seems to have fallen out of Sayo's pocket.*
Mayu: "Why? Sayo should have had the starfish... She should have had Mari's keychain!"
Himeno: "So it was this girl then. I just realized it. When she bumped into you, this girl was the one who dropped the keychain. At that time, you must have gotten them switched up. It was something important to you, right Mayu. You carried it like a charm?"
Mayu: "No."
*I put the keychain inside Himeno's pocket.*
Himeno: "Mayu?"
Mayu: "It's your charm of protection, Himeno. When this girl-- When Sayo wakes up, give it back to her. I'm sure she just missed the chance to return it, but I want to wait until I hear that from Sayo herself."
Himeno: "Okay~ Roger! Be careful!"
Mayu: "Right!"
*Our exchange ends here.*
*If possible, I want to talk with her more.*
*If possible, I want us to go together.*
*Even so...*
*I will grant my wish. Mari tried to do that for me.*
*Not so I can part with the past. Not so I can part with Mari. So that in the distant future, I can surely see her again with a smile...*
Mayu: "This world... really is going to end."
*No matter which way I look, it's a world full of holes. The black erosion continues, the blue particles filling my vision even more than before.*
*I can sense them against my body, and I'm sure I have less than fifteen minutes. This isn't the time limit of when this world will completely disappear, but rather how long I have left to get back going the way I came.*
*I have no more time to spend with Mari.*
*However, I will rescue Mari. Even if I only have a second. In the end, if I can just see Mari's happy smile,*
*I won't regret anything else.*
*But where in the world has she been taken to?*
Mayu: "Mari... answer me..."
*I lace my hands together in front of my chest... and pray.*
*I don't even consider the idea that it probably won't get through.*
*I believe.*
*Because Mari is connected to this aquarium. No matter how I look at it, it doesn't seem strange.*
*... Ster.*
Mayu: "Mari!"
*... Big sister!*
Mayu: "... I got it. Wait for me!"
*I heard it. Mari's voice.*
*Immediately after, a small, glowing fish appears before me. Its transparency isn't from its natural state, but likely from it taking form from the aquarium's attempt to preserve itself.*
*It has a form similar to the tropical fish known as an angelfish. It has no striped pattern, and the light emitting from its body isn't just light blue like Mari's, but has some gold mixed in.*
*It's triangular body is wavering, as if it's trying to signal me.*
*I run, following that form. As the angelfish wiggles along, it swims forward through the air. I realize after a little bit that it's to avoid the holes in the floor.*
Mayu: "I can trust in this little one, can't I?"
*I increase my speed further.*
*Once we arrive at the staff passageway, the fish stops.*
*It begins swimming in circles there, and I understand what it's trying to tell me.*
Mayu: "She's here... isn't she?"
*A ladder has been prepared against the wall.*
*The tunnel tank should be side-by-side with this passage. In other words, if I climb this ladder, I should reach the top part of the tunnel tank. It's a place for doing maintanence on the tank and giving the fish food.*
*... And the place where Saginuma Rieko was pushed around by Kenji-san, and where Mari drowned to death.*
Mayu: "You chose this place on purpose, didn't you, Director?"
*I take a small breath and place one hand on the ladder.*
*The angelfish follows along as if it's pursuing me.*
*As I climb up to the tunnel tank, I instantly feel as if the space has increased. Be that as it may, the scaffolding is still the only bridge connecting one side to the other.*
*The floor space seems wider than the ceiling, likely larger than an entire three stories. The deep water is insignificant compared to the places that have disappeared in the darkness.*
*The director is standing at the other side of the scaffolding.*
*At his side... he holds Mari.*
Mayu: "Please, let Mari go."
*He's holding Mari's neck between his arms.*
