*He can touch Mari. In other words, the director is already--*
Director: "We are already becoming an existence greater than some mere grudge, but I took on this temporary form in order to explain the situation to you."
*The director has seized Mari. She seems to be in pain, trying to resist him by pulling away his hands. However, her voice won't come out, as if her throat is being compressed, and all she can do is kick her legs as she writhes about.*
Mayu: "Wasn't it Mari's power that liberated the aquarium? And now the situation has changed... so your power has exceeded Mari's?"
Director: "There is no need for you to know. At this point, I will give you a warning."
*He ignores my question and continues.*
*The water's surface is calm, I do not see the whale. Still, I am vigilant to the possibility that it could suddenly appear as I lend him an ear.*
Director: "Go back immediately. As long as I have this girl, your sister, within my control, there is no need for you to die. There is no changing it, this world will disappear. I recommend that you return while there is still a path."
Mayu: "What's going on all of a sudden? Earlier, you said it would swallow everything...!"
Director: "The circumstances have changed... This is probably the second time I have said this."
Mayu: "That wasn't you, but Saginuma Rieko who said it."
Director: "At that time, Saginuma Rieko was also part of the aquarium. She did something stupid by vanishing in order to return to her individual self."
*The director continues in an uninterested tone.*
*His inflection has decreased even further compared to when he was talking to Sayo.*
Director: "When you receive a present from someone, you are happy. When you are hurt, you suffer. When someone you love dies, you cry."
Director: "When a child is born, you feel blessed... These are all emotions humans know without having to be taught, but it's a step above instinct as a truth of psychology. That's a general concept."
Director: "This girl is a pure existence, close to the limits of nothingness. I wonder if you understand. She wants you all to be happy... It's hard to believe, but this desire is equal to a general concept."
Director: "You all likely don't realize it, but this girl is already in the process of changing into her desire. This means she's being sublimated into a metaphysical existence."
Mayu: "Don't say 'this girl'! She has a name, Mari."
Mayu: "Moreover, whatever her desire, it is... Mari's wish."
*I say this as I glare at him.*
Director: "That's enough word play. Anyway, this transformation into a concept means that she will lose her individuality."
Director: "The reason I was able to seize this girl is because she has sustained her individuality."
Director: "However, combining with this highly pure aquarium is what I'm doing at this moment. It's due to her lingering attachments from her bond with you that she cannot be completely sublimated."
Director: "After I finish dyeing the seed of a new idea that this girl created with the grudge that keeps this world in order, I will have it blossom according to my liking."
Director: "That was my plan long ago. In the event you didn't go back, I would finish swallowing up this space along with you. If you went back, you would be saved. Either way, the outcome doesn't change."
Director: "This girl probably wishes for you to be saved. I suggest you return to the human world."
*As the director says this, Mari stops resisting.*
*This tells me that this man is not mistaken. If I go back the way I came, I will be saved. This is Mari's wordless message.*
*Then, Mari--*
*Of course. She will be swallowed up by that mysterious darkness and will be unable to go to heaven. I don't know if there is a heaven as that in itself may be a conceptual issue, but my desire is for Mari to go, laughing.*
Director: "Well then, you must choose."
Mayu: "It's already decided..."
*There's no other way about it. I will save Mari.*
*Almost at the same time as I intend to thrust my resolve at the director...*
*The whale takes form above the director's head. Different from before, its shape is clear enough to know that it is a whale.*
Mayu: "...!!"
Director: "Once I'm consumed, this world will vanish within five minutes. That is your time extension."
*The whale swims elegantly throughout the room, tank and all. Despite the fact that the whale has once again taken on the form of a cloud, it stirs up the water, and, as if a bucket were turned over from above my head, I am soaked with water.*
*And just like that, the whale swallows up Mari and Sakuragi Isao in a straight line.*
Mayu: "Mari!"
*It's something I do almost on instinct.*
*I charge across the bridge above the upper part of the tank and leap into the whale's mouth. The angelfish that has followed me heads into the whale's mouth as well, seeming to swim with all its might...*
*It's not as if I have a plan.*
*Still, the fact that I have this fish with me means that Mari wasn't completely swallowed up.*
*He said that her bond with me is what's keeping her from completely purifying the aquarium, but on the other hand, maybe it's protecting her from becoming one with the director.*
*Moreover, when Sayo created the whale, it swallowed me, but it was a phenomena completely different from being eaten. Just as he said, this whale is probably just one general concept from the assembled body of the grudge. It can't just be destroyed.*
*Sayo herself said that it was the same whale.*
*More than anything, if she didn't have an idea, Mari wouldn't have lead me here.*
Mari: "Geez, big sister. You always do stupid stuff."
*My vision is a faint world of light.*
*If I had to describe it, I would say I'm deep within the water, far away from the sun.*
*In order to test that, I try to reach my hands towards the voice.*
Mayu: "Because you believed in me."
Mari: "Then I can't say bye-bye with my heart at ease."
Mayu: "Yeah. That's why I need you to lend me your strength a little longer."
Mari: "Leave it to me, but the only one who can tell you the truth is this lady, so look sharp!"
*The angelfish at my side floats up, disappearing into the light. Having become particles of ultramarine light, the angelfish's remains become one with the light.*
*The light swells even further, completely filling my vision.*
*Within that, a faint image appears.*
Rieko: "This is... What's..."
*It's the voice of a shocked Saginuma Rieko. She's looking over some sort of document.*
Rieko: "I have never comitted embezzlement nor anything of the sort."
*Seated on the antique furniture, Sakuragi Isao peers emotionlessly at her in her flustered state.*
Rieko: "Even if you try to squeeze me out of this company, the employees will no longer trust you. Quite the opposite."
*After that, I cannot hear what Saginuma Rieko is saying.*
*The image has already begun to disappear.*
*Sakuragi Isao continues to sit there, his expression unchanging. Within his eyes, he is trying to stifle his anxiety.*
*Whether Mari is showing me this or I am merely able to see it is not clear.*
*However, at the same time that this is the final action of Saginuma Rieko, it is a moment Sakuragi Isao can never return to.*
*When the image disappears, this time, I am swallowed by a whirlpool. I do not resist, but rather give in to it.*
*When I come to, I am standing in the jellyfish booth. My body and clothes are just the same, and there is a mistiness to the edge of my vision.*
*There is no erosion or sign of bugs eating away at the aquarium, so I realize quickly that this is a scene Mari has created.*
Director: "How ironic. Why did she choose this place?"
*Sakuragi Isao is also here. He looks to the sky to ask his question, but no reply comes.*
Director: "This place was constructed after my death... and is a place detached from my ideal. For Manten Aquarium, you could say it's the one place that has no connection to me."
*Sometimes as he talks, the director's body sways.*
*As I think that he is, perhaps, looking at me, I am once again in a similar situation to before.*
Director: "By limiting my location, you intend to oppose me, then. However, we no longer have the time to waste."
*The director is definitely not looking at me. So I decide to take a step in his direction.*
Mayu: "You are... a human."
Director: "That's right, you couldn't get away. Still, there's nothing to worry about. You are merely heading for your demise."
Mayu: "You're merely trembling before your own sins."
Director: "The one meeting with you is merely one part of me. The majority of me is still continuing the erosion. Soon I shall conquer that girl."
Mayu: "You were scared, weren't you? You were made to believe, 'Even if I die, nothing is impossible.' That's why you chose to hate. To throw away your humanity, and to evade their reproachful eyes."
Director: "You are already losing your form. You understand what that means, don't you? Unrelated to your will, there's nothing for you to do but become part of the larger flow of wills."
Mayu: "You're trying to run away, as if nothing ever happened. Even now, that basis hasn't changed. That's why... you can't run away from the fact that you are a human. There's no difference between the directive known as Sakuragi Isao and us."
Director: "Silence!"
*In contrast to his emotionlessness up to this point, he shouts as if to obstruct my words.*
*This is probably the feeling of anger. As if acting in concert with this, several tanks in the jellyfish booth break.*
*I close my eyes out of shock at the sound, but manage not to raise my voice in response.*
*I am watching the director's wavering form as I say...*
Mayu: "So you aren't a human after all?"
*At almost the same time as I finish saying this, everything is dyed in black. The jellyfish booth disappears in an instant, and it becomes like we have sunk into the lightless ocean.*
*When I lay my eyes on that last scene, it is the truth I see through.*
*Mari erasing the directive was likely to stop the director.*
*I sink further. As I feel something brush against my butt, darkness once again gives way to the world.*
*It's the Deep-sea Fish booth I visited with Mari and Sayo. Like it was before, the mist still hasn't faded from my vision.*
Director: "Why?! Why am I not being sublimated?!"
*Letting out a cry, his form is clearer than before.*
*I stand up, and once again face him.*
Mayu: "I think..."
Mayu: "There's no one who's okay with dying. No matter what happens, no one feels happy to die. Not Mari, not me. Surely not Himeno or Sayo, maybe not even Saginuma Rieko, or you."
Mayu: "If only someone could have accepted you in spite of your self-loathing. With that alone, you might have been saved."
Director: "Don't speak as if you know! My objective is to become a concept. It isn't to escape."
*The tank pulses, and all of the water changes to blood.*
*Before the red light that flows out fills the whole room, I once again sink into the sea of darkness.*
*He has probably already realized that he cannot escape.*
*Not showing any surprise, Sakuragi Isao stands stock still.*
*I drift toward the souvenir stand. It's the place that, five years ago, led to Mari and I being separated forever.*
Mayu: "People do good things and bad things... Isn't that natural? For me, it is. Right, Mari?"
*That's exactly why I ask Mari.*
*Mari, enveloped in light, is there. The light is so intense it's incomparable to anything that came before.*
Mari: "That's right! Even though you should have at least traded with me!"
Mayu: "I'm sorry about that time. Are you still mad?"
Mari: "I'm not mad, since you said you were sorry."
Mari: "I'm also sorry... looks like my time is up. I'm going to disappear."
Mayu: "Yeah, but since it's you, Mari, I'm sure you'll be happy no matter where you go."
Mari: "You too, big sister! That's why..."
*That's because it's my... our wish.*
*Time is terribly short. We've finally reached this parting, one that took the passage of five years to last only half a day, and could never be enough.*
*Still, I am satisfied with it. Reality will not change. I know that no matter what I do today, I can never revive Mari.*
*That's why, at the very least, I don't want to have regrets.*
*Those feelings are granted in this moment.*
Mayu: "Mari, you okay?"
Mari: "Yeah. I know!"
Director: "Wha-- This body is......!"
*A blue light begins flowing out of Sakuragi Isao's body.*
Director: "I have transcended my individuality... Why am I disappearing as Sakuragi Isao...?!"
Mayu: "Mari is taking you with her. No matter what sins you are shouldering, since you are a human, the place you will go... is the same."
Director: "Stop this... I... will live eternally as a concept..."
Mayu: "But this is proof that your soul has already accepted its humanity."
*I don't take my eyes off of the disappearing director.*
Director: "Gah... Stop this, please... I..."
*It takes no time for all of him to become particles and become one with the light.*
*He crossed over, suffering, as a human.*
*... Fear is born only in the hearts of humans.*
*I have no idea whether or not he was saved.*
*Taking a small breath, I turn to Mari, who has almost completely become light.*
Mayu: "Mari..."
*There is no response, merely a nod.*
*She is enveloped by a gentle light.*
*Whether Mari is a human, or a mere concept, I honestly am not sure. Without a doubt, Mari was able to interfere in this world after all.*
*But the last expression Mari showed me was a smile.*
*At the very least, I was saved by her.*
*That's why, from now on, I'll treat her wish like a treasure and continue to help it come true.*
*No matter what, to me, she was human.*
*That night, I dreamt of a whale swimming into the heavens.*
*Numerous fish were chasing its large tail fin, the swell creating whirlpools. The mixture of light and dark was like déjà vu.*
Himeno: "Mayu! Mayu!"
*It's a familiar voice calling my name that wakes me up.*
Sayo: "Yelling at a hospital patient, do you have no common sense?"
*There's another voice as well.*
*Even in my sleep, I know right away the owners of the voices.*
Himeno: "But I'm worried about her! I finally woke up and was surprised to hear from the nurse that my friend was in the hospital too... So much so that I couldn't stand!"
Sayo: "You're so noisy. This isn't your area of expertise, so just be quiet and wait."
Himeno: "When you put it that way, it isn't yours either, Sayo-chan!"
Sayo: "Tch. Whatever, chubby."
Himeno: "Chub-- Hey, that has nothing to do with this! And I'm not particularly chubby!"
Sayo: "You're somewhat like a cow, making you at least ten kilos heavier than me."
Himeno: "I-I'm not that heavy!"
Sayo: "There's a ten kilo difference between me before Sayo eats and you after you've eaten."
Himeno: "Hey... what's up with that meanness...? And you speak in the third person quite often... Could you be prissy...?"
Sayo: "I'm not prissy. Don't push the boundaries of common sense."
Himeno: "I think it's fine~ You can be so cute and yet still have these moments, eh, Sayo-chan?"
Sayo: "Don't make fun of me!"
Himeno: "Geez, don't be so loud around a hospital patient!"
*Finally my body has begun to awaken, the desire to stay like this with my eyes closed lingers.*
*Himeno and Sayo continue their exchange. I'm not sure when it was that they got to know each other, but regardless, they're like fire and oil.*
*Speaking of which, the two of them mentioned a hospital patient. Does that mean, in this case... I'm the patient?*
Miku: "I could hear you two from out there. This is a hospital, doncha think you should be a little quieter?"
*Another voice mixes in with the other two, who are getting along as well as dogs and cats, and now I begin to find myself really confused.*
*Looking through thin slits, a small girl is standing next to Himeno and Sayo. She's probably shorter than I am and is wearing a soft and frilly dress. She's fashionable and quite a different type from Himeno and Sayo.*
Sayo: "You did it!"
Himeno: "Sayo-chan did it!"
Miku: "Okay, okay. Now, now. If you fight everyday, you'll just annoy Mayumi-chan."
*She warns the both of them in a gentle tone.*
*I close my eyes once more, and think about what I should do.*
Himeno: "Well... it's already been a week since Mayu was hospitalized. The doctor said her life is in no danger, but... I'm worried..."
*I see, I've been hospitalized for a week already.*
*It was the end of June when we visited Manten Aquarium, so it should now be about the first week of July.*
*There was something really really big waiting for me in July...*
Mayu: "There's... finals next week!"
*Without realizing it, I pop up. Pushing up the white fabric, I once more realize that this is a hospital room.*
*Even though I felt differently on the day I hung out with Himeno, I had intended to study.*
*My results from the previous midterm exams weren't good, so I had planned to take about two weeks to study, but--*
Himeno: "Ah."
Sayo: "Ah."
Mayu: "Ah..."
*I awaken, too quickly for my liking, and surely in a different way than what they expected.*
*Himeno, blinking her eyes rapidly, stares at me.*
*From a surprised look to a smile, then to a tearful one. In such a short span of time, all of Himeno's feelings come across to me.*
Mayu: "Himeno..."
Himeno: "Mayu..."
*Before I realize it, drops begin to fall from my eyes as well.*
*For me, it's equivalent to a reunion after several hours, so why do I feel so nostalgic?*
Himeno: "Mayu!"
*Unable to bear it any longer, Himeno leaps at me. Feeling the softness of her and her warmth, I am sure that this really is my best friend.*
Sayo: "I'm here too."
Miku: "Sayo-chan, did you want in on the warm and fuzzies as well?"
Sayo: "Don't make this weird."
*For awhile, Himeno doesn't leave my side. It doesn't bother me at all, so I hug her back, and we remain on top of the bed like that until the doctor comes.*
*After that, a lot of difficult things happened.*
*First, somehow, on that day, at midnight on the dot, it seems I had collapsed at the entrance to Manten Aquarium. The security officer who had found me called an ambulance and I was hospitalized, bringing us to the present.*
*Sayo was immediately hospitalized, and Himeno, who had been with her, fell into a slight panic learning I had also been brought in.*
*The soft and frilly girl, her name is Minato Miku. She's Sayo's classmate, and was apparently one of the victims of Manten Aquarium, so she has been hospitalized here in the same hospital up to now. On that day, her mental state was restored.*
*From this to that, it seems a lot has happened, but those are all other stories.*
*Like the fact that somehow I avoided any sort of interrogation from my parents, or that, despite the doctor trying to get me to rest, I've been studying really hard since I woke up, or that once again a gravestone has been made for Mari.*
*All of those are other stories.*
*I have no time to think about how I'm going to live my life from now on... Surely my busy days will continue, but ever since that date, something surrounding me has definitely changed.*
*That's why, just a little...*
*I'll live my life, facing things head on.*
*It isn't that I chose to stay here because I don't care about what happens to Himeno.*
*I remember the emotions I felt when I saved this girl in the underground tunnel.*
*... If I abandon someone right before my eyes who is in trouble, Himeno will be mad at me.*
*Kenji-san didn't say I should abandon the girl to save Himeno, but in reality, there is no difference.*
*Abandoning the girl... is what that means.*
*Even if I did save Himeno, if she heard I did such a thing, she would definitely be angry.*
*Himeno can surely make such weighty decisions for herself. It's a thin-line between that and cruelty. That's why Himeno wouldn't want me burdened with such a choice.*
*I'm different from Himeno. I'm not as decisive as she is.*
*We may argue, but if I'm honest when looking into my own heart, this is why Himeno and I are friends.*
*However, once I make a decision, I stick with it and believe in it.*
Mayu: "I said I wanted to save her... because I felt that I wanted to be her friend."
*Upon giving those words voice, I can feel that they have power.*
*I sit next to the girl in a comfortable position.*
Mayu: "On top of that, I haven't made up with Mari yet."
*Mari is definitely here inside this Red Manten Aquarium.*
*I can sense that if I don't solve all of the mysteries here, I cannot return to a normal life. This is what the intuition rushing through me is telling me.*
*My story.*
*Ever since Mari went missing, each day seems to pass indistinctly.*
*But each and every day, I sought out Mari.*
*Submitting police reports, having the police continue their investigation.*
*It became a small piece of news, but as it was unrelated to cases of bizarre murderers and kidnappers, it quickly stopped being a topic of conversation.*
*It was about the time I moved on to junior high. All of a sudden, my parents began to erase all traces of Mari.*
*Speaking honestly, it wasn't a big deal. My parents just began to dispose of anything of Mari's left in the house.*
*The kids room the two of us used became a room for one, the square table with four chairs became a circular table for three.*
*It's not like it all happened at once, but to me, it felt so sudden.*
*... It's like I was an only child from the start.*
*Those words were never said, but I always felt like I was hearing them.*
*I had moved on to my third year of junior high -- ninth grade.*
*Not even a trace of Mari's existence remained for my parents.*
Sayo: "You."
Mayu: "Wah!"
*It was so sudden.*
*I had been ruminating on the past and have now been abruptly yanked back to the present.*
*Having opened her eyes, the girl slowly sits up and looks at me.*
*She is pressing on the injury on her head and seems to be in pain, but in her eyes shines a great strength.*
Sayo: "It seems I'm in debt to you once more."
Mayu: "Stop calling it a debt, or anything of the sort. I saved you because I wanted to... and I wasn't working alone."
Sayo: "Huh?"
*The girl continues to listen with a haughty attitude as I tell her about what happened with Kenji-san.*
*I can't help but feel some admiration for someone like her, who is able to maintain her typical attitude even with such terrible injuries.*
*The girl says nothing until I finish my story.*
Sayo: "I have a general idea of what you're saying."
*The girl slowly blinks her eyes once.*
*After looking me in the eye, she turns away. Her cheeks have begun to turn red from the flow of blood.*
*Although she normally speaks about everything so plainly, in a rare turn of events she seems to be chewing the corners of her mouth, searching for what she wants to say.*
*After she slowly blinks once more, she returns her gaze to meet mine.*
Sayo: "---- Ou."
*Her voice is extremely soft.*
Mayu: "... Huh?"
*Unable to clearly make out what she said, I tilt my head.*
Mayu: "What did you say just now?"
Sayo: "Nothing. It wasn't important."
Mayu: "Nope. I'm curious, so tell me."
Sayo: "Shut up. Enough with the pushiness."
Mayu: "Aw. Tell me!"
*Facing the girl, who has fallen silent and averted her eyes from me, I continue to prompt her.*
*She seems to be giving in, her broad, pouty expression finally giving way to a loud sigh.*
Sayo: "I said 'thank you'! Any complaints?"
*She folds her arms and looks away.*
Mayu: "You were able to thank me..."
Sayo: "You are way too rude."
Mayu: "That's not something I wanna hear from you."
Sayo: "Tch. Stop calling me 'you.' It's Sayo. Sakuragi Sayo."
Mayu: "Sayo... chan. Well, Sayo-chan, you quit calling me 'you' too."
Sayo: "I got it, Mayumi. That's fine, isn't it? Now you stop adding 'chan.' I'm not a child."
Mayu: "Hehe... Thank you."
*It's kind of refreshing to be called Mayumi. Himeno, my parents, and everyone else always seems to call me "Mayu." But then, trying to have everyone call you by a nickname of your own choosing would also be weird.*
Sayo: "Still, is this all right? By saving me, your friend might die, Mayumi."
Mayu: "What? You heard that...?"
Sayo: "Somewhat. It doesn't matter. What will you do?"
Mayu: "Think of a way for her not to die. It's not that I'm abandoning Himeno after all."
Sayo: "You're too optimistic. Even when you have no idea what will happen."
*At Sayo's blunt assertion, I look at her and shake my head.*
Mayu: "The answer's in what you just said. Even if something happens, it won't be out of place... even if everything goes well, that won't be out of place either."
Sayo: "You're nitpicking. Tch, why did I have to be rescued by someone like you?"
Mayu: "Really, you're so rude..."
*A laugh slips out in spite of my shock.*
*The nervous tension that has stretched further and further over time feels as if it has finally loosened. Of course, that doesn't mean we can be careless.*
*It's just that being able to talk to someone around my age in a safe place is giving me a sense of calm.*
*It's scary how consistently cold she's been, but she seems to be lightening up now. Sayo's way of speaking shows that her feelings have surely been stressing out her since she came here.*
Sayo: "Can I trust you, Mayumi?"
Mayu: "Huh...? What do you mean...?"
Sayo: "This is my situation. I don't know who or what I can trust here. That's why I want to get confirmation from you. If I can believe you, no matter what happens, I'll give what you have to say precedence."
Sayo: "If you hadn't come, Mayumi, I would have died. It's frustrating, but it's also proof that I can't handle this on my own."
Sayo: "You act with the intent to return home. You have determination. Mine wasn't strong enough, but with your resolve, I think I can believe in you."
*Sayo looks at me with a serious expression.*
*It's not an expression of hate. When I look into Sayo's eyes, I can see a determination even deeper than mine.*
Mayu: "I get it. Trust me. Because I trust you."
Sayo: "That's not enough. If we don't trust each other completely, we'll definitely falter."
*In response to Sayo's clipped words, I nod deeply.*
Mayu: "Okay. I promise."
*I know what she's trying to say, so I'm satisfied.*
Mayu: "So then, we're friends."
Sayo: "That's a separate issue."
Mayu: "Really... you aren't very sincere."
*I'm sure she's not satisfied, but the two of us have ended up in the same boat.*
*A smile creeps onto my face and I change the subject. Sayo has a sullen expression on her face.*
Mayu: "So I want to go ahead and talk to you about this. Sayo, why... how did you end up almost getting killed by Saginuma Rieko?"
*Kenji-san had said that she was the criminal who had killed the director.*
*Sayo wasn't surprised when she heard that piece of information, so she must have already known. Still, I can't imagine what sort of exchange took place between them that lead to something like that.*
*There was nothing normal about what happened there. She bashed Sayo's head so many times into that hard aquarium glass.*
*It was a different fear than what I felt when Kenji-san went crazy.*
*I want to understand what it was that could make someone show that side of themselves.*
Sayo: "You already know that... she's the criminal who killed Papa, right?"
*Sayo was emphasizing this information, so I returned my gaze to her in response.*
Sayo: "If so, then this will go quicker. She... Saginuma Rieko, is already dead."
Mayu: "She's dead...?!"
*My voice seems to be twisted.*
*It's so sudden, I can hardly believe it. Even though Sayo was able to touch her.*
*I refuse to believe this information, since if she were a ghost, she wouldn't be able to be touched by anything.*
*As if to soothe the doubts that spring up in my moment of shock, Sayo continues in a warning tone.*
Sayo: "I don't know the principle behind it. However, within this Red Manten Aquarium, Saginuma has continued to exist for the past five years since that incident."
Sayo: "It's just, she can't exist anywhere but within this space. There are many facts to support that theory. First of all, her outer appearance hasn't changed at all in five years from the Saginuma Rieko in my memories. She isn't aging."
Mayu: "... She was an acquaintance?"
Sayo: "That shouldn't be surprising. I am the director's daughter, and she was an employee of the aquarium. We've spoken before."
*Sayo dispassionately moves the conversation along.*
Sayo: "She was a little bewildered at first, but then she realized who I was. Even though over five years have passed since we met. Something about her has transcended time."
Sayo: "This is just speculation, but she won't leave here. Just as that Hiyoshi guy told you, it's not that one can't get out of here. Saginuma Rieko doesn't even seem to have considered leaving this place."
Sayo: "Still, because there's no nourishment or anything here, one can't lead a life."
Sayo: "It doesn't seem like someone can really continue to live in the place where bizarre phenomena occur for five years. That's to say nothing of the fact that she's so calm, even after losing her life."
Mayu: "Speaking of which, when she appeared before Himeno and I on the stage, she said she was looking for a child."
Sayo: "She has some lingering attachment. You've probably heard of vengeful spirits. Saginuma Rieko is probably that sort of ghost. The child she is looking for is likely the one who witnessed her murdering the director five years ago."
*I draw in my breath with a gulp.*
*Because it was in relation to the director, I had hoped the child in question was Sayo.*
*But that was nothing more than wishful thinking. I have a feeling I know exactly who the child is.*
Mayu: "That must be... Mari. So Mari must also be like Saginuma Rieko then..."
*Sayo blinks, then responds.*
*She seems to be unable to decide whether she should tell me the truth or not.*
*But contrary to Sayo's doubts, I have resolved myself to accept the truth.*
Mayu: "It's all right. I trust you, so I can accept it. Have you forgotten our promise?"
Sayo: "You talk too much."
*Brushing her bangs aside, Sayo's eyes stare intently into mine.*
Sayo: "The little girl you saw is already dead. The reason she looks the same... is because her appearance is the same as when she died."
Mayu: "... Right."
Sayo: "She was probably drawn to those of us still living and showed herself to us. We possess corporeal bodies, which are very rare here."
Sayo: "The phenomena that take place in this version of Manten Aquarium must operate under the same principle. There is the fact that the orca and frogs directly attacked us and broke things to consider."
*Before Sayo can finish telling me everything, I tell her my own hunch.*
Mayu: "The 'spirits' here in this Manten Aquarium are trying to pull us in too. But Mari was definitely... different."
Sayo: "Saginuma Rieko was as well. The human souls are maintaining their own will. I'm sure that's why they're different when it comes to the issue of trying to pull us in."
*Sayo continues with an, "And then..."*
Sayo: "When I spoke with Saginuma Rieko, I realized she was already dead. So I threw that fact back at her."
Sayo: "She became frenzied after that. Surely, her desire to 'pursue and kill that child' is all that remains within her, leaving out the realization that she is 'already dead.'"
Mayu: "So in other words... she can't accept that she has passed away herself?"
Sayo: "Probably, but my words seem to have awakened some doubts in her mind. The next time we meet, she may be more in touch with her place as a resident of the world of the dead, or she may just try to drag me all the way in with her."
*If it were me, I'd probably see it the same way.*
*Her own death was brought to her attention. Even if she denies it, that may have planted a larger seed.*
*Without treating it as a dream in order to accept it, she may forget who she is altogether.*
*If that's the case, one could probably empathize.*
*Saginuma Rieko didn't just kill the director, but was cruel to Sayo.*
*It's unforgivable for her to resort to violence just because she heard something she didn't like.*
Sayo: "If one can only come and go on the night of the full moon, then we don't have much time. Let's go."
Mayu: "Go... where?"
Sayo: "Ahead."
Mayu: "Sayo-chan, you found the one who murdered your father... You've attained your goal. Is it really okay for you to not go home yet?"
Sayo: "I still don't have the truth. If I can meet up with people who have died here, then I can definitely see my father."
Sayo: "Also, I want to end this world. If this world is supposedly created by malice, then I will help all of those souls to cross over."
*Help all of the souls cross over.*
*Guide the souls here to the heavens. All of them.*
*In other words...*
Mayu: "I should... say farewell to Mari."
Mayu: "If Mari's already dead, then I at least want to say goodbye. It might be out of the way for you, Sayo, but will you come with me?"
Sayo: "It isn't out of the way. I get the feeling that everything is connected. If all of the phenomena disappear, then those things inside of your friend will probably disappear as well."
Mayu: "Yeah. Let's solve everything, then the three of us... Himeno and you, Sayo, and I, leave this place."
*Without even nodding, Sayo stands up. Her stance is a firm one.*
*I stand up and clench my fist.*
Mayu: "What's up... ahead of here?"
Sayo: "If we'd taken the normal route, we would have ended up on the side with the tunnel tank, but this is the other side so there's a path connecting to the staff passageway."
Sayo: "Before that is my father's room, although by now I'm sure someone else is using it."
*What Sayo is pointing at is the opposite side of the tank. It's in the direction of where I got lost after entering the staff door from the jellyfish booth.*
*Even without taking the upper path, there seems to be an entrance and exit that you'd never notice if you didn't know about it. If we go that way, we should reach the director's office.*
Sayo: "If this world was created based on the aquarium of five years ago, then the director's office should be my father's room."
Mayu: "Got it. If we don't go... we won't know."
Sayo: "We don't know what will happen. So, don't let your guard down."
Mayu: "... Right!"
*With a door serving as a boundary, it's like a completely different world. We have no idea what dangers could be lurking within.*
*However, not being alone gives me strength.*
*I've felt loneliness so many times over, but I haven't really been alone since I came to Manten Aquarium. Hiyoshi-san and Sayo have both saved me.*
*And Himeno is supporting me from within my heart, so I definitely will not fall apart.*
*My objective is clear.*
*To reunite with Mari... and to say goodbye. I feel like I've accepted it as a fact, but I have no idea how I'll feel on actually seeing Mari's face.*
*Even so, I keep telling myself that I cannot run away.*
*Then we'll go get Himeno.*
*Sayo was the one who said back at the stage that it is because we're in Manten Aquarium that the parasites are overrunning her body and weakening her.*
*But like Sayo said before, if we erase this alternate universe, Himeno should also be cured.*
*It's all starting to come together.*
Sayo: "Don't space out. Let's go."
Mayu: "It's all right. I was just thinking a bit."
*Even as I say that, I'm in denial.*
*Just thinking won't change anything. Right now, there's nothing to do but keep going.*
*As I follow Sayo, we enter a narrow passageway.*
*We are standing before the door of the director's office, not having encountered any particularly strange phenomena.*
*Near the director's office is a reference room and a storeroom, but we decide not to investigate those places. We don't want to invite any unnecessary danger.*
*The design on the imposing door seems very different from what I've seen in Manten Aquarium so far.*
Mayu: "Coming this far and having nothing happen... feels a bit unnatural."
Sayo: "Mulling it over will change nothing. On the other side of this door, something unimaginably horrible might happen. Don't let your guard down."
Mayu: "I know that. I'm not going to get careless or anything."
*I see Sayo put her hand on the door, so I put my hand on it as well.*
Mayu: "Let's go."
*Using those words as a signal, we both put all our strength into opening the door at the same time.*
Mayu: "What's going on here...?"
*Those first words, said upon entering the dim room, echo in a hollow manner.*
Sayo: "Papa's room."
*Sayo's words are cut off there.*
*I think it would be the most accurate to say that the room is ruined.*
*The antique furniture in the room is not merely old, but in ruins. The deterioration of the floor and ceiling is visible, and the time frame of when it was destroyed also seems strange.*
*I've seen Manten Aquarium in so many different circumstances, but this room seems to be from the most distant time period. It's bizarre.*
Mayu: "Ack..."
*As I take a step in, I kick up dust.*
*While it's by no means a large room, it's so cloaked in darkness that I can't see to the center.*
*Sayo's mouth becomes a straight line, and it seems that she doesn't mind the dust. She said this was her father's room. Seeing this room looking like ancient ruins, it's stranger to me that she isn't shaken.*
*Sayo's serious expression seems to be pushing back her sadness and pain.*
Sayo: "Where's Papa? If he really is a vengeful spirit, then he should be here."
*Sayo's father -- the murdered director -- may have been the genesis of this Manten Aquarium's creation.*
*This theory seems very likely. After all, it was written in the staff member's diary that the strange phenomena began immediately after the director's death.*
*However, there is no reply.*
*I step further inside, standing at Sayo's side.*
Mayu: "Look... on top of the desk... There's something there..."
*It is a magnificent desk, like those found in the studies of rich people I've seen on TV. But the dark brown wood has lost its luster, and here and there are cracks that catch the eye.*
*On that desk, I...*
Mayu: "Kyaah!"
Sayo: "What happened?"
*... A hand.*
*On top of the desk... is a hand.*
*But it isn't just a hand.*
*That hand connects to a wrist, which connects to an arm, and the body that connects to is--*
Mayu: "Noo! Wh-Why?!"
*Before I know it, I've fallen to the ground.*
*Hearing me shout, Sayo eyes me suspiciously.*
*Sayo doesn't seem to have noticed what I'm looking at.*
Mayu: "Ken... ji... san..."
*My right hand is shaking.*
*My trembling fingertips point to the object on top of the desk. The fact that they won't stop shaking is not the problem.*
*Just turning my head is easily enough to make me have to directly confront this phenomena...*
Sayo: "A... corpse."
*Sayo steps in front of the lifeless body of Kenji-san that leans on the desk without moving, a thin voice slipping from her lips.*
Mayu: "No... Why...?!"
*I've completely lost my ability to think, much less speak, coherently.*
*Seeing the flesh of the felled Kenji-san before my eyes, my thoughts seem to have left me.*
Mayu: "Why is he... dead...?"
*The fact that I don't even consider whether he could be saved or not leads me to doubt myself.*
*Kenji-san's flesh has lost its vitality, that it won't move again is a thought that I can't seem to accept.*
*The silence of death.*
*It reminds me of the quiet of a relative's body being before me at a funeral.*
Mayu: "Ah... No... o..."
*In contrast to me having slid to the ground and shaking, Sayo moves closer to the body.*
*She puts her hands on his back and then her ear close to his mouth. In my mind I can't grasp what she's doing, I merely reject it.*
*However, Sayo merely answers coldly.*
Sayo: "He's dead, no mistake about it."
Mayu: "This is... terrible! It's Kenji-san!"
Sayo: "Rather than screaming, we have to figure out how and why he died. If it was due to a phenomenon, you and I might be pulled in as well. He's not bleeding, so it may have been poison."
Mayu: "How... can you be so calm, Sayo...?"
*I cover my face at this point.*
*I don't want to face the truth of an acquaintance's death head on.*
*As usual, Sayo continues with disinterest.*
Sayo: "This guy isn't an acquaintance of mine. Our objective is to get out of here with Himeno. I have no time to worry about mourning the death of someone I didn't know."
Rieko: "My... I find that to be a most prudent decision."
Sayo: "Saginuma Rieko."
*Clack, clack.*
*The proceeding footsteps echo throughout the whole room.*
Sayo: "Why are you here?"
Rieko: "Are your injuries all right now, Sayo-chan?"
Sayo: "Shut up. Stay away."
Rieko: "That manner of speaking is unappealing."
*The footsteps reach the side of the desk and stop.*
Sayo: "Stay away."
Rieko: "Should I? After all..."
*Lifting my face to look at her, I see Saginuma Rieko and--*
Mayu: "Mari!"
*Mari is next to Saginuma Rieko, a frightened expression on her face.*
*On the other hand, Saginuma Rieko is smiling.*
*The two of them are horribly mismatched. There is a cruelty to her, like someone forcing a shy child in front of a crowd of strangers.*
*I propel my body into a standing position and take a step in Saginuma Rieko's direction.*
Mayu: "Ahh."
*My feelings leading the charge, my legs become entangled and I topple over on the spot.*
*Immediately after, something crashes hard into my shoulder. Saginuma Rieko is kicking at my shoulder with the pointed tip of her heel.*
Mari: "Bi... g... sis..."
*It's as if Mari's fear and the confusion resulting from my uncertainty about what I should do are mixing together.*
*Having her look down at me after I lost my balance is making my vision swim.*
Rieko: "Oh, you needn't pity her."
Mari: "......"
Mayu: "Please let Mari go!"
*I raise my voice, but Saginuma Rieko merely continues to smile.*
Rieko: "Oh, Mari-chan chose to be with me."
*Saginuma Rieko says in that sticky tone of hers.*
*Mari says nothing to refute this. She merely turns her unstable eyes my way.*
Sayo: "Mayumi! Something's not right."
*Sayo reaches out a hand to pull me up.*
*As I take her hand, Sayo pulls me up towards her with a jerk. We move away from the desk with Kenji's body on top of it and go almost diagonally across the room.*
Mayu: "Mari... if you're afraid, come over here."
*I turn to Mari and say.*
*It isn't as if Saginuma Rieko is controlling Mari merely by putting her hand on her shoulder.*
Mari: "I won't go."
Mayu: "Mari! That lady... she's a bad person. Come here."
Mari: "I won't go... I won't go!"
*Mari seems to be nothing short of terrified as her words grow stronger.*
*Mari shakes her head and turns me down.*
Sayo: "Something's definitely not right."
*Sayo says this as she cuts in between us.*
*Glaring at Saginuma Rieko, she continues in a forceful tone.*
Sayo: "You. What did you do to Mayumi's little sister? Moreover, what's with this body? This room? You came here because you know."
Rieko: "You need to use the proper tone when asking someone something, Sayo-chan. You know that, don't you?"
Sayo: "Shut up. Answer me."
Rieko: "And you were such an earnest child when you were little. Whatever happened to you? Got a little rough around the edges after your father died, did you?"
Sayo: "What did you say?"
Mayu: "Sayo, calm down! This woman's also acting strange!"
*Already being dead, this woman should be cut off from the flow of time.*
*In spite of that, she seems to have obtained some growth. She's aware of the director's death. There's something inconsistent about Saginuma Rieko.*
Mayu: "She's trying to make us mad. Don't let her drag you along."
*Now that Sayo's worked up, I'm the one who's gotten calm.*
*I grip Sayo's wrist and give a firm squeeze. I want her to know she isn't alone.*
Mayu: "What are you going to do to Mari? I came here to see her. I won't allow you to cause her harm."
Rieko: "You won't allow me? Then what do you intend to do?"
Mayu: "There's two of us, we'll come at you with all we've got."
*Right now, Sayo is with me.*
*As I say this, Sayo glances at me and nods in agreement.*
Rieko: "Oh my, how scary. But no need to worry. Since Mari-chan has chosen to be with me of her own free will, I have no intention of doing anything to her."
Rieko: "Quite the contrary, actually. From here on out, you could say that Mari-chan will be doing the meddling here in Manten Aquarium."
Mayu: "... What do you mean by that?"
*Saginuma Rieko laughs with a sly "Heehee," and without even answering my question, starts talking again.*
Rieko: "You two have experienced this room... no, this aquarium's, puzzle. You two have also experienced the way this place changes once you go through a door."
Rieko: "This is a world full of possibilities. It is a world that contains within it the past, present, and future."
Sayo: "Possibilities?"
Mayu: "I understand about the souvenir shop from the past and the old staff room. But the future... it wouldn't make sense for things that haven't happened yet to take place here!"
Sayo: "That's not all. The phenomena that happen here are things that should never have been possible."
*The Fish of the World booth with fish corpses scattered about, the jellyfish booth where the glass suddenly shattered.*
*Those are what one might call possibilities that shouldn't have happened.*
Rieko: "It's a construct decided on by humans that time only proceeds in one direction. I wonder if you can comprehend... Heehee... That it isn't the truth."
*No matter how much we look at her with doubt in our eyes, she merely looks back with a wicked smile.*
Rieko: "This world known as Manten Aquarium is made up of the numerous souls of living things that perished along with the director. This story... you heard it from Kenji-san, didn't you...? Heehee..."
Mayu: "How did you..."
Rieko: "The form of energy we know as souls gives this place its shape. Though strictly speaking they aren't living, souls possess a fluidity and directionality to them that makes 'living thing' the closest way to describe them. Do you understand?"
*She seems to be toying with us and watching for our reactions. However, what she's saying seems to be the truth, so we listen intently. It's missing the exaggeration that would seem like a lie.*
*After a pause, Saginuma Rieko pats Mari on the head. Even when our eyes meet, Mari's expression doesn't change, her mouth shut tight.*
*It's just that Mari's eyes seem to be rejecting me when I call out to her.*
Rieko: "Entities that have become spirits are not bound by time, demonstrating the possibility to pursue their own directionality. So in this world, those that tried to kill you did so to try and pull in your souls."
Sayo: "Wait a minute. Wasn't this world created by my father's hatred for you?"
Rieko: "At the moment it was created... yes. The director's malice guided the souls of those creatures and they created this place, but now things are different."
Rieko: "In trying to steal energy from the outside world to maintain this place, this world's directive has switched to 'maintenance.'"
Rieko: "The souls now bear only a grudge and their curse, having changed into creatures that seek only this place's preservation."
Rieko: "I suppose you understand what that means, don't you?"
Sayo: "Papa is-- You mean Sakuragi Isao is no longer here? Not even his spirit?"
Rieko: "You've finally gotten skilled at making inferences, haven't you? But it's pitiful. Speaking in mathematical terms, it's not that your solution is insufficient, just that your calculations were off, so your answer is wrong."
Mayu: "You're going in circles. Come on...!"
*For Sayo, it was a fact she had to hear.*
*If the truth we arrived at doesn't exist, then...*
Rieko: "He merely exists without the shell of an individual body. This Manten Aquarium is like a pool of souls that have lost their individuality."
Sayo: "Then why are you here? There's no mistaking that you're Saginuma Rieko, right?"
Rieko: "There are two reasons, you know. Because I was guided here by you..."
*She pats Mari's head. When Mari is touched by Saginuma Rieko, she closes her eyes for a moment.*
*There's definitely another side to this.*
*As this thought crosses my mind, I wait for her to continue.*
Rieko: "And because of my hatred. I want to remain Saginuma Rieko... Heehee..."
Mari: "I'm doing this of my own will, big sister..."
Rieko: "As time passes this way, Mari-chan will become one with us and will be sublimated into a general concept. Heehee... Can you stand the thought of her becoming a mere idea...?"
Mayu: "A concept...? What exactly do you mean by that?"
Rieko: "What does that mean, I wonder. Since it's already a used up possibility, there's no need for you to know."
Sayo: "Used up? What do you mean?"
Rieko: "It's toppled over there before you. A tool that person tried to use..."
Mayu: "You mean Kenji-san?"
Rieko: "He is Kenji-kun, but he is no longer Kenji-kun. He is merely the living flesh that Sakuragi Isao used in order to interact with the physical world. To make it easy to understand, shall we say he hijacked this body?"
Rieko: "But in trying to forcibly pull away one of the souls, Kenji-kun's soul itself disappeared..."
Rieko: "Thanks to HIS disappearance, we were able to seize control of this world called Manten Aquarium. Haha... I'll have to thank him..."
Sayo: "My papa did? You just said something about losing one's individuality."
Rieko: "He was both an individual... and part of the whole. He was an existence that was Sakuragi Isao and that was Manten Aquarium. He apparently never understood it. Hahaha... I thought he was a little more clever than that though."
*Saginuma Rieko casts a sidelong glance in the direction of Kenji-san's body, then gives a daring smile.*
*I can barely understand anything she is saying.*
*Individuals and the whole, I don't get it at all. It might be something living beings like us are unable to comprehend.*
*It's just that, in all of that, there's one question that is beginning to come into focus.*
Mayu: "Did you... kill Kenji-san?"
Rieko: "Haha..."
Sayo: "Are you saying you killed Papa twice?"
Rieko: "Hahahaha... Ahahahaha..."
*That high-pitched laughter is growing gradually louder.*
*Sayo knits her brows together and glares at Saginuma Rieko. I do the same.*
*The laughter echoing throughout the room serves as our confirmation.*
*We still don't know how or why she's doing this. We also don't know her motive for doing so.*
*But Saginuma Rieko... she killed both Kenji-san, and the director.*
Mayu: "Mari! You have to get away from that woman! She is... a murderer."
Rieko: "Ahahahahahahahah. Hahahaha!"
Sayo: "What's so funny?"
Mayu: "I won't forgive you. Leaving Kenji-san in that state, as you continue to laugh about it..."
Mari: "Big... sis..."
Rieko: "Well, maybe Mari-chan should laugh too!"
Mayu: "Please stop this! Mari, come over here! That woman... it's not good for you to stand by someone as bad as her!"
Rieko: "Sakuragi Isao, who used Kenji-kun's body, killed many people to preserve this world called Manten Aquarium. Are you saying it's bad to erase such evil?"
Mayu: "Of course!"
Sayo: "Mayumi, that's enough. We don't have time to deal with her. Papa was this place's core. Having lost its core, this place should disappear. You need to say farewell to Mari, and we need to escape with that Himeno girl."
Mayu: "But..."
Rieko: "That's right. How about you finally realize the mistake you've made?"
*Seeing Sayo pull on my arm, Saginuma Rieko says this to grab our attention.*
*Her expression unchanging, a wicked smile appears on her face.*
Rieko: "She said you're 'bad'... Mari-chan."
Mayu: "What are you...?!"
Rieko: "The one Kenji-kun -- Sakuragi Isao -- killed was... Mari-chan! Haha.... Ahahahahah!"
Mayu: "Huh......"
Sayo: "Mayumi, don't be deceived!"
Rieko: "Sayo-chan... you've realized the cause of Kenji-san's death, haven't you? Since there are no external wounds, you figured that it might be poison, but did you really figure it out?"
Rieko: "There's no change to his lips or eyeballs, or even his internal organs. There's no swelling or inflammation anywhere on him. Poison wouldn't be able to kill someone like that."
Sayo: "Stop this."
Rieko: "You listened to part of my story, then made a guess, didn't you? That's why you hurried me on."
Sayo: "No. That's not it."
Rieko: "The soul itself was obliterated. It was completely removed from this existence known as Manten Aquarium."
Rieko: "Heehee... That's not something humans can handle, is it?"
Mayu: "But you're... already..."
Sayo: "Dead. You've already become like a ghost. Is that why you can drain away someone's life energy?"
Rieko: "It's possible for humans unified with the aquarium, isn't it? I was once the individual known as Saginuma Rieko."
Mayu: "Then... Mari should be the same."
Rieko: "She is not. My soul is a mixture of emotions... Hatred towards the director, anxiety over the fear of the aquarium disappearing, rage at being killed."
Rieko: "All of them negative emotions. With all of these as examples, my grudge, like all of the souls in Manten Aquarium, is red in color."
Rieko: "Mari-chan is different. This girl's soul is pure, the same color as water."
Rieko: "The true meaning of this is that she won't melt into the existence known as Manten Aquarium... although she is a part of the world known as Manten Aquarium."
Rieko: "If you can become self-aware, you can take control of the power that is Manten Aquarium. It's a different concept from the start."
Rieko: "It is no exaggeration to say that Mari-chan can cause any of the things that could possibly happen in this closed off world."
Mayu: "Even though you say that, there's no way that Mari could kill... or erase anybody."
Rieko: "I told you. In order for you to return from this aquarium safely, you will have to end this world once and for all... Heehee... To do that you would have had to remove the director's soul anyway."
Rieko: "And if the Manten Aquarium under Mari-chan's control supplies itself with more bodies, she can meddle in the real world just as the director did, just as I told you. You understand what that means, don't you?"
Mayu: "Mari... can... come back...?"
Sayo: "Mayumi!"
*But... that's...*
*More bodies, it's not like those will just appear when she needs one.*
*If Kenji-san was used as the director's tool, then that means...*
Mayu: "People who are uninvolved in this will..."
*If someone else is sacrificed, Mari can come back.*
*I think about how I want to see Mari.*
*I love Mari.*
*Of course this is something unacceptable.*
*I don't want to have Mari do something like that.*
*She's my little sister, I can't overlook such a thing.*
*No, it's because she's my little sister that I can't overlook it.*
*I look into Mari's face.*
*I have no idea what kind of face I'm making myself.*
Mayu: "You can't do that, Mari..."
Mari: "But I want to be with you, big sister."
Mayu: "But... that way i-is unacceptable..."
*I avert my gaze from Mari.*
*I can't look at her face anymore.*
*My emotions and my sense of logic are at war. There is an internal struggle as I try to hold on to my sense of ethics. Feelings that I know are wrong are raging within me.*
*I want to be with her, forever.*
*But that's not a good way to go about it.*
*I don't want to say goodbye here.*
*This is reality.*
*B-But--*
Mari: "But... I thought that you would be happy... big sister..."
Mayu: "There's no way I could be!"
*The reason I screamed out like that is merely to alleviate some of these emotions.*
Mari: "Big... sis..."
Mayu: "No, that isn't what I... I......"
Rieko: "Hahahahahahaha! So you've rejected her! Your own sister!"
*Saginuma Rieko runs her hand along Mari's shoulders, then brings it back to her side.*
*Mari opens her eyes wide, a terrified expression on her face.*
Sayo: "Shut up! It's obvious that nothing will come from such a decision."
Mari: "I... just..."
*Mari crumples over, falling to her knees on the spot.*
Mayu: "Mari!"
Rieko: "It's you... who's been rejected, isn't it?"
*Faltering at Saginuma Rieko's provocative words, I stop moving for a moment.*
*In that moment, the entire director's office seems to begin pulsing.*
*A blue light begins to flow out from the tank.*
Mayu: "The room is... being repaired..."
*As the room is enveloped in light, the damage fades away.*
*Instantly, the director's office changes form into a clean room with no ruin or deterioration.*
Rieko: "That's fine... all of this power... I'll gather it here..."
*It doesn't end with the blue light flowing into the room.*
Mayu: "What...?"
Sayo: "There's some sort of projection within the light."
*Just as Sayo said, something is faintly being projected within the blue light.*
*Gradually the contours come into focus, revealing it to be Manten East Building.*
Mayu: "This is..."
*The blue light that has filled the room is reminiscent of a movie projection screen.*
*After one minute passes, what's being projected becomes clear beyond the shadow of a doubt.*
Mayu: "Himeno and I..."
*Himeno and I are shopping inside Manten East Building.*
*We both look like we're having fun.*
*What we're looking at appears to be recorded video. However, the version of myself shown in the video is unfamiliar to me.*
Rieko: "Haha... hahahaha..."
Rieko: "What you're looking at is one possibility. It's a possible future."
Rieko: "Mari-chan, you should understand!"
*Saginuma Rieko shouts toward Mari in a loud voice.*
Mari: "A-A... Ah...."
Rieko: "You've been rejected. Your big sister chose not you, Mari-chan, but a future with her friend."
Rieko: "Mari-chan, you've been cast aside!"
*The video is showing other images of us now. Mixed in with those images are things I actually did experience.*
*All of them are possibilities.*
*But what Saginuma Rieko is saying is wrong.*
Mayu: "That's not true! There's no way it could be true! I would never toss Mari aside."
Mayu: "I just..."
Rieko: "What are you trying to do by making excuses? Within this footage, Mari-chan is nowhere to be seen, but you're smiling like you're having such a good time."
Sayo: "Me, too. Why?"
*Footage of Sayo hanging out with us is also being shown.*
*There are also images of her chatting happily with a girl I don't know.*
Mayu: "This version of me is... I don't know... This is wrong, Mari, it is!"
Mari: "I don't want to hear it! That's a lie!"
*Even as I shout out to her, there's no way it'll reach Mari.*
*I've already been rejected by Mari.*
Rieko: "Nakanobe Mari is not needed in the life of Nakanobe Mayu. Even if you're not there, this is all your sister needs in her future to keep a smile on her face!"
*The images are shooting by too fast now. There's too much to take in, and it's all become impossible to pick up on.*
*But Mari is no longer looking at the future displayed before us.*
*She's not looking at anything at all.*

*I gather myself and rush to Mari's side.*
*There's nothing Saginuma Rieko can say now that will make me falter.*

*But when I reach out my hand to Mari, something gets in my way.*
*It feels as if I've been thrust back hard by somebody.*
*No, at that moment, I'm sure that I was thrown back by something.*
*Mari still isn't looking at me.*
*The projection has already disappeared, and the light enveloping the room has gradually become red.*
*It's a deep red, just like blood.*
Sayo: "It's getting red. It feels like something's about to happen."

*However, I have to tell Mari. I have to get her to understand.*
*Because there's no way I could ever not need Mari.*
Rieko: "Now, erase Sakuragi Sayo once and for all! If you want to have your sister all to yourself!"

*To erase Sayo, and to tempt Mari into doing so...*
*That is absolutely--*
Mayu: "Unforgivable!"
*Saying this, I step between Mari and Sayo and spread both my hands out wide.*
*An imposing stance.*
*No matter what happens, I won't take my eyes off of Mari.*
*With that as my intent, I continue watching her.*
*After some time passes, there is no change.*
*A slight silence flows through this world enveloped in red.*
*The one who finally opens her mouth is Mari.*
Mari: "So I guess no one needs me after all."
*Mari's voice is so discouraged, it seems as if it will sink into the depths of my ears. It's the first time I've ever heard such a voice.*
*Before Mari's words completely vanish from this room, I cry out.*
Mayu: "That's not true!"
Mari: "Liar!"
*Along with her strong words, a purple light begins to swirl around Mari.*
Sayo: "Mayumi, you need to run."
Mayu: "I can't... I can't leave Mari behind!"
*As if trying to match the appearance of the light, the room begins to vibrate.*
*I lose my balance, but grab on to the desk to keep myself standing.*

Mayu: "Hey, listen to what I'm telling you! That's... not what I'm thinking at all!"

Mari: "I'll have you all to myself. I want to be together with you forever. She said I was the only one who could do it..."
*After Mari's words, it's Saginuma Rieko's sticky voice that takes over.*
Rieko: "Heehee... It's beginning now. This world... it's being reconstructed..."
Sayo: "I can't... move."
*She clicks her tongue after those short words. Sayo's harsh stare is being returned by both Mari and Saginuma Rieko.*
*The room's shaking immediately becomes stronger.*
*Just keeping myself standing is taking all I have.*
*The violet light with Mari at its center grows even more intense. The strikingly colored light makes keeping my eyes open painful.*
Mari: "I can't take it anymore!"
*I think those words were my final chance.*
Rieko: "Huh...? What is this... this sensation..."
Mayu: "I can't... see anything..."
*Thanks to a violent shock from the striking light, I shut my eyes and collapse onto the ground. The next moment, I lose my sense of equilibrium.*
*Which is up, and which is down?*
*At the moment I lose any sense of direction, my consciousness sinks into the sea.*
*Riiiiiiiiiiiiiing.*
*The sound of my alarm clock is a little delayed, but I open my eyes.*
*I open my eyes to a piercing light. I sit up, my body feeling so heavy it's as if I haven't truly woken up yet. I'm on top of a bed I bought recently.*
*Looking over at the hard-cover book at my bedside, I remember that I fell asleep while reading it. I believe that it was a murder-mystery, but seeing as how I was half-asleep while reading it, I don't really remember.*
*Since a ribbon is dangling freely from the book, it seems that I fell asleep without setting the bookmark.*
Mayu: "Uwah..."
*My voice comes out a mixture of a sigh and a yawn.*
*I have school again today.*
*I change into my school uniform and head for the kitchen table on the first floor.*
*I am Nakanobe Mayumi. You could say I'm a typical third year junior high student.*
*My hobbies are reading and watching TV and movies. I like fashion, but I'm on a fixed allowance, so I can't buy much.*
Mayu: "G'mornin."
*I live in a three story house, where the first floor is the living room and kitchen, the second floor is my parents' room, and the third floor has the kids' room.*
Mari: "Ah, big sister! You're already running late! Make sure you wash your face!"
Mayu: "I did. Ah, are we having salmon for breakfast today?"
*Our parents are-- That's right, they get up early for work in the morning and come home late at night. That's why my morning routine is to eat the breakfast that Mari makes.*
*I'm the older one, but Mari's cooking skills are light-years ahead of mine. My speciality is eating.*

Mayu: "Hey, I said something a little different today."
*Mari goes to the same school as I do. She's one grade below me, but she's just a little taller than I am.*
Mari: "Okay, okay. Ah, it's almost done. By the way, your prediction was right, big sis!"
*Mari sets the food on the table in the way she's become accustomed to, setting down the salmon grilled with salt, pickled vegetables, mekabu, and miso soup.*
*Meanwhile, I dish out rice from the rice cooker and set it on the table along with the chopsticks.*
Mayu: "Bon appetit!"
Mari: "Yeah, eat up."

*In terms of grades, Mari's are better than mine. Sometimes we study together and our comprehension is about the same. I'm chalking it up to our difference in grade level and scope of classwork.*
*In other words, the difference is definitely in the difficulty of the real tests. Why can't I seem to master doing good when it counts?*
Mari: "After the summer, you have entrance exams, don't you?"
Mayu: "Ah~ let's talk even less about that. My head's full enough with all this stuff about midterms."
Mari: "Make sure you pick a high school we can both go to!"
Mayu: "It doesn't matter, I can't make it into a private school. Even though Mitsuba's uniforms are so cute."
*We chat away lightly as time passes at the breakfast table.*
*Listening to the news flowing from the TV that was left on, we both get ready for school. Our lunches will be leftovers from last night, but boiled and stewed foods that have set overnight are more permeated with flavor, making them delicious.*
Mari: "Okay, let's go, big sister!"
Mayu: "O~kay."
*The two of us always walk to school together.*
*I think we get along pretty well as sisters while at school. It's not a large school, so we often pass each other in the building, but Mari always says hi to me so excitedly like she's gonna jump after me.*
*Although it's a little embarrassing that she won't stop doing that even after getting into junior high, I don't think it's a bad thing.*
Himeno: "Ah, it's Mayu! Yoohoo~!"
*Approaching the school, I hear a familiar voice calling me.*
*My classmate, Tamagawa Himeno. She is my best friend.*
*Himeno is a little ways ahead, waving her hand and calling me.*
Mayu: "Ah, Himeno."
Mayu: "Well, I'm gonna catch up to her. See you later!"
Mari: "Big s..."
*I turn to Mari and give a little wave, then jog after Himeno.*
*Within Mari's eyes, I get the sense of seeing a deep color I've never seen before.*
Mayu: "Morning, Himeno!"
Himeno: "G'morning~! Hey, did you hear, Mayu..."
*As soon as I catch up to Himeno, she begins talking really fast. When I'm with her, I get the feeling that about 80%% of the time it's Himeno doing the talking, but I think it's more fun that way.*
*It's at that moment.*
Himeno: "Ugh... Urk..."
*Himeno begins to have a coughing fit. I assume it's just her throat getting choked up from starting to talk so fast and so suddenly.*
*But I'm wrong.*
*Gloop.*
*When Himeno coughs, she vomits something out of her mouth. It's a cream-colored something.*
*Plip, plop.*
*On top of the asphalt, granules and fibers dance about.*
*Even though I realize they're all living things, I think it takes me at least a minute.*
Mayu: "Nooo!"
*Parasites.*
*I've seen them on TV and in illustrated reference books. Himeno is throwing up so many that they are clumping together.*
*Each one moves on its own with such stimulation. A bit of blood seems to be mixed in with them.*
Mayu: "Hime... no...?"
*I lift my face.*
*The hole in Himeno's face is completely blocked up with the parasites she's coughed up.*
*They squirm around on her skin as if they might be alive.*
*Before I can comprehend the sight before my eyes, my consciousness becomes turbid.*
Mayu: "OK, I'll go first."
*This is Manten Aquarium.*
*Manten is a premiere shopping district where one can enjoy shopping, food, movies, bowling, and karaoke.*
*It's a small aquarium inside Manten East Building, which serves as this town's symbol.*
Mari: "Big sister, do your best!"
*This girl is Nakanobe Mari. She's my sister, one year younger than I. Today the two of us have come to have fun at Manten Aquarium on our way home from school.*
*We've seen the tunnel tank and the jellyfish booth, and even a show at the stage. Since we have year-long passports, we come here all the time, but no matter how often we come, we never get sick of it.*
*It seems there was a Deep-sea Fish booth long before we started coming here, but it's closed off now.*
*I've always wanted to see the rare fish of the deep sea. When we're older, Mari and I want to check out the big aquarium down in Okinawa together.*
Mayu: "Here goes."
*And now I'm taking a shot at the GachaGacha machines in the souvenir shop.*
*There are cute keychains with charms shaped like drops with creatures from the aquarium inside.*
*The line-up consists of jellyfish, starfish, orca, penguins, and whales. There aren't any whales in Manten Aquarium, but oh well. Each one has a different colored droplet as well.*
*My goal is, of course, the jellyfish. I put my coin in and turn the lever.*
Mayu: "Did it!"
*A hole in one.*
*It's the jellyfish keychain.*
Mari: "Ah, big sister, you're amazing. It's my turn now~!"
*In a goofy manner, Mari puts her coin in with great force.*
*... It's a starfish.*
Mari: "O-One more time."
Mayu: "D-Do your best..."
*Mari puffs up her cheeks and puts in another coin.*
*The result is another starfish.*
Mayu: "H-Haha... Maybe I'll get one..."
Mari: "Ah~ Big sister, you say it like it's so easy~!"
Mayu: "Should we trade? I also like the starfish and think it's cute."
Mari: "It's fine, I'll keep going until I get one! Because I want one that matches yours!"
Mayu: "Then why don't we just have matching starfish ones...?"
*I say this in an exasperated tone, but Mari says, "No~!" in a careless tone and puts both of her hands up.*
Mari: "It has to be matching jellyfish ones!"
*When things get like this, Mari becomes obstinate and won't budge. Even though our triggers might be different, our personalities are similar in this regard, so I understand.*
*Staying true to her word, Mari carries on relentlessly.*
*Starfish, starfish, starfish...*
*No matter how much time passes, no jellyfish ones will come out.*
*I take a step back and watch over Mari, then turn around when I hear someone call, "Mayumi."*
Sayo: "Is that girl that's with you all right?"
Mayu: "Sayo!"
Miku: "That's no good... Gambling takes a bit of magic."
Mayu: "Ah, and Miku."
*Sakuragi Sayo and Minato Miku. We go to different schools, but they're friends of mine.*
*Though she was brusque with me at first, Sayo and I get along, and it was she who introduced me to Miku. After that, we started hanging out with each other on occasion.*
Sayo: "Your little sister... Mari, was it? Which one does she want?"
Mayu: "The jellyfish. She wants one that matches mine."
Miku: "Ah, there're way too many starfish, eh? Good thing I wanted a starfish myself. Wanna trade?"
Sayo: "Minato, every time you come you play those machines."
*Sayo says this in an exasperated tone.*
*There's no mistaking that we're thinking the same thing.*
*Be that as it may, this is good news.*
*I call out to Mari, who is so dedicated to her repeated attempts that she has zoned out.*
Mayu: "Mari! Miku-chan says she'll trade you! Come here!"
*Mari turns back once and looks this way.*
*At that moment I get the sense of seeing a deep color within Mari's eyes that I've never seen before.*
*Mari quickly looks back to the GachaGacha machine and turns her back to us.*
Mayu: "Sorry, seems like she's really trying to concentrate..."
*The two of them are not there.*
*No, they probably are.*
*I'm more confused than I can put into words.*
Mayu: "Kyaaah!"
*The two of them have all but disappeared, with the exception of their legs.*
*Nothing but their legs.*
*Just their legs.*
*Their torsos have melted into a red, slime-like form.*
*A viscous gel spreads along the floor. It seems to twitch and shake, as if there are living things within.*
Mayu: "What... is this...?"
*I can't even begin to comprehend this.*
*Everything above the little that's left of their ankles has also been dyed a deep red and is melting. The melted, slime-like portion has mixed with the previously melted parts of their bodies.*
*Right before my eyes, all that remains is their clothes and shoes.*
Mayu: "Noooo!"
*In an instant, my friends have been erased from this world.*
*My consciousness becomes turbid.*
*Unable to brace my tottering body, I sink into the deep sea.*
Mari: "Today in music class, Hiraike-sensei was so funny..."
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "Speaking of which, next week, Kimizono part three comes out. Big sister, let's go see it!"
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "It's been hot lately~ I wanna go get a new shirt and mini skirt, will you go with me?"
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "The final exams are awful, aren't they? Do you still have your questions from last year? Well, it's not like they'd use the same questions anyway."
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "I'm gonna be the Prime Minister when I grow up. I'll get rid of all tests and exams. Everyone can just focus on doing lots of fun things."
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "What's your dream, big sister? What do you like these days, big sister?"
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "Hey, big sister. Are you happy?"
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "You aren't having fun?"
*... Yeah.*
Mari: "I see."
Mari: "This is... where I died."
*The Deep-sea Fish booth.*
*Unable to see any fish or other living things, there's nothing but space.*
Mari: "You know, I thought about how I wanted to go to school with you, big sister."
*Mari places her hand on the tank and turns her gaze upward as if she's looking to the water's surface.*
*Mari is a little bit taller than I. Her physical development, while not complete, still seems to be ahead of my own.*
*That's right, surely that's because...*
*It's one of Mari's possible appearances if she had lived.*
Mayu: "Mari..."
*I clasp Mari's hand and look up at the tank as Mari is doing.*

*I feel like Mari's somewhat adult-like tone of voice resembles mine a bit.*
Mari: "Using your memories, big sister, as well as the accumulated memories within Manten Aquarium, I reconstructed this place."
Mari: "However... there was definitely a disturbance. Surely, even more than what you experienced, over and over again -- I failed."
Mayu: "I have no idea what you're saying, Mari."
*I take Mari's hand and smile.*
*In actuality, what Mari is saying is on a larger scale, and is something that I cannot grasp.*
Mari: "In other words -- I couldn't keep you all to myself after all. Because, within your heart, big sister, you have other friends."
Mari: "Is it because I was no longer with you, big sister -- no, because you no longer needed me?"
*Mari looks into my eyes and tilts her head.*
*I answer, "No."*
*I laugh a little and say, "You're so foolish."*
Mayu: "There wasn't even a single day that I forgot about you, Mari."
Mayu: "I had to struggle to overcome my sadness from not being able to see you over and over again, but that was my own selfish desire."
*My parents' behavior must surely have been that way. Trying to pretend it didn't exist, they averted their eyes from their sadness.*
*That must be the single-minded logic of those who are left behind.*
*But--*
Mayu: "Because I love you, Mari."
*This is beyond a doubt my true feeling.*
*With large eyes, Mari looks at me.*
*At our feet, the world begins to crumble.*
Mari: "Hey, big sister."
Mayu: "What?"
Mari: "Do you think the stars will grant a wish for me?"
Mayu: "Hm~ I dunno."
*I wonder when that was.*
*Was it when we went on a trip as a family, or when we were coming home? I think it was a day we spent all afternoon having fun together.*
*On a sandy beach at night, Mari and I were talking like that. As our parents watched over us from a distance, I'm pretty sure we had a fairly complicated conversation.*
*Bathing in the light of the stars that fill the expansive sky feels even more refreshing than sunbathing.*
Mayu: "Mari, what would you wish for?"
Mari: "Hm~"
*The two of us look so much alike.*
*The two of us enjoy letting our hair down and playing with each other's.*
*When together, we were often mistaken for twins. At those times, I had a tendency to pout.*
*But I'm the big sister -- is what I thought.*
Mari: "To be happy with you, big sister, always. That was my wish."
*Mari, speaking beyond her years, says softly.*
*Unchanged from that time, it's an infinitely pure wish.*
Mayu: "Thank you, Mari."
*Bathed in the light of the stars, I can do nothing other than smile.*
*The feelings that flow out from me are ones I can't clearly put into words.*
Mari: "My wish wasn't granted by the stars."
Mari: "But if I think back on it, I was wrong. My wish to have you all to myself, big sister, wouldn't have been granted."
Mari: "At the moment that I died, my wish was supposed to be put on hold. When you go against reality, it can never go well."
Mari: "Because if this were reality, we would have never seen each other again."
*Mari says this in a light tone.*
*It is merely a voice of surrender.*
*Even amongst the distortion...*
Mayu: "I'm so happy I saw you, Mari."
Mayu: "But that we have to part so soon afterwards makes me sad."
Mayu: "No... I don't want this... I finally found you. I want......"
*As I give those words voice, I realize my heart is more brittle than I thought.*
*That which overflows from my eyes is something I can't stop with my own strength.*
Mayu: "Mari..."
*I've been holding it in the whole time.*
*I've really wanted to say something the whole time. That I don't want us to say goodbye.*
*That's why, from the moment I saw Mari, it was impossible for me not to pursue her.*
Mari: "Geez, you're the older sister, you can't cry."
Mayu: "But..."
Mari: "Besides, the one who's disappearing is me, you know?"
*Mari says, jokingly.*
*There's no way I can respond to that.*
Mayu: "You know, I... I just want you to stay the kind girl you are, Mari..."
*Somehow holding back my sobs, I continue.*
Mayu: "No matter what happens, you can't harm anyone or start anything. That's what I was rejecting. That's what... I wanted to tell you, Mari."
Mayu: "Because I am your big sister."
*Before I know it, five years come between us once more.*
*At this rate, we won't be mistaken for twins anymore.*
Mari: "Big sister..."
Mari: "I decided what I'm going to wish for!"
*Mari has become just as she was in my memories. Jumping up, Mari gives me an innocent smile.*
Mari: "That you'll always be happy, from now on."
Mari: "Along with your friends."
Mari: "I won't think of it as you being taken away from me anymore."
Mari: "Because I'm sure your friends make you happy, big sister."
Mari: "This is my prayer. The stars won't grant my wishes, but if I pray, it's something I can grant on my own."
Mari: "I'm leaving all that I am to you, big sister."
Mayu: "... Okay."
Mayu: "...... Thank you."
*No matter how the aquarium's power fades.*
*No matter if the energy from the grudge disappears.*
*Mari has long since transcended time itself.*
*Because up until now and from today forward, she will always be in my heart.*
Mari: "Well, it's time for me to go."
Mari: "Before I go, rub my head!"
Mayu: "Of course."
*With Mari's innocent words, her mouth smiles broadly. Her eyes and mouth have completely different expressions. This is really strange.*
*The drops that flow over sink to the ground.*
*I lightly drop my hand onto Mari's head and begin to rub it.*
*She has no body heat, but still she seems warm.*
*I gently stop moving my hand.*
*I'm hesitant to part with her, but I have to accept reality.*
*This instant in and of itself is a miracle.*
*The two of us, torn apart so unreasonably, can actually meet once more and say farewell.*
*But that tragic truth shines brighter than any jewel.*
Mayu: "Thanks for everything up til now. And, everything after this..."
Mari: "Yeah! Big sister, bye-bye!"
Mari: "I'll always love you!"
Mayu: "Me too... I love you."
Mayu: "Mari, if it's what you really want, I'll--"
*As I say this, Mari shakes her head. Her long hair sways, bathed in the light of the stars.*
Mari: "I told you I would wish for it!"
Mari: "This isn't your world, big sister."
*Mari gently pushes away my hand which had been resting on her head.*
Mayu: "Mari..."
*My hand, floating in midair with no place to go, lingers for a bit.*
*Still, because of that, I softly make a fist.*
Mari: "And... punch!"
*Mari straightens up and hits my fist with her hand.*
Mari: "See you later, big sister!"
Mayu: "Yeah... I love you, Mari."
Mari: "Me too, I love you!"
*My fist doesn't feel as if anything's touched it.*
*As if Mari's body has become transparent in that last moment, we are unable to touch, but it's not a big deal.*
*Because our hearts have certainly become in sync.*
*Mari goes into the sea of stars.*
*I go the opposite way.*
*Both of us walk in different directions.*
